# Runs (sub-project B): one session per lane, the gate, the judge, the run card · implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A plan becomes a run: a planner turn in the chat lane declares a lane map, Chronicle runs each lane as its own ACP session inside the one working tree under a write fence and a git guard, saves each lane by explicit path, checks it in a scratch copy, has it reviewed, closes the round itself, and shows all of it as one run card with lane threads, while a small decision model (through the provider layer) answers the run's bounded questions.

**Architecture:** Everything with policy lives in Rust under `src-tauri/src/runs/`: the run record (the only writer), lane-map validation, the fence and git guard, the gate (scratch worktree, checks with a kill, commit under one git mutex, leak report), the judge and reviewer (on the provider layer's `Calls`), a pure scheduler, and a run controller thread per live run that drives `Lane::Worker` sessions through a `LaneHost` trait (the real host is ACP; tests use a fake). The acp seam gains one generic hook: a per-session `LanePolicy` that answers permission asks and direct writes without the user. Lanes and the planner reach Chronicle through a scoped `chronicle --mcp` server whose `chronicle.run.*` tools go over the existing bridge socket and are answered in Rust. The frontend gets a run store fed by `run-update` events, pure selectors whose strings are the comps', a `run` entry in the chat thread that renders the run card, a lane view, and the notes card mirroring.

**Tech Stack:** Rust/Tauri 2 (`serde_json`, `regex`, `sha2`, `libc` — all already in `Cargo.toml`), React 19.2.7 + TypeScript, Vite 8 + Tailwind 4, vitest (node environment, pure modules only).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-agent-orchestrator-design.md` — §1, §2, §3 (lifecycle and the record), §4 (isolation), §5 as amended 2026-10-15 (one session per lane; §5a is the rejected variant and is not built), §6 (the judge), §7 (entry points, checks), §8 (what the user sees), §10–§12, §14 boards 1–4, §15. Comps (approved): `design/comps/runs/project/01-Run-dark.dc.html` … `05-Needs-you.dc.html`, generated by `design/comps/runs/gen.py` — every user-facing string and spacing value in Tasks 13–15 is copied from `gen.py`. Depends on `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-agents-and-providers-design.md` §3, §7–§10.

**Plan C must land first.** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-agents-and-providers.md` (the agents-and-providers plan) builds the agent profiles, `setup::agent_launch`, the profile argument to `acp::start`, the normalised `_chronicle` tool meta, `acp::Lane::Reviewer`, and the provider layer. This plan consumes those by the spec's names. Three names the spec does not spell out are assumed, and each is used in exactly one function so a different spelling in Plan C is a one-line fix:

| Assumed name | Used only in |
|---|---|
| `crate::providers::CompleteRequest { system: String, prompt: String, schema: Value, model: Option<String> }` | `runs/oracle.rs` · `ProviderOracle::complete_json` |
| `crate::providers::for_role(role: Role, project: &Path) -> Box<dyn Calls + Send + Sync>`, `Role::{Review, Decisions}`, and `crate::providers::agent_calls(project: &Path) -> Box<dyn Calls + Send + Sync>` (the `Provider::Agent` instance) | `runs/oracle.rs` · `for_project` |
| `crate::agents::project_profile(project: &Path) -> &'static AgentProfile` (with `.id`) | `runs/host.rs` · `launch_worker` |

Before Task 1, the executor runs `grep -n "pub fn for_role\|pub struct CompleteRequest\|pub fn project_profile\|pub fn agent_calls" -r src-tauri/src` and, where Plan C chose other names, uses those in the two functions above. Nothing else in this plan depends on them.

## Global Constraints

- Branch in place on `react-shadcn`; never a worktree for development (the 1.2 GB `src-tauri/target`). Stage by explicit path; never `git add -A`, `stash`, `reset`, `checkout --`, `clean`.
- The installed Chronicle app runs on this repo and writes `.chronicle/` live: never touch `.chronicle/`, never delete `.chronicle/kanban.json` from the index.
- vitest runs `src/**/*.test.ts` in the **node** environment. No component tests, no jsdom/happy-dom, no new dev dependencies. Pure logic goes in `src/lib/*.ts` so it can be tested.
- Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- No new crates and no new npm dependencies. Globs are matched with the `regex` crate already present; the clock is `std::time`; ISO dates are formatted by hand (Task 1).
- Every git write in a test happens in a temporary repository under `std::env::temp_dir()` that the test creates. No test runs git against this repo. The product code's own git writes (`git add -- <paths>`, `git commit --only`, `git commit-tree` + `git update-ref`, `git worktree add`, `git checkout -- <paths>` on the user's Discard click, and — inside Chronicle's own scratch copy only, never the user's tree — `git checkout --detach --force` and `git clean`) exist only inside `runs/gate.rs`.
- Chronicle holds no Anthropic key. Lanes run on the project's agent profile (agents spec §3, §12.7); lane instructions travel in the prompt and tools in MCP, never in a plugin (agents spec §8).
- Vocabulary law for user-facing copy: sentence case; saved, not committed; published, not pushed; a lane is "waiting", "working", "being checked", "fixing", "finished", "needs you", "stopped"; the run is "planning", "running", "final check", "done", "needs you", "stopped", "interrupted". Copy comes from `gen.py`; strings this plan adds where the boards are silent are marked "(new copy)" where they appear.
- Design tokens only (`var(--…)` / Tailwind token classes); no raw hex. Motion uses the pane's existing `.wv-*` classes and `--dur-*` tokens.
- Numbers from the spec, verbatim: stall after 5 minutes without a wire message; backoff `min(10 s × 2^(attempt−1), 300 s)`; 3 attempts; at most 2 fix passes; one head-triage fix pass; `maxLanes` default 3; a check is killed at 10 minutes; bands 0.75 and 0.45; the scratch worktree under `~/Library/Application Support/Chronicle/scratch/<repo-hash>/`.
- Checks: `npm run typecheck`, `npx vitest run`, `npm run build`, and `cd src-tauri && cargo test` (PATH needs `$HOME/.cargo/bin`). Release binary for live checks: `cargo build --release --features tauri/custom-protocol`.

## Review Focus

Inputs the spec implies but no other test in this plan exercises; each has its test pinned to the owning task.

1. **Two lanes whose owned globs overlap only through a symlink** (`src/shared -> ../lib/shared`, lane a owns `src/shared/**`, lane b owns `lib/shared/x.ts`) — the map must be refused as overlapping, naming both lanes, and the fence must judge a write by where it really lands. Pinned in Task 2: `owned_globs_that_meet_through_a_symlink_overlap`; and Task 3: `a_write_through_a_symlink_is_judged_where_it_lands`.
2. **A check command that never exits, or that leaves a child holding on** (`sleep 30 & wait`) — killed at the timeout with its whole process group, counted as failed with its partial output, and the gate goes on. Pinned in Task 5: `a_check_that_never_exits_is_killed_with_its_children`.
3. **A lane's save racing the user's own git in the shared tree** (an `index.lock` held for a moment; the user's own staged file in the index) — the save waits the lock out, commits only the lane's paths, and leaves the user's staged file staged and out of the commit. Pinned in Task 5: `a_lane_save_waits_out_the_users_index_lock_and_leaves_their_staged_files_alone`.
4. **The judge's provider becoming unreachable mid-run** — the next decision falls to the agent provider for the rest of the run with one notice, the log records which model answered, and if the fallback fails too the stricter option is taken (refuse, park, none, fewer lanes). Pinned in Task 6: `an_unreachable_provider_falls_back_once_and_then_fails_strict`.
5. **Resume after the record says `gating`** (the app died during the head check, or during a lane's gate) — the head check runs again, a lane caught mid-gate is re-checked on the commit it already has, and nothing is committed twice. Pinned in Task 7: `resume_from_gating_runs_the_head_check_again` and `resume_rechecks_a_lane_caught_mid_gate`; and Task 9: `resuming_a_run_caught_in_its_final_check_saves_nothing_twice`.

Deviations from the spec's text, each argued where it lands: the record gains a handful of fields the lifecycle needs and §3 does not list (`source`, `items` in the lane map, `fixes`, `retry_at_ms`, `session` per lane, `tail`/`timed_out` per check, `by` per leak, `reason`/`lane`/`subject` per decision, `planner_tries`, `planner_error`, `notices`, `target_lanes`, `interrupted_from`, `skipped`, `head_pending`, `planner_cost_usd`) — all additive, all `#[serde(default)]`; gates run one at a time (one scratch copy cannot hold two checkouts); the Setup "Decisions" row is Plan C's "Providers" row (spec §6, amended) and is not built here.

### Task 1: The run record

**Files:**
- Create: `src-tauri/src/runs/mod.rs`, `src-tauri/src/runs/record.rs`
- Modify: `src-tauri/src/main.rs` (add `mod runs;` beside `mod bridge;`; extract `journal_append_in` from the `journal_append` command)
- Modify: `src-tauri/src/notes/rounds.rs` (`settle_done` skips a round whose run is live)
- Test: `#[cfg(test)]` in `record.rs` and `rounds.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `crate::epoch_ms()`, `crate::notes::rounds::{load, save, Round}`.
- Produces (all `pub` in `crate::runs::record`):
  - enums `RunKind { Notes, Plan, Prompt }` (serde lowercase), `RunState { Planning, Running, Gating, Closing, Done, NeedsYou, Failed, Stopped, Interrupted }` (serde kebab-case: `"needs-you"`), `LaneState { Waiting, Preparing, BuildingPrompt, Launching, Initialising, Streaming, Finishing, Gating, Fixing, Closed, Failed, TimedOut, Stalled, Cancelled, Parked }` (serde snake_case)
  - `RunState::terminal(self) -> bool`; `LaneState::busy(self) -> bool`; `LaneState::between_tries(self) -> bool`
  - structs `Item`, `LaneSpec`, `LaneMap`, `Tokens`, `CheckResult`, `Finding`, `Review`, `Gate` (+ `passed()`), `Leak`, `LaneRecord` (+ `new(id)`), `HeadCheck`, `DecisionLog`, `NeedsYou`, `Cost`, `RunRecord` with fields exactly as in Step 3
  - `RunRecord::new(n, kind, round, plan_path, prompt_path, source) -> RunRecord`; `set_state(&mut self, RunState) -> Result<(), String>`; `lane(&self, &str) -> Option<&LaneRecord>`; `lane_mut`; `set_lane_state(&mut self, &str, LaneState) -> Result<(), String>`; `total_cost(&self) -> Cost`; `round_n(&self) -> u64`
  - `can_move(RunState, RunState) -> bool`, `lane_can_move(LaneState, LaneState) -> bool`
  - `path(dir, n) -> PathBuf`, `load(dir, n) -> Result<Option<RunRecord>, String>`, `save(dir, &mut RunRecord) -> Result<(), String>`, `list(dir) -> Vec<RunRecord>`, `is_live(dir, round) -> bool`
  - `iso(ms: u64) -> String`, `iso_now() -> String`, `transition_lines(&RunRecord, &RunRecord) -> Vec<String>`, `round_state_for(RunState) -> &'static str`
  - in `main.rs`: `pub(crate) fn journal_append_in(dir: &Path, kind: &str, text: &str)`

Create `src-tauri/src/runs/record.rs` with only the tests for now (the types follow in Step 3):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn tmp(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-run-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join(".chronicle")).unwrap();
        d.canonicalize().unwrap()
    }
    fn sample(n: u64) -> RunRecord {
        RunRecord::new(n, RunKind::Notes, Some(n), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md"), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md"), "")
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_record_round_trips_in_the_spec_shape() {
        let d = tmp("io");
        assert!(load(&d, 10).unwrap().is_none(), "no file yet");
        let mut r = sample(10);
        save(&d, &mut r).unwrap();
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(path(&d, 10)).unwrap()).unwrap();
        for key in ["n", "round", "kind", "state", "created", "updated", "plan_path", "prompt_path", "lane_map", "lanes", "head_check", "decisions", "needs_you", "cost"] {
            assert!(v.get(key).is_some(), "{key} is in the record");
        }
        assert_eq!(v["kind"], "notes");
        assert_eq!(v["state"], "planning");
        assert_eq!(v["cost"], serde_json::json!({ "usd": 0.0, "in": 0, "out": 0 }));
        let back = load(&d, 10).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(back.n, 10);
        assert_eq!(list(&d).len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unreadable_record_is_never_written_over() {
        let d = tmp("corrupt");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join(".chronicle/runs")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(path(&d, 3), "{ not json").unwrap();
        assert!(load(&d, 3).is_err());
        assert!(save(&d, &mut sample(3)).is_err(), "refuses to overwrite");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(path(&d, 3)).unwrap(), "{ not json");
        assert!(list(&d).is_empty(), "the listing skips it rather than failing");
    }

    #[test]
    fn run_states_move_only_along_the_lifecycle() {
        use RunState::*;
        for (a, b) in [(Planning, Running), (Running, Gating), (Gating, Closing), (Closing, Done), (Running, NeedsYou),
                       (NeedsYou, Running), (Gating, Running), (Interrupted, Gating), (Planning, Failed), (Running, Stopped)] {
            assert!(can_move(a, b), "{a:?} → {b:?}");
        }
        for (a, b) in [(Done, Running), (Failed, Running), (Stopped, Running), (Planning, Closing), (Closing, Running)] {
            assert!(!can_move(a, b), "{a:?} ↛ {b:?}");
        }
        let mut r = sample(1);
        assert!(r.set_state(Closing).is_err());
        r.set_state(Running).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.state, Running);
        assert!(Done.terminal() && Failed.terminal() && Stopped.terminal() && !Interrupted.terminal());
    }

    #[test]
    fn lane_states_follow_the_attempt_machine() {
        use LaneState::*;
        let path = [Waiting, Preparing, BuildingPrompt, Launching, Initialising, Streaming, Finishing, Gating, Fixing, Finishing, Gating, Closed];
        for w in path.windows(2) { assert!(lane_can_move(w[0], w[1]), "{:?} → {:?}", w[0], w[1]); }
        for s in [Failed, TimedOut, Stalled] {
            assert!(lane_can_move(Streaming, s));
            assert!(lane_can_move(s, Preparing), "a retry");
            assert!(lane_can_move(s, Parked), "three tries, then parked");
        }
        assert!(lane_can_move(Closed, Fixing), "head triage reopens a closed lane");
        assert!(lane_can_move(Gating, Finishing), "resume re-gates");
        assert!(!lane_can_move(Closed, Cancelled), "a finished lane is never cancelled");
        assert!(!lane_can_move(Waiting, Streaming));
        let mut r = sample(1);
        r.lanes.push(LaneRecord::new("a"));
        r.set_lane_state("a", Preparing).unwrap();
        assert!(r.lane("a").unwrap().started.is_some());
        assert!(r.set_lane_state("a", Closed).is_err());
        assert!(Streaming.busy() && Gating.busy() && !Waiting.busy() && !Closed.busy());
        assert!(Stalled.between_tries() && !Parked.between_tries());
    }

    #[test]
    fn iso_dates_are_utc() {
        assert_eq!(iso(0), "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(iso(1_789_571_787_592), "2026-09-16T15:16:27Z");
        assert_eq!(iso(951_782_400_000), "2000-02-29T00:00:00Z");
    }

    #[test]
    fn transitions_become_journal_lines() {
        let mut a = sample(10);
        a.lane_map = Some(LaneMap { items: vec![Item { id: "T-1".into(), title: "x".into(), note: None, rewrite: None }; 7], lanes: vec![] });
        a.lanes = vec![LaneRecord::new("a"), LaneRecord::new("b")];
        let mut b = a.clone();
        b.state = RunState::Running;
        assert_eq!(transition_lines(&a, &b), vec!["Round 10 started: 2 lanes."]);
        let mut c = b.clone();
        c.lanes[1].state = LaneState::Closed;
        assert_eq!(transition_lines(&b, &c), vec!["Round 10 · lane B finished."]);
        let mut d = c.clone();
        d.state = RunState::Done;
        assert_eq!(transition_lines(&c, &d), vec!["Round 10 is saved: 7 items done."]);
        assert!(transition_lines(&d, &d).is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_live_run_is_seen_by_round_number() {
        let d = tmp("live");
        let mut r = sample(4);
        save(&d, &mut r).unwrap();
        assert!(is_live(&d, 4));
        r.state = RunState::Done;
        save(&d, &mut r).unwrap();
        assert!(!is_live(&d, 4));
        assert!(!is_live(&d, 5));
    }
}
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn a_ready_round_with_a_live_run_does_not_settle() {
        // a prompt-kind run's round has no notes, and "every note done" is vacuously
        // true — without this the round would flip to done the moment it went ready
        let d = tmp("live-run");
        save(&d, &[round(7, "ready", &[])]).unwrap();
        let mut run = crate::runs::record::RunRecord::new(7, crate::runs::record::RunKind::Prompt, Some(7), "fixes/phase_7_fixes_plan.md", "fixes/phase_7_fixes_prompt.md", "do it");
        run.state = crate::runs::record::RunState::Running;
        crate::runs::record::save(&d, &mut run).unwrap();
        settle_done(&d);
        assert_eq!(load(&d).unwrap()[0].state, "ready");
        run.state = crate::runs::record::RunState::Done;
        crate::runs::record::save(&d, &mut run).unwrap();
        settle_done(&d);
        assert_eq!(load(&d).unwrap()[0].state, "done");
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The record**

Put this above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/record.rs`:

```rust
//! `.chronicle/runs/<n>.json` — one run: a plan executed as lanes (spec §3).
//! Rust is the only writer. The two rules of rounds.rs hold here too: a file we
//! cannot parse is never written over, and nothing here can strand a note locked.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum RunKind { Notes, Plan, Prompt }

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum RunState { Planning, Running, Gating, Closing, Done, NeedsYou, Failed, Stopped, Interrupted }

impl RunState {
    pub fn terminal(self) -> bool { matches!(self, RunState::Done | RunState::Failed | RunState::Stopped) }
}

/// Symphony's attempt states (preparing … finishing, then the outcomes), plus
/// the gate's own: `finishing` is a turn that ended and waits for its gate.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum LaneState {
    Waiting, Preparing, BuildingPrompt, Launching, Initialising, Streaming, Finishing, Gating, Fixing,
    Closed, Failed, TimedOut, Stalled, Cancelled, Parked,
}

impl LaneState {
    /// Holds a slot: a session is starting, working, or being checked.
    pub fn busy(self) -> bool {
        use LaneState::*;
        matches!(self, Preparing | BuildingPrompt | Launching | Initialising | Streaming | Finishing | Gating | Fixing)
    }
    /// An attempt ended badly and the next one waits for its backoff.
    pub fn between_tries(self) -> bool { matches!(self, LaneState::Failed | LaneState::TimedOut | LaneState::Stalled) }
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Item {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    /// the note this item came from (vault-relative), when the run is from notes
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] pub note: Option<String>,
    /// the user's rewrite after the item was parked (needs-you · Rewrite the item)
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] pub rewrite: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct LaneSpec {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub items: Vec<String>,
    pub owns: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub depends_on: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub checks: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct LaneMap {
    #[serde(default)] pub items: Vec<Item>,
    pub lanes: Vec<LaneSpec>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Tokens { #[serde(rename = "in")] pub input: u64, pub out: u64 }

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct CheckResult {
    pub name: String,
    /// `None` when the check was killed or never started
    pub exit: Option<i32>,
    pub ms: u64,
    #[serde(default)] pub timed_out: bool,
    /// the last few KB of output — the fix prompt and the gate card show it
    #[serde(default)] pub tail: String,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Finding { pub file: String, pub line: u32, pub what: String }

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Review { pub model: String, pub pass: bool, #[serde(default)] pub findings: Vec<Finding> }

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Gate {
    pub commit: String,
    pub checks: Vec<CheckResult>,
    #[serde(default)] pub review: Option<Review>,
    /// e.g. "the checks ran in your project while the lanes waited" (spec §11)
    #[serde(default)] pub notice: Option<String>,
    /// how many fix passes came before this gate in its attempt (0 = the first save)
    #[serde(default)] pub fix: u32,
}

impl Gate {
    /// Exit codes are law; the review only counts once they all pass.
    pub fn passed(&self) -> bool {
        self.checks.iter().all(|c| c.exit == Some(0)) && self.review.as_ref().map_or(true, |r| r.pass)
    }
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Leak {
    pub path: String,
    /// the lane that owns the path (`None`: no lane does)
    #[serde(default)] pub owner: Option<String>,
    /// on the OWNER's row: the lane whose turn changed it
    #[serde(default)] pub by: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct LaneRecord {
    pub id: String,
    pub state: LaneState,
    pub attempt: u32,
    #[serde(default)] pub started: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub ended: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub cost_usd: f64,
    #[serde(default)] pub tokens: Tokens,
    #[serde(default)] pub commit: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub leak: Vec<Leak>,
    #[serde(default)] pub gates: Vec<Gate>,
    /// why the lane is parked (three tries, or fixes that never passed)
    #[serde(default)] pub parked: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub retry_at_ms: Option<u64>,
    /// the lane session's id — the lane view replays its transcript after a restart
    #[serde(default)] pub session: Option<String>,
    /// fix passes used in this attempt
    #[serde(default)] pub fixes: u32,
}

impl LaneRecord {
    pub fn new(id: &str) -> Self {
        LaneRecord { id: id.into(), state: LaneState::Waiting, attempt: 0, started: None, ended: None, cost_usd: 0.0,
            tokens: Tokens::default(), commit: None, leak: vec![], gates: vec![], parked: None, retry_at_ms: None, session: None, fixes: 0 }
    }
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct HeadCheck {
    pub commit: String,
    pub checks: Vec<CheckResult>,
    /// the lane triage reopened for its one fix pass
    #[serde(default)] pub reopened: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct DecisionLog {
    pub at: String,
    /// shell | block | triage | sizing | ask
    pub kind: String,
    pub model: String,
    pub choice: String,
    #[serde(default)] pub probabilities: BTreeMap<String, f32>,
    pub confidence: f32,
    /// act | second-opinion | strict
    pub band: String,
    pub action: String,
    #[serde(default)] pub self_reported: bool,
    #[serde(default)] pub lane: Option<String>,
    /// what was decided about: the command, the item, the failing check
    #[serde(default)] pub subject: String,
    /// the half-sentence the decision line ends with ("it reads only")
    #[serde(default)] pub reason: String,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct NeedsYou {
    /// parked | exhausted | head | refused
    pub kind: String,
    #[serde(default)] pub lane: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub item: Option<String>,
    pub reason: String,
    /// the judge's reading (yes-probability "impossible as written") for a parked item
    #[serde(default)] pub reading: Option<f32>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Cost { pub usd: f64, #[serde(rename = "in")] pub input: u64, pub out: u64 }

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct RunRecord {
    pub n: u64,
    pub round: Option<u64>,
    pub kind: RunKind,
    pub state: RunState,
    pub created: String,
    pub updated: String,
    pub plan_path: String,
    pub prompt_path: String,
    /// the prompt text (prompt kind) or the plan path (plan kind)
    #[serde(default)] pub source: String,
    pub lane_map: Option<LaneMap>,
    pub lanes: Vec<LaneRecord>,
    pub head_check: Option<HeadCheck>,
    pub decisions: Vec<DecisionLog>,
    pub needs_you: Vec<NeedsYou>,
    pub cost: Cost,
    #[serde(default)] pub planner_tries: u32,
    #[serde(default)] pub planner_error: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)] pub planner_cost_usd: f64,
    #[serde(default)] pub notices: Vec<String>,
    /// the lane count the sizing decision told the planner to aim for
    #[serde(default)] pub target_lanes: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)] pub interrupted_from: Option<RunState>,
    /// items the user skipped from needs-you; their notes went back to the queue
    #[serde(default)] pub skipped: Vec<String>,
    /// the check names the final check is running right now (the card lists them)
    #[serde(default)] pub head_pending: Vec<String>,
}

pub fn can_move(from: RunState, to: RunState) -> bool {
    use RunState::*;
    if from == to { return true; }
    matches!((from, to),
        (Planning, Running | Failed | Stopped | Interrupted)
        | (Running, Gating | NeedsYou | Failed | Stopped | Interrupted)
        | (Gating, Closing | Running | NeedsYou | Stopped | Interrupted)
        | (Closing, Done | Interrupted)
        | (NeedsYou, Running | Gating | Closing | Stopped)
        | (Interrupted, Planning | Running | Gating | Closing | Stopped))
}

pub fn lane_can_move(from: LaneState, to: LaneState) -> bool {
    use LaneState::*;
    if from == to { return true; }
    if to == Cancelled { return from != Closed; }
    matches!((from, to),
        (Waiting, Preparing)
        | (Preparing, BuildingPrompt) | (BuildingPrompt, Launching) | (Launching, Initialising) | (Initialising, Streaming)
        | (Streaming, Finishing)
        | (Finishing, Gating) | (Finishing, Streaming)
        | (Gating, Closed | Fixing | Parked | Finishing)
        | (Fixing, Finishing)
        | (Preparing | BuildingPrompt | Launching | Initialising | Streaming | Finishing | Gating | Fixing, Failed | TimedOut | Stalled)
        | (Preparing | BuildingPrompt | Launching | Initialising | Streaming | Fixing, Waiting)
        | (Failed | TimedOut | Stalled, Preparing | Parked)
        | (Parked, Waiting)
        | (Cancelled, Waiting)
        | (Closed, Fixing))
}

impl RunRecord {
    pub fn new(n: u64, kind: RunKind, round: Option<u64>, plan_path: &str, prompt_path: &str, source: &str) -> Self {
        let now = iso_now();
        RunRecord { n, round, kind, state: RunState::Planning, created: now.clone(), updated: now,
            plan_path: plan_path.into(), prompt_path: prompt_path.into(), source: source.into(),
            lane_map: None, lanes: vec![], head_check: None, decisions: vec![], needs_you: vec![], cost: Cost::default(),
            planner_tries: 0, planner_error: None, planner_cost_usd: 0.0, notices: vec![], target_lanes: None,
            interrupted_from: None, skipped: vec![], head_pending: vec![] }
    }
    pub fn round_n(&self) -> u64 { self.round.unwrap_or(self.n) }
    pub fn set_state(&mut self, to: RunState) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !can_move(self.state, to) { return Err(format!("a run can't go from {:?} to {to:?}", self.state)); }
        self.state = to;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn lane(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&LaneRecord> { self.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == id) }
    pub fn lane_mut(&mut self, id: &str) -> Option<&mut LaneRecord> { self.lanes.iter_mut().find(|l| l.id == id) }
    pub fn set_lane_state(&mut self, id: &str, to: LaneState) -> Result<(), String> {
        let l = self.lane_mut(id).ok_or_else(|| format!("there's no lane {id}"))?;
        if !lane_can_move(l.state, to) { return Err(format!("lane {id} can't go from {:?} to {to:?}", l.state)); }
        if to == LaneState::Preparing { l.started = Some(iso_now()); l.ended = None; }
        if matches!(to, LaneState::Closed | LaneState::Parked | LaneState::Cancelled) { l.ended = Some(iso_now()); }
        l.state = to;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn total_cost(&self) -> Cost {
        Cost {
            usd: self.planner_cost_usd + self.lanes.iter().map(|l| l.cost_usd).sum::<f64>(),
            input: self.lanes.iter().map(|l| l.tokens.input).sum(),
            out: self.lanes.iter().map(|l| l.tokens.out).sum(),
        }
    }
}

/// What the round record says while its run is in a given state (spec §7:
/// "the round's state follows the run"). Live rounds lock their notes.
pub fn round_state_for(s: RunState) -> &'static str {
    match s {
        RunState::Planning => "generating",
        RunState::Done => "done",
        RunState::Failed | RunState::Stopped => "failed",
        _ => "ready",
    }
}

pub fn runs_dir(dir: &Path) -> PathBuf { dir.join(".chronicle/runs") }
pub fn path(dir: &Path, n: u64) -> PathBuf { runs_dir(dir).join(format!("{n}.json")) }

/// `Ok(None)`: no record. `Err`: a record we can't read — never overwritten.
pub fn load(dir: &Path, n: u64) -> Result<Option<RunRecord>, String> {
    let text = match std::fs::read_to_string(path(dir, n)) {
        Ok(t) => t,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(e) => return Err(format!("couldn't read .chronicle/runs/{n}.json: {e}")),
    };
    if text.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
    serde_json::from_str(&text).map(Some)
        .map_err(|e| format!(".chronicle/runs/{n}.json can't be read ({e}) — fix or move that file, then try again"))
}

pub fn save(dir: &Path, rec: &mut RunRecord) -> Result<(), String> {
    load(dir, rec.n)?; // an unreadable file stays exactly as it is
    std::fs::create_dir_all(runs_dir(dir)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    rec.updated = iso_now();
    let body = serde_json::to_string_pretty(rec).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let p = path(dir, rec.n);
    let tmp = p.with_extension("json.tmp");
    std::fs::write(&tmp, body).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    std::fs::rename(&tmp, &p).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

/// Every readable record, oldest first. An unreadable one is skipped, not fatal.
pub fn list(dir: &Path) -> Vec<RunRecord> {
    let Ok(rd) = std::fs::read_dir(runs_dir(dir)) else { return vec![] };
    let mut out: Vec<RunRecord> = rd.filter_map(|e| e.ok())
        .filter_map(|e| e.file_name().to_str()?.strip_suffix(".json")?.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .filter_map(|n| load(dir, n).ok().flatten())
        .collect();
    out.sort_by_key(|r| r.n);
    out
}

pub fn is_live(dir: &Path, round: u64) -> bool {
    list(dir).iter().any(|r| r.round_n() == round && !r.state.terminal())
}

/// RFC 3339 UTC, to the second (Howard Hinnant's civil-from-days).
pub fn iso(ms: u64) -> String {
    let secs = ms / 1000;
    let days = (secs / 86_400) as i64;
    let rem = secs % 86_400;
    let (h, mi, s) = (rem / 3600, (rem % 3600) / 60, rem % 60);
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z.rem_euclid(146_097);
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let mo = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let y = yoe + era * 400 + if mo <= 2 { 1 } else { 0 };
    format!("{y:04}-{mo:02}-{d:02}T{h:02}:{mi:02}:{s:02}Z")
}
pub fn iso_now() -> String { iso(crate::epoch_ms()) }

/// One journal line per transition the user would want to hear about (spec §8).
pub fn transition_lines(before: &RunRecord, after: &RunRecord) -> Vec<String> {
    let n = after.round_n();
    let mut out = vec![];
    if before.state != after.state {
        let items = after.lane_map.as_ref().map(|m| m.items.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        match after.state {
            RunState::Running if before.state == RunState::Planning => out.push(format!("Round {n} started: {} lanes.", after.lanes.len())),
            RunState::Gating => out.push(format!("Round {n}: every lane passed; checking the whole project.")),
            RunState::Done => out.push(format!("Round {n} is saved: {items} items done.")),
            RunState::NeedsYou => out.push(format!("Round {n} needs you.")),
            RunState::Stopped => out.push(format!("Round {n} was stopped.")),
            RunState::Failed => out.push(format!("Round {n} couldn't start: the plan couldn't be split into lanes.")),
            RunState::Interrupted => out.push(format!("Round {n} was interrupted.")),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    for l in &after.lanes {
        let was = before.lane(&l.id).map(|b| b.state);
        if was == Some(l.state) { continue; }
        match l.state {
            LaneState::Closed => out.push(format!("Round {n} · lane {} finished.", l.id.to_uppercase())),
            LaneState::Parked => out.push(format!("Round {n} · lane {} needs you.", l.id.to_uppercase())),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    out
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `journal_append_in`, and `settle_done` skips live runs**

In `src-tauri/src/main.rs`, replace the body of `journal_append` with a call to a new free function, defined directly above it:

```rust
/// One journal line, written by Rust (runs) or by the frontend's command below.
pub(crate) fn journal_append_in(dir: &Path, kind: &str, text: &str) {
    let e = json!({ "kind": kind, "text": text, "ts": epoch_ms() });
    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join(".chronicle"));
    use std::io::Write;
    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(journal_path(dir)) {
        let _ = writeln!(f, "{}", e);
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
async fn journal_append(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, dir: String, entry: Value) -> Result<(), String> {
    let p = project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    if !entry.is_object() { return Err("malformed journal entry".into()); }
    let mut e = entry;
    if let Some(obj) = e.as_object_mut() { obj.insert("ts".into(), json!(epoch_ms())); }
    std::fs::create_dir_all(p.dir.join(".chronicle")).map_err(|x| x.to_string())?;
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut f = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true)
        .open(journal_path(&p.dir)).map_err(|x| x.to_string())?;
    writeln!(f, "{}", serde_json::to_string(&e).map_err(|x| x.to_string())?).map_err(|x| x.to_string())
}
```

(The command keeps its own body: the frontend's entries carry more keys than `kind` and `text`.)

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn tmp(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-checks-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&d).unwrap();
        d
    }

    #[test]
    fn checks_are_detected_from_package_scripts_and_a_cargo_crate() {
        let d = tmp("detect");
        std::fs::write(d.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{"test":"vitest run","typecheck":"tsc -b","dev":"vite","build":"vite build"}}"#).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join("src-tauri")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(d.join("src-tauri/Cargo.toml"), "[package]\nname='x'\n").unwrap();
        let names: Vec<String> = detect(&d).into_iter().map(|c| c.name).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, vec!["test", "typecheck", "build", "cargo test"], "dev is not a check; lint is absent");
        let cargo = detect(&d).into_iter().find(|c| c.name == "cargo test").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cargo.command, "cd 'src-tauri' && cargo test");
        assert_eq!(detect(&d)[0].command, "npm run test");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_manifest_list_wins_and_nothing_else_runs() {
        let d = tmp("manifest");
        std::fs::write(d.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{"test":"vitest run"}}"#).unwrap();
        let m = json!({ "checks": [{ "name": "unit", "command": "make unit" }, { "name": "", "command": "x" }] });
        assert_eq!(repo_checks(&d, Some(&m)), vec![Check { name: "unit".into(), command: "make unit".into() }]);
        let m2 = json!({ "checks": { "lint": "npm run lint" } });
        assert_eq!(repo_checks(&d, Some(&m2))[0].name, "lint");
        assert_eq!(repo_checks(&d, None)[0].name, "test", "detected when the manifest has no list");
    }

    #[test]
    fn max_lanes_defaults_to_three_and_stays_sane() {
        assert_eq!(max_lanes(None), 3);
        assert_eq!(max_lanes(Some(&json!({ "maxLanes": 5 }))), 5);
        assert_eq!(max_lanes(Some(&json!({ "maxLanes": 0 }))), 1);
        assert_eq!(max_lanes(Some(&json!({ "maxLanes": 99 }))), 8);
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::checks::Check;

    fn repo(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-map-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        for p in ["src/lib", "src/screens/agent", "src-tauri/src"] { std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join(p)).unwrap(); }
        std::fs::write(d.join("src/lib/a.ts"), "").unwrap();
        d.canonicalize().unwrap()
    }
    fn lane(id: &str, items: &[&str], owns: &[&str], deps: &[&str], checks: &[&str]) -> LaneSpec {
        let v = |xs: &[&str]| xs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        LaneSpec { id: id.into(), title: format!("Lane {id}"), items: v(items), owns: v(owns), depends_on: v(deps), checks: v(checks) }
    }
    fn map(items: &[&str], lanes: Vec<LaneSpec>) -> LaneMap {
        LaneMap { items: items.iter().map(|i| Item { id: i.to_string(), title: format!("item {i}"), note: None, rewrite: None }).collect(), lanes }
    }
    fn checks() -> Vec<Check> {
        vec![Check { name: "test".into(), command: "npm run test".into() }, Check { name: "typecheck".into(), command: "npm run typecheck".into() }]
    }
    fn errs(r: Result<LaneMap, Vec<String>>) -> String { r.expect_err("the map must be refused").join("\n") }

    #[test]
    fn globs_match_the_way_a_planner_writes_them() {
        let g = |s: &str| Glob::parse(s).unwrap();
        assert!(g("src/lib/*.ts").matches("src/lib/a.ts"));
        assert!(!g("src/lib/*.ts").matches("src/lib/x/a.ts"));
        assert!(g("src/**").matches("src/a/b/c.ts"));
        assert!(g("src/**/*.ts").matches("src/a.ts") && g("src/**/*.ts").matches("src/a/b.ts"));
        assert_eq!(g("src/lib/").text, "src/lib/**");
        assert_eq!(g("./src/a.ts").text, "src/a.ts");
        assert_eq!(g("src/lib/*.ts").static_prefix(), "src/lib/");
        assert_eq!(g("src/a.ts").static_prefix(), "src/a.ts");
        assert_eq!(g("**/*.css").static_prefix(), "");
        for bad in ["", "/etc/passwd", "../x", "src/../../x"] { assert!(Glob::parse(bad).is_err(), "{bad}"); }
    }

    #[test]
    fn overlap_is_conservative_but_not_silly() {
        let o = |a: &str, b: &str| overlap(&Glob::parse(a).unwrap(), &Glob::parse(b).unwrap());
        assert!(o("src/a.ts", "src/a.ts"));
        assert!(!o("src/a.ts", "src/b.ts"));
        assert!(o("src/lib/a.ts", "src/lib/*.ts"));
        assert!(o("src/**", "src/lib/*.ts"));
        assert!(!o("src/**", "src-tauri/**"), "a shared spelling is not a shared folder");
        assert!(!o("src/*.ts", "src/*.css"));
        assert!(o("src/*.test.ts", "src/*.ts"));
        assert!(o("**", "src/a.ts"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_valid_map_passes_and_its_globs_come_back_normalised() {
        let r = repo("valid");
        let m = map(&["T-1", "T-2", "T-3"], vec![
            lane("a", &["T-1", "T-2"], &["src/lib/", "src/screens/agent/AgentPane.tsx"], &[], &["test", "typecheck"]),
            lane("b", &["T-3"], &["src-tauri/src/**"], &["a"], &[]),
        ]);
        let ok = validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ok.lanes[0].owns, vec!["src/lib/**", "src/screens/agent/AgentPane.tsx"]);
        assert_eq!(owner_of(&ok, "src/lib/x.ts").as_deref(), Some("a"));
        assert_eq!(owner_of(&ok, "src-tauri/src/acp.rs").as_deref(), Some("b"));
        assert_eq!(owner_of(&ok, "README.md"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_directory_named_without_a_slash_is_owned_whole() {
        let r = repo("dir");
        let m = map(&["T-1"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1"], &["src/lib"], &[], &[])]);
        assert_eq!(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[]).unwrap().lanes[0].owns, vec!["src/lib/**"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn overlapping_owned_paths_are_refused_with_the_rule() {
        let r = repo("overlap");
        let m = map(&["T-1", "T-2"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1"], &["src/lib/**"], &[], &[]), lane("b", &["T-2"], &["src/lib/a.ts"], &[], &[])]);
        let e = errs(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[]));
        assert!(e.contains("Lanes a and b both own"), "{e}");
        assert!(e.contains("make the other depend on it"), "{e}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn owned_globs_that_meet_through_a_symlink_overlap() {
        let r = repo("symlink");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(r.join("lib/shared")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("lib/shared/x.ts"), "").unwrap();
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("../lib/shared", r.join("src/shared")).unwrap();
        let m = map(&["T-1", "T-2"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1"], &["src/shared/**"], &[], &[]), lane("b", &["T-2"], &["lib/shared/x.ts"], &[], &[])]);
        let e = errs(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[]));
        assert!(e.contains("Lanes a and b both own"), "the symlink is followed before comparing: {e}");
        // and a link that leaves the project is refused outright
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("/tmp", r.join("src/out")).unwrap();
        let m2 = map(&["T-1"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1"], &["src/out/**"], &[], &[])]);
        assert!(errs(validate(&m2, &r, &checks(), &[])).contains("outside the project"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_item_goes_in_exactly_one_lane_and_every_note_is_covered() {
        let r = repo("items");
        let m = map(&["T-1", "T-2", "T-3"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1", "T-2"], &["src/lib/**"], &[], &[]), lane("b", &["T-2", "T-9"], &["src-tauri/**"], &[], &[])]);
        let e = errs(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &["Tasks/A.md".into()]));
        assert!(e.contains("Item T-2 is in lane a and lane b"), "{e}");
        assert!(e.contains("Item T-3 isn't in any lane"), "{e}");
        assert!(e.contains("names item T-9, which isn't in the items list"), "{e}");
        assert!(e.contains("The note Tasks/A.md isn't covered by any item"), "{e}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn dependencies_must_exist_and_never_loop() {
        let r = repo("deps");
        let m = map(&["T-1", "T-2", "T-3"], vec![
            lane("a", &["T-1"], &["src/lib/**"], &["c"], &[]),
            lane("b", &["T-2"], &["src/screens/**"], &["a", "z"], &[]),
            lane("c", &["T-3"], &["src-tauri/**"], &["b"], &[]),
        ]);
        let e = errs(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[]));
        assert!(e.contains("depends on lane z, which isn't in the map"), "{e}");
        assert!(e.contains("in a circle: a → c → b → a"), "{e}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn checks_come_from_the_repos_list_and_nowhere_else() {
        let r = repo("checks");
        let m = map(&["T-1"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1"], &["src/lib/**"], &[], &["test", "rm -rf /"])]);
        let e = errs(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[]));
        assert!(e.contains("names the check \"rm -rf /\"; this project's checks are: test, typecheck"), "{e}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_lane_owns_chronicles_own_files() {
        let r = repo("forbidden");
        for owns in [".chronicle/notes/**", "fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md", "chronicle.json", "**"] {
            let m = map(&["T-1"], vec![lane("a", &["T-1"], &[owns], &[], &[])]);
            assert!(errs(validate(&m, &r, &checks(), &[])).contains("lanes never write those"), "{owns}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn ids_titles_and_shapes_are_checked() {
        let r = repo("shape");
        let mut bad = lane("AB", &[], &[], &[], &[]);
        bad.title = " ".into();
        let e = errs(validate(&map(&[], vec![bad]), &r, &checks(), &[]));
        for s in ["must be one lowercase letter", "has no title", "has no items", "owns no files", "lists no items"] { assert!(e.contains(s), "{s}: {e}"); }
        assert!(errs(validate(&map(&["T-1"], vec![]), &r, &checks(), &[])).contains("The map has no lanes."));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The check list**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/checks.rs`:

```rust
//! The checks a lane may name (spec §7): `chronicle.json` `checks` when present,
//! else detected — package.json scripts `test`, `typecheck`, `lint`, `build`, and
//! `cargo test` where a Cargo.toml is found. Each is a name mapped to a command
//! Chronicle owns. Nothing else ever runs.
use serde::Serialize;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct Check { pub name: String, pub command: String }

pub fn repo_checks(repo: &Path, manifest: Option<&Value>) -> Vec<Check> {
    match manifest.and_then(|m| m.get("checks")) {
        Some(list) => from_manifest(list),
        None => detect(repo),
    }
}

fn from_manifest(v: &Value) -> Vec<Check> {
    let pairs: Vec<(String, String)> = match v {
        Value::Array(a) => a.iter().filter_map(|c| Some((c.get("name")?.as_str()?.to_string(), c.get("command")?.as_str()?.to_string()))).collect(),
        Value::Object(o) => o.iter().filter_map(|(k, c)| Some((k.clone(), c.as_str()?.to_string()))).collect(),
        _ => vec![],
    };
    pairs.into_iter()
        .map(|(n, c)| Check { name: n.trim().to_string(), command: c.trim().to_string() })
        .filter(|c| !c.name.is_empty() && !c.command.is_empty())
        .collect()
}

pub fn detect(repo: &Path) -> Vec<Check> {
    let mut out = vec![];
    if let Some(pkg) = std::fs::read_to_string(repo.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|t| serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&t).ok()) {
        for name in ["test", "typecheck", "lint", "build"] {
            if pkg.pointer(&format!("/scripts/{name}")).and_then(|v| v.as_str()).is_some() {
                out.push(Check { name: name.into(), command: format!("npm run {name}") });
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(dir) = cargo_dir(repo) {
        let command = match dir.strip_prefix(repo).ok().filter(|r| !r.as_os_str().is_empty()) {
            Some(rel) => format!("cd '{}' && cargo test", rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\'', "'\\''")),
            None => "cargo test".to_string(),
        };
        out.push(Check { name: "cargo test".into(), command });
    }
    out
}

/// The repo root when it holds a Cargo.toml, else the first child folder that does.
pub fn cargo_dir(repo: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if repo.join("Cargo.toml").is_file() { return Some(repo.to_path_buf()); }
    let mut kids: Vec<PathBuf> = std::fs::read_dir(repo).ok()?.filter_map(|e| e.ok()).map(|e| e.path())
        .filter(|p| p.is_dir())
        .filter(|p| p.file_name().map(|n| { let n = n.to_string_lossy(); !n.starts_with('.') && n != "node_modules" && n != "target" }).unwrap_or(false))
        .collect();
    kids.sort();
    kids.into_iter().find(|k| k.join("Cargo.toml").is_file())
}

/// How many lanes run at once (spec §3, §15.4): `maxLanes`, default 3, kept within 1–8.
pub fn max_lanes(manifest: Option<&Value>) -> usize {
    manifest.and_then(|m| m.get("maxLanes")).and_then(|v| v.as_u64()).map(|n| n.clamp(1, 8) as usize).unwrap_or(3)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The lane map**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/lane_map.rs`:

```rust
//! The planner's lane map, checked before anything runs (spec §3): owned paths
//! pairwise disjoint (after following symlinks), every item in exactly one lane,
//! every round note covered, dependencies known and acyclic, checks from the
//! repo's list. An invalid map goes back to the planner with every reason at once.
use super::checks::Check;
use super::record::{Item, LaneMap, LaneSpec};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::Path;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Glob { pub text: String, re: regex::Regex }

impl Glob {
    /// Repo-relative only. A trailing `/` owns the folder (`src/lib/` → `src/lib/**`).
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Glob, String> {
        let mut t = s.trim().trim_start_matches("./").to_string();
        if t.is_empty() { return Err("an owned path is empty".into()); }
        if t.starts_with('/') { return Err(format!("{t} is absolute; owned paths are relative to the project")); }
        if t.split('/').any(|p| p == "..") { return Err(format!("{t} climbs out with ..")); }
        if t.ends_with('/') { t.push_str("**"); }
        let re = regex::Regex::new(&to_regex(&t)).map_err(|e| format!("{t} isn't a path pattern ({e})"))?;
        Ok(Glob { text: t, re })
    }
    pub fn matches(&self, rel: &str) -> bool { self.re.is_match(rel) }
    pub fn has_wildcard(&self) -> bool { self.text.contains('*') || self.text.contains('?') }
    /// Up to the last `/` before the first wildcard; the whole text when literal.
    pub fn static_prefix(&self) -> &str {
        match self.text.find(|c| c == '*' || c == '?') {
            None => &self.text,
            Some(i) => match self.text[..i].rfind('/') { Some(j) => &self.text[..=j], None => "" },
        }
    }
}

fn to_regex(g: &str) -> String {
    let cs: Vec<char> = g.chars().collect();
    let mut re = String::from("^");
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < cs.len() {
        match cs[i] {
            '*' if cs.get(i + 1) == Some(&'*') => {
                if cs.get(i + 2) == Some(&'/') { re.push_str("(?:.*/)?"); i += 3; } else { re.push_str(".*"); i += 2; }
                continue;
            }
            '*' => re.push_str("[^/]*"),
            '?' => re.push_str("[^/]"),
            c => re.push_str(&regex::escape(&c.to_string())),
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    re.push('$');
    re
}

fn literal_suffix(t: &str) -> &str {
    match t.rfind(|c| c == '*' || c == '?') { Some(i) => &t[i + 1..], None => t }
}

/// Could one path be owned by both? Exact for literals; for two patterns,
/// "yes" unless their folders diverge or their fixed endings can't both match.
pub fn overlap(a: &Glob, b: &Glob) -> bool {
    match (a.has_wildcard(), b.has_wildcard()) {
        (false, false) => a.text == b.text,
        (false, true) => b.matches(&a.text),
        (true, false) => a.matches(&b.text),
        (true, true) => {
            let (pa, pb) = (a.static_prefix(), b.static_prefix());
            if !(pa.starts_with(pb) || pb.starts_with(pa)) { return false; }
            let (sa, sb) = (literal_suffix(&a.text), literal_suffix(&b.text));
            !( !sa.is_empty() && !sb.is_empty() && !sa.ends_with(sb) && !sb.ends_with(sa) )
        }
    }
}

/// Rewrite the fixed part of a pattern to where it really is: symlinks
/// followed, relative to the project's canonical root. Refuses a pattern
/// whose fixed part resolves outside the project.
pub fn normalise(repo: &Path, g: &Glob) -> Result<Glob, String> {
    let prefix = g.static_prefix().to_string();
    let rest = g.text[prefix.len()..].to_string();
    let croot = repo.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut existing = repo.join(prefix.trim_end_matches('/'));
    let mut tail: Vec<String> = vec![];
    while !existing.exists() {
        match existing.file_name() { Some(n) => tail.push(n.to_string_lossy().into_owned()), None => break }
        if !existing.pop() { break; }
    }
    let canon = existing.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let rel = canon.strip_prefix(&croot).map_err(|_| format!("{} reaches outside the project", g.text))?;
    let mut parts: Vec<String> = rel.components().map(|c| c.as_os_str().to_string_lossy().into_owned()).collect();
    parts.extend(tail.into_iter().rev());
    let mut text = parts.join("/");
    if prefix.ends_with('/') && !text.is_empty() { text.push('/'); }
    text.push_str(&rest);
    Glob::parse(&text)
}

/// `.chronicle/`, `fixes/`, `chronicle.json`: the app's and the planner's, never a lane's.
pub fn forbidden_path(rel: &str) -> bool {
    rel == "chronicle.json" || rel.starts_with(".chronicle/") || rel == ".chronicle" || rel.starts_with("fixes/") || rel == "fixes"
}

fn reaches_forbidden(g: &Glob) -> bool {
    ["chronicle.json", ".chronicle/**", "fixes/**"].iter().any(|f| overlap(g, &Glob::parse(f).expect("fixed pattern")))
}

pub fn compile(owns: &[String]) -> Vec<Glob> { owns.iter().filter_map(|o| Glob::parse(o).ok()).collect() }

pub fn owner_of(map: &LaneMap, rel: &str) -> Option<String> {
    map.lanes.iter().find(|l| compile(&l.owns).iter().any(|g| g.matches(rel))).map(|l| l.id.clone())
}

fn find_cycle(lanes: &[LaneSpec]) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    let deps: HashMap<&str, Vec<&str>> = lanes.iter().map(|l| (l.id.as_str(), l.depends_on.iter().map(|d| d.as_str()).collect())).collect();
    fn visit<'a>(n: &'a str, deps: &HashMap<&'a str, Vec<&'a str>>, color: &mut HashMap<&'a str, u8>, stack: &mut Vec<&'a str>) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
        color.insert(n, 1);
        stack.push(n);
        for &d in deps.get(n).map(|v| v.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&[]) {
            if !deps.contains_key(d) { continue; }
            match color.get(d).copied().unwrap_or(0) {
                1 => {
                    let from = stack.iter().position(|&s| s == d).unwrap_or(0);
                    let mut cyc: Vec<String> = stack[from..].iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
                    cyc.push(d.to_string());
                    return Some(cyc);
                }
                0 => if let Some(c) = visit(d, deps, color, stack) { return Some(c); },
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        stack.pop();
        color.insert(n, 2);
        None
    }
    let mut color = HashMap::new();
    for l in lanes {
        if color.get(l.id.as_str()).copied().unwrap_or(0) == 0 {
            if let Some(c) = visit(&l.id, &deps, &mut color, &mut vec![]) { return Some(c); }
        }
    }
    None
}

pub fn validate(map: &LaneMap, repo: &Path, checks: &[Check], round_notes: &[String]) -> Result<LaneMap, Vec<String>> {
    let mut errs: Vec<String> = vec![];
    let mut out = map.clone();
    if map.lanes.is_empty() { errs.push("The map has no lanes.".into()); }
    if map.lanes.len() > 26 { errs.push("A map can have at most 26 lanes.".into()); }
    let mut ids: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
    for l in &map.lanes {
        if !(l.id.len() == 1 && l.id.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase())) { errs.push(format!("Lane id \"{}\" must be one lowercase letter.", l.id)); }
        if !ids.insert(l.id.clone()) { errs.push(format!("Lane id \"{}\" is used twice.", l.id)); }
        if l.title.trim().is_empty() { errs.push(format!("Lane {} has no title.", l.id)); }
        if l.items.is_empty() { errs.push(format!("Lane {} has no items.", l.id)); }
        if l.owns.is_empty() { errs.push(format!("Lane {} owns no files.", l.id)); }
    }
    let declared: Vec<&str> = map.items.iter().map(|i: &Item| i.id.as_str()).collect();
    if declared.is_empty() { errs.push("The map lists no items.".into()); }
    let mut dup = HashSet::new();
    for d in &declared { if !dup.insert(*d) { errs.push(format!("Item {d} is listed twice.")); } }
    let mut seen: HashMap<&str, &str> = HashMap::new();
    for l in &map.lanes {
        for it in &l.items {
            if !declared.contains(&it.as_str()) { errs.push(format!("Lane {} names item {it}, which isn't in the items list.", l.id)); }
            if let Some(prev) = seen.insert(it.as_str(), l.id.as_str()) {
                errs.push(format!("Item {it} is in lane {prev} and lane {}; every item goes in exactly one lane.", l.id));
            }
        }
    }
    for d in &declared { if !seen.contains_key(d) { errs.push(format!("Item {d} isn't in any lane.")); } }
    for note in round_notes {
        if !map.items.iter().any(|i| i.note.as_deref() == Some(note.as_str())) { errs.push(format!("The note {note} isn't covered by any item.")); }
    }
    let mut compiled: Vec<(String, Vec<Glob>)> = vec![];
    for (li, l) in map.lanes.iter().enumerate() {
        let mut gs = vec![];
        for o in &l.owns {
            let g = Glob::parse(o).and_then(|g| normalise(repo, &g)).and_then(|g| {
                // a folder named without its slash is owned whole
                if !g.has_wildcard() && repo.join(&g.text).is_dir() { Glob::parse(&format!("{}/**", g.text)) } else { Ok(g) }
            });
            match g {
                Ok(g) if reaches_forbidden(&g) => errs.push(format!("Lane {} owns {}, which reaches .chronicle/, fixes/ or chronicle.json; lanes never write those.", l.id, g.text)),
                Ok(g) => gs.push(g),
                Err(e) => errs.push(format!("Lane {}: {e}.", l.id)),
            }
        }
        out.lanes[li].owns = gs.iter().map(|g| g.text.clone()).collect();
        compiled.push((l.id.clone(), gs));
    }
    for i in 0..compiled.len() {
        for j in i + 1..compiled.len() {
            for a in &compiled[i].1 {
                for b in &compiled[j].1 {
                    if overlap(a, b) {
                        errs.push(format!("Lanes {} and {} both own {} / {}: owned paths must not overlap. Give the shared file to one lane and make the other depend on it.", compiled[i].0, compiled[j].0, a.text, b.text));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for l in &map.lanes {
        for d in &l.depends_on {
            if d == &l.id { errs.push(format!("Lane {} depends on itself.", l.id)); }
            else if !ids.contains(d) { errs.push(format!("Lane {} depends on lane {d}, which isn't in the map.", l.id)); }
        }
    }
    if let Some(c) = find_cycle(&map.lanes) { errs.push(format!("Lanes depend on each other in a circle: {}.", c.join(" → "))); }
    let known: Vec<&str> = checks.iter().map(|c| c.name.as_str()).collect();
    for l in &map.lanes {
        for c in &l.checks {
            if !known.contains(&c.as_str()) {
                errs.push(format!("Lane {} names the check \"{c}\"; this project's checks are: {}.", l.id, if known.is_empty() { "none".to_string() } else { known.join(", ") }));
            }
        }
    }
    if errs.is_empty() { Ok(out) } else { Err(errs) }
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `src-tauri/src/runs/fence.rs`

- Test: `#[cfg(test)]` in `fence.rs`; the existing design-fence tests in `acp.rs` must stay green unchanged

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `lane_map::{Glob, forbidden_path}` (Task 2); `crate::acp::{strictly_inside, canonical_deep}`.
- Produces (in `crate::runs::fence`):
  - `pub(crate) const WRITE_TOOLS: [&str; 4]`; `pub(crate) fn tool_name(params: &Value) -> Option<&str>`; `pub(crate) fn write_target(call: &Value) -> &str`
  - `pub enum Fence { Design(PathBuf), Lane { repo: PathBuf, lane: String, owns: Vec<Glob>, others: Vec<(String, Vec<Glob>)> } }`
  - `Fence::verdict(&self, params: &Value) -> Verdict`; `Fence::may_write(&self, abs: &Path) -> Result<(), String>`
  - `#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)] pub enum Verdict { Allow { what: String }, Refuse { path: String, reason: String }, Publish { command: String }, Judge { command: String }, NotMine }`

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    #[test]
    fn every_read_only_git_verb_is_allowed() {
        for c in ["git status", "git diff --stat", "git log --oneline -5", "git show HEAD", "git blame src/a.ts",
                  "git ls-files", "git grep foo", "git rev-parse HEAD", "git branch", "git branch --show-current",
                  "git -C src-tauri log --oneline", "git --no-pager diff", "git status 2>&1"] {
            assert_eq!(classify(c), Shell::ReadOnlyGit, "{c}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_other_git_verb_is_refused() {
        for c in ["git commit -m x", "git add -A", "git add src/a.ts", "git checkout -- src", "git reset --hard", "git stash",
                  "git merge main", "git rebase main", "git cherry-pick abc", "git restore src", "git rm x", "git mv a b",
                  "git clean -fd", "git switch main", "git pull", "git fetch", "git branch -D x", "git init", "git apply p",
                  "git am p", "git revert HEAD", "git worktree add ../x"] {
            assert_eq!(classify(c), Shell::GitWrite, "{c}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn push_tag_publish_and_release_are_publish_wherever_they_hide() {
        for c in ["git push", "git push origin main", "git tag v1", "gh release create v1", "npm publish", "pnpm publish --tag next",
                  "yarn npm publish", "cargo publish", "./release.sh 0.12.0", "bash scripts/release.sh", "sh -c \"git push\"",
                  "FOO=1 git push", "sudo git push", "env -i git tag x", "git status && git push", "echo $(git push)",
                  "echo `git tag v1`", "cd src; npm publish"] {
            assert_eq!(classify(c), Shell::Publish, "{c}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn quoting_is_respected() {
        assert_eq!(classify("echo \"git push\""), Shell::Other, "a string that mentions git push is not git push");
        assert_eq!(classify("git commit -m \"a && git push\""), Shell::GitWrite, "the && is inside the message");
        assert_eq!(classify("echo 'npm publish'"), Shell::Other);
        assert_eq!(classify("cd src && git status"), Shell::Other, "a chain is as severe as its worst part");
        assert_eq!(classify("npm test 2>&1 | tail -5"), Shell::Other);
        assert_eq!(split_words("git commit -m \"two words\" 'x y' a\\ b"), vec!["git", "commit", "-m", "two words", "x y", "a b"]);
    }

    fn ask(tool: &str, kind: &str, input: serde_json::Value) -> serde_json::Value {
        json!({ "toolCall": { "toolCallId": "t1", "name": tool, "kind": kind, "rawInput": input },
                "options": [{ "optionId": "allow", "kind": "allow_once" }, { "optionId": "reject", "kind": "reject_once" }] })
    }

    fn lane_fence(repo: &Path) -> Fence {
        Fence::Lane {
            repo: repo.to_path_buf(), lane: "a".into(),
            owns: vec![Glob::parse("src/lib/**").unwrap()],
            others: vec![("c".into(), vec![Glob::parse("src/index.css").unwrap()])],
        }
    }
    fn repo(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-fence-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join("src/lib")).unwrap();
        d.canonicalize().unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_writes_its_own_files_and_nothing_else() {
        let r = repo("writes");
        let f = lane_fence(&r);
        let p = |rel: &str| r.join(rel).to_string_lossy().into_owned();
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("Write", "edit", json!({ "file_path": p("src/lib/a.ts") }))), Verdict::Allow { .. }));
        match f.verdict(&ask("Edit", "edit", json!({ "file_path": p("src/index.css") }))) {
            Verdict::Refuse { reason, .. } => assert_eq!(reason, "src/index.css belongs to lane C — lane A can't change it."),
            v => panic!("{v:?}"),
        }
        match f.verdict(&ask("Write", "edit", json!({ "file_path": p("README.md") }))) {
            Verdict::Refuse { reason, .. } => assert_eq!(reason, "README.md isn't one of lane A's files."),
            v => panic!("{v:?}"),
        }
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("Write", "edit", json!({ "file_path": p(".chronicle/notes/x.md") }))), Verdict::Refuse { reason, .. } if reason == FORBIDDEN_REFUSAL));
        assert!(f.may_write(Path::new("/etc/hosts")).is_err());
        assert!(f.may_write(Path::new("src/lib/a.ts")).is_err(), "a relative path is never trusted");
        // an agent without Claude's tool names still says `kind: edit`
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("", "edit", json!({ "file_path": p("src/lib/b.ts") }))), Verdict::Allow { .. }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_write_through_a_symlink_is_judged_where_it_lands() {
        let r = repo("symlink");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(r.join("src/styles")).unwrap();
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("../index.css", r.join("src/lib/alias.css")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("src/index.css"), "").unwrap();
        let f = lane_fence(&r);
        let v = f.verdict(&ask("Write", "edit", json!({ "file_path": r.join("src/lib/alias.css").to_string_lossy() })));
        assert!(matches!(v, Verdict::Refuse { reason, .. } if reason.contains("belongs to lane C")), "the link lands in lane C's file");
    }

    #[test]
    fn shells_go_to_the_guard_and_the_rest_to_the_judge() {
        let r = repo("shell");
        let f = lane_fence(&r);
        let sh = |c: &str| f.verdict(&ask("Bash", "execute", json!({ "command": c })));
        assert!(matches!(sh("git diff"), Verdict::Allow { .. }));
        assert_eq!(sh("git commit -am x"), Verdict::Refuse { path: "git commit -am x".into(), reason: GIT_REFUSAL.into() });
        assert_eq!(sh("npm publish"), Verdict::Publish { command: "npm publish".into() });
        assert_eq!(sh("npx vitest run src/lib"), Verdict::Judge { command: "npx vitest run src/lib".into() });
    }

    #[test]
    fn chronicles_run_tools_and_reads_are_allowed_other_tools_are_not() {
        let r = repo("tools");
        let f = lane_fence(&r);
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("mcp__chronicle-run__chronicle_run_block", "other", json!({}))), Verdict::Allow { .. }));
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("mcp__chronicle__chronicle_notes_update", "other", json!({}))), Verdict::Refuse { .. }));
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("mcp__playwright__browser_navigate", "other", json!({}))), Verdict::Refuse { .. }));
        for (t, k) in [("Read", "read"), ("Grep", "search"), ("WebFetch", "fetch"), ("", "read")] {
            assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask(t, k, json!({}))), Verdict::Allow { .. }), "{t}/{k}");
        }
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&ask("ExitPlanMode", "switch_mode", json!({}))), Verdict::Refuse { .. }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_design_fence_is_the_same_rule_it_always_was() {
        let r = repo("design");
        let root = r.join(".chronicle/designs");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("home")).unwrap();
        let f = Fence::Design(root.clone());
        let w = |p: PathBuf| ask("Write", "edit", json!({ "file_path": p.to_string_lossy() }));
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&w(root.join("home/frames/a.html"))), Verdict::Allow { .. }));
        assert!(matches!(f.verdict(&w(r.join("src/escape.ts"))), Verdict::Refuse { .. }));
        assert_eq!(f.verdict(&ask("Bash", "execute", json!({ "command": "ls" }))), Verdict::NotMine, "the design lane's other asks keep their own handling");
    }
}
```
