- [ ] **Step 3: The fence**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/fence.rs`:

```rust
//! The write fence and the git guard (spec §4), generalised from the design
//! lane's `design_write_decision`. A permission ask is answered by rule:
//! writes inside the lane's owned paths are allowed; outside them refused with
//! the owner named; git reads allowed; git writes refused ("Chronicle commits
//! for you"); push, tag, publish and release refused everywhere and shown to the
//! user; Chronicle's own run tools and read tools allowed; every other shell
//! command goes to the judge. Shell redirects are not parsed — the gate's
//! turn-end snapshot reports what they changed (the shell hole).
use super::lane_map::{forbidden_path, Glob};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

pub(crate) const WRITE_TOOLS: [&str; 4] = ["Write", "Edit", "MultiEdit", "NotebookEdit"];
pub const GIT_REFUSAL: &str = "Chronicle commits for you";
pub const FORBIDDEN_REFUSAL: &str = "Lanes never change .chronicle/, fixes/ or chronicle.json.";
pub const PUBLISH_REFUSAL: &str = "Chronicle doesn’t publish for you.";
/// The MCP server name lanes and the planner reach Chronicle's run tools through.
pub const RUN_SERVER: &str = "chronicle-run";

/// The tool a permission ask is for: the call's `name`, or the adapter's `_meta` tool name.
pub(crate) fn tool_name(params: &Value) -> Option<&str> {
    let call = params.get("toolCall")?;
    call.get("name").and_then(|v| v.as_str())
        .or_else(|| call.pointer("/_meta/claudeCode/toolName").and_then(|v| v.as_str()))
        .or_else(|| call.pointer("/_chronicle/toolName").and_then(|v| v.as_str()))
}

/// The path a file-writing permission ask targets ("" when it names none).
pub(crate) fn write_target(call: &Value) -> &str {
    call.pointer("/rawInput/file_path").and_then(|v| v.as_str())
        .or_else(|| call.pointer("/rawInput/notebook_path").and_then(|v| v.as_str()))
        .or_else(|| call.pointer("/locations/0/path").and_then(|v| v.as_str()))
        .unwrap_or("")
}

pub enum Fence {
    /// The design lane: writes strictly inside `.chronicle/designs/<slug>/`.
    Design(PathBuf),
    /// A run's lane: writes inside `owns`; `others` name who owns the rest.
    Lane { repo: PathBuf, lane: String, owns: Vec<Glob>, others: Vec<(String, Vec<Glob>)> },
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Verdict {
    Allow { what: String },
    Refuse { path: String, reason: String },
    /// refused, and shown to the user as a needs-you row
    Publish { command: String },
    /// a shell command no rule decides: the judge does (spec §6, decision 1)
    Judge { command: String },
    /// not this fence's to answer (the design lane's other asks)
    NotMine,
}

impl Fence {
    pub fn may_write(&self, abs: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        match self {
            Fence::Design(root) => {
                if crate::acp::strictly_inside(abs, root) { Ok(()) } else { Err("The design session can only change files in .chronicle/designs/.".into()) }
            }
            Fence::Lane { repo, lane, owns, others } => {
                let rel = repo_rel(repo, abs).ok_or_else(|| format!("{} is outside the project.", abs.display()))?;
                if forbidden_path(&rel) { return Err(FORBIDDEN_REFUSAL.into()); }
                if owns.iter().any(|g| g.matches(&rel)) { return Ok(()); }
                match others.iter().find(|(_, gs)| gs.iter().any(|g| g.matches(&rel))) {
                    Some((id, _)) => Err(format!("{rel} belongs to lane {} — lane {} can't change it.", id.to_uppercase(), lane.to_uppercase())),
                    None => Err(format!("{rel} isn't one of lane {}'s files.", lane.to_uppercase())),
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn verdict(&self, params: &Value) -> Verdict {
        let name = tool_name(params).unwrap_or("");
        let call = params.get("toolCall").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        let kind = call.get("kind").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
        let is_write = WRITE_TOOLS.contains(&name) || matches!(kind, "edit" | "delete" | "move");
        if let Fence::Design(_) = self {
            if !WRITE_TOOLS.contains(&name) { return Verdict::NotMine; }
        }
        if is_write {
            let target = write_target(&call).to_string();
            return match self.may_write(Path::new(&target)) {
                Ok(()) => Verdict::Allow { what: target },
                Err(reason) => Verdict::Refuse { path: target, reason },
            };
        }
        if name.starts_with("mcp__") {
            if name.starts_with(&format!("mcp__{RUN_SERVER}__")) { return Verdict::Allow { what: name.into() }; }
            return Verdict::Refuse { path: name.into(), reason: "A lane uses its own files, git reads and Chronicle's run tools — not other tools.".into() };
        }
        if name == "Bash" || kind == "execute" {
            let cmd = call.pointer("/rawInput/command").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
            return match classify(&cmd) {
                Shell::ReadOnlyGit => Verdict::Allow { what: cmd },
                Shell::GitWrite => Verdict::Refuse { path: cmd, reason: GIT_REFUSAL.into() },
                Shell::Publish => Verdict::Publish { command: cmd },
                Shell::Other => Verdict::Judge { command: cmd },
            };
        }
        if matches!(name, "Read" | "Grep" | "Glob" | "LS" | "TodoWrite" | "Task" | "Agent" | "WebFetch" | "WebSearch")
            || matches!(kind, "read" | "search" | "think" | "fetch")
        {
            return Verdict::Allow { what: if name.is_empty() { kind.to_string() } else { name.to_string() } };
        }
        Verdict::Refuse { path: name.into(), reason: "Chronicle answers for lanes and has no rule for that tool, so it wasn't used.".into() }
    }
}

/// Canonical, repo-relative; `None` for anything relative, climbing, or outside.
fn repo_rel(repo: &Path, abs: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    if !abs.is_absolute() || abs.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir)) { return None; }
    let canon = crate::acp::canonical_deep(abs)?;
    let croot = repo.canonicalize().ok()?;
    let rel = canon.strip_prefix(&croot).ok()?;
    let s = rel.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    if s.is_empty() { None } else { Some(s) }
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Clone, Copy)]
pub enum Shell { ReadOnlyGit, Other, GitWrite, Publish }

/// The most severe class of any command in the line, substitutions included.
pub fn classify(cmd: &str) -> Shell {
    let segs = segments(cmd);
    if segs.is_empty() { return Shell::Other; }
    segs.iter().map(|s| classify_segment(s)).max().unwrap_or(Shell::Other)
}

/// Split on `;` `&&` `||` `|` `&` and newlines outside quotes; `$( … )` and
/// backtick bodies become commands of their own.
fn segments(cmd: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let cs: Vec<char> = cmd.chars().collect();
    let mut out: Vec<String> = vec![];
    let mut cur = String::new();
    let mut q: Option<char> = None;
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < cs.len() {
        let c = cs[i];
        if q == Some('\'') { if c == '\'' { q = None; } cur.push(c); i += 1; continue; }
        if c == '\\' && i + 1 < cs.len() { cur.push(c); cur.push(cs[i + 1]); i += 2; continue; }
        if c == '$' && cs.get(i + 1) == Some(&'(') {
            let (inner, end) = balanced(&cs, i + 2);
            out.extend(segments(&inner));
            cur.push_str("SUBST");
            i = end + 1;
            continue;
        }
        if c == '`' {
            let end = cs[i + 1..].iter().position(|&x| x == '`').map(|p| i + 1 + p).unwrap_or(cs.len());
            let inner: String = cs[i + 1..end.min(cs.len())].iter().collect();
            out.extend(segments(&inner));
            cur.push_str("SUBST");
            i = end + 1;
            continue;
        }
        if q == Some('"') { if c == '"' { q = None; } cur.push(c); i += 1; continue; }
        if c == '\'' || c == '"' { q = Some(c); cur.push(c); i += 1; continue; }
        let redirect = c == '&' && (i > 0 && cs[i - 1] == '>' || cs.get(i + 1) == Some(&'>'));
        if !redirect && matches!(c, ';' | '\n' | '|' | '&') {
            if !cur.trim().is_empty() { out.push(cur.trim().to_string()); }
            cur.clear();
            if (c == '&' || c == '|') && cs.get(i + 1) == Some(&c) { i += 1; }
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }
        cur.push(c);
        i += 1;
    }
    if !cur.trim().is_empty() { out.push(cur.trim().to_string()); }
    out
}

fn balanced(cs: &[char], start: usize) -> (String, usize) {
    let mut depth = 1;
    let mut i = start;
    while i < cs.len() {
        match cs[i] { '(' => depth += 1, ')' => { depth -= 1; if depth == 0 { break; } } _ => {} }
        i += 1;
    }
    (cs[start..i.min(cs.len())].iter().collect(), i)
}

/// Shell-like words: quotes group and are removed, a backslash escapes.
pub fn split_words(s: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out = vec![];
    let mut cur = String::new();
    let mut has = false;
    let mut q: Option<char> = None;
    let mut it = s.chars();
    while let Some(c) = it.next() {
        match q {
            Some(qc) if c == qc => q = None,
            Some('"') if c == '\\' => { if let Some(n) = it.next() { cur.push(n); } }
            Some(_) => cur.push(c),
            None => match c {
                '\'' | '"' => { q = Some(c); has = true; }
                '\\' => { if let Some(n) = it.next() { cur.push(n); has = true; } }
                c if c.is_whitespace() => { if has || !cur.is_empty() { out.push(std::mem::take(&mut cur)); has = false; } }
                c => cur.push(c),
            },
        }
    }
    if has || !cur.is_empty() { out.push(cur); }
    out
}

fn is_assignment(w: &str) -> bool {
    match w.split_once('=') { Some((k, _)) => !k.is_empty() && k.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_'), None => false }
}

fn classify_segment(seg: &str) -> Shell {
    let words = split_words(seg);
    let mut w: &[String] = &words;
    loop {
        match w.first().map(|s| s.as_str()) {
            Some(x) if is_assignment(x) => w = &w[1..],
            Some("sudo" | "env" | "command" | "exec" | "nohup" | "time" | "xargs" | "builtin") => {
                w = &w[1..];
                while w.first().map(|s| s.starts_with('-') || is_assignment(s)).unwrap_or(false) { w = &w[1..]; }
            }
            _ => break,
        }
    }
    let Some(first) = w.first() else { return Shell::Other };
    let prog = first.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(first).to_string();
    let rest: Vec<&str> = w[1..].iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();
    if matches!(prog.as_str(), "bash" | "sh" | "zsh" | "dash") {
        if let Some(i) = rest.iter().position(|a| matches!(*a, "-c" | "-lc" | "-ic")) {
            if let Some(script) = rest.get(i + 1) { return classify(script); }
        }
        let script = rest.iter().find(|a| !a.starts_with('-')).map(|s| s.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(s));
        return if script == Some("release.sh") { Shell::Publish } else { Shell::Other };
    }
    let first_verb = |xs: &[&str]| xs.iter().find(|a| !a.starts_with('-') && !a.starts_with('+')).copied();
    match prog.as_str() {
        "release.sh" => Shell::Publish,
        "git" => git_verb(&rest),
        "gh" if first_verb(&rest) == Some("release") => Shell::Publish,
        "npm" | "pnpm" if first_verb(&rest) == Some("publish") => Shell::Publish,
        "yarn" if rest.contains(&"publish") => Shell::Publish,
        "cargo" if first_verb(&rest) == Some("publish") => Shell::Publish,
        _ => Shell::Other,
    }
}

fn git_verb(args: &[&str]) -> Shell {
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < args.len() {
        let a = args[i];
        if matches!(a, "-C" | "-c" | "--git-dir" | "--work-tree" | "--namespace") { i += 2; continue; }
        if a.starts_with('-') { i += 1; continue; }
        break;
    }
    let Some(verb) = args.get(i) else { return Shell::ReadOnlyGit };
    let tail: Vec<&str> = args[i + 1..].iter().copied().filter(|a| !a.starts_with("2>") && *a != "2>&1").collect();
    match *verb {
        "push" | "tag" => Shell::Publish,
        "status" | "diff" | "log" | "show" | "blame" | "ls-files" | "grep" | "rev-parse" => Shell::ReadOnlyGit,
        "branch" if tail.is_empty() || tail == ["--show-current"] => Shell::ReadOnlyGit,
        _ => Shell::GitWrite,
    }
}
```

```rust
pub fn design_write_decision(params: &Value, designs_root: &Path) -> Option<WriteDecision> {
    let call = params.get("toolCall")?;
    let name = permission_tool_name(params)?;
    if !WRITE_TOOLS.contains(&name) { return None; }
    let path = write_target(call).to_string();
    let options = params.get("options").and_then(|v| v.as_array()).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
    let pick = |kind: &str| options.iter().find(|o| o["kind"] == kind).and_then(|o| o["optionId"].as_str()).map(String::from);
    let inside = !path.is_empty()
        && matches!(crate::runs::fence::Fence::Design(designs_root.to_path_buf()).verdict(params), crate::runs::fence::Verdict::Allow { .. });
    Some(match (inside, pick("allow_once"), pick("reject_once")) {
        (true, Some(option_id), _) => WriteDecision::Allow { option_id },
        (false, _, Some(option_id)) => WriteDecision::Refuse { option_id, path },
        _ => WriteDecision::Cancel { path },
    })
}
```

(If Plan C already replaced the `claudeCode.toolName` read in `permission_tool_name` with the normalised `_chronicle` name, `fence::tool_name` above reads both, so nothing is lost.)

```rust
    fn start_mock_with(name: &str, lane: Lane, extras: LaneExtras) -> Option<Harness> {
        let py = python3()?;
        let dir = tmp(name);
        let script = dir.join(".mock-agent.py");
        std::fs::write(&script, MOCK_AGENT).unwrap();
        let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(py);
        cmd.arg(&script).arg(&dir);
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<Value>();
        let emit: Emit = Arc::new(move |v| { let _ = tx.send(v); });
        let state = AcpState::new();
        let key = lane.key(&dir.to_string_lossy());
        assert!(start(&state, emit, key.clone(), lane, dir.clone(), dir.clone(), vec![dir.clone()], None, extras, None, cmd).unwrap());
        Some(Harness { state, rx, key, dir })
    }

    struct PathPolicy;
    impl LanePolicy for PathPolicy {
        fn answer(&self, params: &Value) -> PolicyAnswer {
            let path = params.pointer("/toolCall/rawInput/file_path").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let allow = path.ends_with("frames/a.html");
            PolicyAnswer { allow, reason: if allow { String::new() } else { "not this lane's".into() }, path,
                           decision: Some(json!({ "choice": "allow", "confidence": 0.93, "model": "jev-1.13.0", "band": "act" })) }
        }
        fn may_write(&self, _abs: &Path) -> Result<(), String> { Err("this lane owns no files".into()) }
    }

    #[test]
    fn worker_lanes_parse_key_and_store() {
        let w = Lane::parse(Some("w/10-2")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(w, Lane::Worker { run: 10, k: 2 });
        assert_eq!(w.key("/p"), "/p#w/10-2");
        assert_eq!(w.name(), "w/10-2");
        assert_eq!(w.tag(), "w-10-2");
        assert_eq!(Lane::Chat.name(), "chat");
        assert_eq!(Lane::Design.tag(), "design");
        let d = tmp("worker-store");
        assert_eq!(w.store(&d), d.join(".chronicle/runs/10/lanes/2"));
        assert!(Lane::parse(Some("w/x-1")).is_err());
        assert!(Lane::parse(Some("w/10")).is_err());
        assert_eq!(dir_of("/a#b/p#w/10-2"), "/a#b/p", "a # in the project path survives");
        assert_eq!(dir_of("/p#design"), "/p");

    #[test]
    fn policy_options_are_once_only() {
        let p = json!({ "options": [
            { "optionId": "always", "kind": "allow_always" }, { "optionId": "allow", "kind": "allow_once" },
            { "optionId": "reject", "kind": "reject_once" } ] });
        assert_eq!(policy_option(&p, true).as_deref(), Some("allow"));
        assert_eq!(policy_option(&p, false).as_deref(), Some("reject"));
        assert_eq!(policy_option(&json!({ "options": [{ "optionId": "always", "kind": "allow_always" }] }), true), None, "no once-only option: the ask is cancelled");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_worker_lane_answers_its_own_asks_by_policy_and_never_shows_one() {
        let extras = LaneExtras { policy: Some(PolicyHandle(Arc::new(PathPolicy))), ..Default::default() };
        let Some(h) = start_mock_with("worker-policy", Lane::Worker { run: 1, k: 0 }, extras) else { eprintln!("skipped: no python3"); return };
        wait_for(&h.rx, 20, |v| state_is(v, "ready"));
        let s = h.state.get(&h.key).unwrap();
        s.prompt(vec![json!({ "type": "text", "text": "DESIGN-FENCE" })], "DESIGN-FENCE".into()).unwrap();
        let mut guards: Vec<Value> = vec![];
        loop {
            let v = h.rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(20)).expect("events from the worker lane");
            assert!(!method_is(&v, "session/request_permission"), "a worker lane never shows an ask: {v}");
            assert_eq!(v["lane"], "w/1-0");
            assert_eq!(v["dir"], h.dir.to_string_lossy().as_ref());
            if method_is(&v, "_chronicle/guard") { guards.push(v["message"]["params"].clone()); }
            if method_is(&v, "_chronicle/turn_end") { break; }
        }
        while guards.len() < 3 { guards.push(wait_for(&h.rx, 5, |v| method_is(v, "_chronicle/guard"))["message"]["params"].clone()); }
        let answers: Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(h.dir.join(".fence-answers.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(answers, json!({ "300": "allow", "301": "reject", "302": "reject" }));
        let allowed = guards.iter().find(|g| g["toolCallId"] == "w300").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(allowed["allowed"], true);
        assert_eq!(allowed["decision"]["confidence"], 0.93);
        assert_eq!(guards.iter().filter(|g| g["allowed"] == false).count(), 2);
        assert!(h.dir.join(".chronicle/runs/1/lanes/0").is_dir(), "the lane's transcript lives under the run");
        s.stop(true);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&h.dir);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_worker_lanes_direct_writes_go_through_its_fence() {
        let extras = LaneExtras { policy: Some(PolicyHandle(Arc::new(PathPolicy))), ..Default::default() };
        let Some(h) = start_mock_with("worker-write", Lane::Worker { run: 1, k: 1 }, extras) else { eprintln!("skipped: no python3"); return };
        wait_for(&h.rx, 20, |v| state_is(v, "ready"));
        let s = h.state.get(&h.key).unwrap();
        s.prompt(vec![json!({ "type": "text", "text": "do the round" })], "do the round".into()).unwrap();
        wait_for(&h.rx, 20, |v| method_is(v, "_chronicle/turn_end"));
        assert!(!h.dir.join("agent-made.txt").exists(), "fs/write_text_file obeys the lane's fence");
        s.stop(true);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&h.dir);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_screen_answers_an_ask_before_the_user_sees_it() {
        struct RefuseCommands;
        impl AskScreen for RefuseCommands {
            fn screen(&self, params: &Value) -> Option<PolicyAnswer> {
                (params.pointer("/toolCall/title").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) == Some("Run a command"))
                    .then(|| PolicyAnswer { allow: false, path: "Run a command".into(), reason: "Chronicle doesn’t publish for you.".into(), decision: None })
            }
        }
        let extras = LaneExtras { screen: Some(ScreenHandle(Arc::new(RefuseCommands))), ..Default::default() };
        let Some(h) = start_mock_with("screen", Lane::Chat, extras) else { eprintln!("skipped: no python3"); return };
        wait_for(&h.rx, 20, |v| state_is(v, "ready"));
        let s = h.state.get(&h.key).unwrap();
        s.prompt(vec![json!({ "type": "text", "text": "do the round" })], "do the round".into()).unwrap();
        let mut saw_guard = false;
        loop {
            let v = h.rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(20)).expect("events");
            assert!(!method_is(&v, "session/request_permission"), "the screened ask never reached the pane");
            if method_is(&v, "_chronicle/guard") { saw_guard = v["message"]["params"]["allowed"] == false; }
            if method_is(&v, "_chronicle/turn_end") { break; }
        }
        assert!(saw_guard);
        assert!(h.dir.join("agent-made.txt").exists(), "a screen is not a fence: the chat lane's writes are untouched");
        s.stop(true);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&h.dir);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The lane, its key and its store**

Replace `enum Lane` and its `impl` in `src-tauri/src/acp.rs` with (keep Plan C's `Reviewer` variant and arms where Plan C added them):

```rust
/// Which of a project's agent sessions a key belongs to. The chat lane keeps
/// the historic key (the canonical project path) and `.chronicle/agent/`; the
/// design lane gets `<path>#design`; a run's lane `k` of run `run` gets
/// `<path>#w/<run>-<k>` and keeps its records under the run's folder.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Lane { Chat, Design, Worker { run: u64, k: u16 } }

impl Lane {
    pub fn parse(s: Option<&str>) -> Result<Lane, String> {
        match s {
            None | Some("chat") => Ok(Lane::Chat),
            Some("design") => Ok(Lane::Design),
            Some(w) if w.starts_with("w/") => w[2..].split_once('-')
                .and_then(|(r, k)| Some(Lane::Worker { run: r.parse().ok()?, k: k.parse().ok()? }))
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("there's no agent lane called {w}")),
            Some(other) => Err(format!("there's no agent lane called {other}")),
        }
    }
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str { match self { Lane::Chat => "chat", Lane::Design => "design", Lane::Worker { .. } => "worker" } }
    /// What the `lane` field of every acp-update says — the frontend keys by it.
    pub fn name(self) -> String {
        match self { Lane::Worker { run, k } => format!("w/{run}-{k}"), other => other.as_str().to_string() }
    }
    /// File-name safe: temp index files and logs are named with it.
    pub fn tag(self) -> String { self.name().replace('/', "-") }
    pub fn key(self, canon: &str) -> String {
        match self {
            Lane::Chat => canon.to_string(),
            Lane::Design => format!("{canon}#design"),
            Lane::Worker { run, k } => format!("{canon}#w/{run}-{k}"),
        }
    }
    /// Where this lane's transcripts, ledger and `current` pointer live.
    pub fn store(self, project: &Path) -> PathBuf {
        match self {
            Lane::Chat => ledger::agent_dir(project),
            Lane::Design => crate::designs::designs_root(project).join(".threads"),
            Lane::Worker { run, k } => project.join(format!(".chronicle/runs/{run}/lanes/{k}")),
        }
    }
}

/// The project dir a session key belongs to. Suffix-aware, so a `#` in a real
/// folder name is never mistaken for a lane.
pub fn dir_of(key: &str) -> &str {
    if let Some(d) = key.strip_suffix("#design") { return d; }
    if let Some(i) = key.rfind("#w/") { return &key[..i]; }
    key
}
```

```rust
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The policy and screen hooks**

Beside `LaneExtras` in `acp.rs`:

```rust
/// What Chronicle answers to one permission ask on a lane it answers for.
pub struct PolicyAnswer { pub allow: bool, pub path: String, pub reason: String, pub decision: Option<Value> }

/// A run's lane (Runs §4): every ask is answered by Chronicle, never shown.
/// `answer` runs off the reader thread and may take as long as a judge call.
pub trait LanePolicy: Send + Sync {
    fn answer(&self, params: &Value) -> PolicyAnswer;
    /// The fence for a direct `fs/write_text_file` from this lane.
    fn may_write(&self, abs: &Path) -> Result<(), String>;
}

/// A narrower hook for a lane the user still answers (the chat lane during a
/// run): `Some` answers an ask at once, before the pane shows it.
pub trait AskScreen: Send + Sync {
    fn screen(&self, params: &Value) -> Option<PolicyAnswer>;
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct PolicyHandle(pub Arc<dyn LanePolicy>);
impl std::fmt::Debug for PolicyHandle { fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result { f.write_str("PolicyHandle") } }
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct ScreenHandle(pub Arc<dyn AskScreen>);
impl std::fmt::Debug for ScreenHandle { fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result { f.write_str("ScreenHandle") } }

/// The once-only option for an answer. Never an "always": those write durable
/// rules outside the run. No once-only option → `None`, and the ask is cancelled.
pub fn policy_option(params: &Value, allow: bool) -> Option<String> {
    let kind = if allow { "allow_once" } else { "reject_once" };
    params.get("options").and_then(|v| v.as_array())?.iter()
        .find(|o| o["kind"] == kind).and_then(|o| o["optionId"].as_str()).map(String::from)
}
```

and give `LaneExtras` the two fields (it keeps `#[derive(Clone, Default, Debug)]`):

```rust
    /// `Some` for a run's lane: Chronicle answers its asks and fences its writes.
    pub policy: Option<PolicyHandle>,
    /// `Some` for the chat lane: screens asks before the user sees them.
    pub screen: Option<ScreenHandle>,
```

Add a helper to `impl AcpSession`, beside `emit_state`:

```rust
    /// Record and show what Chronicle decided about one ask.
    fn emit_guard(&self, params: &Value, a: &PolicyAnswer) {
        let mut p = json!({ "toolCallId": params.pointer("/toolCall/toolCallId").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
                            "allowed": a.allow, "path": a.path, "reason": a.reason });
        if let Some(d) = &a.decision { p["decision"] = d.clone(); }
        let guard = json!({ "method": "_chronicle/guard", "params": p });
        self.append_transcript(&guard);
        self.emit_msg(guard);
    }
```

```rust
                // a run's lane: Chronicle answers by policy, off the reader thread
                // (a judge call can take a while); the user never sees the ask
                if let Some(PolicyHandle(policy)) = self.extras.policy.clone() {
                    // registered first, so a turn end or Stop resolves it
                    if let Ok(mut g) = self.perms.lock() { g.insert(id.to_string(), id.clone()); }
                    let me = self.clone();
                    let rid = id.to_string();
                    std::thread::spawn(move || {
                        let a = policy.answer(&params);

                        let _ = me.respond_permission(&rid, policy_option(&params, a.allow));
                        me.emit_guard(&params, &a);
                    });

                            None => json!({ "outcome": "cancelled" }),
                        };
                        self.respond_ok(&id, json!({ "outcome": outcome }));
                        self.emit_guard(&params, &a);
                        return;
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

At the bottom of a new `src-tauri/src/runs/gate.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::record::LaneSpec;

    fn tmpdir(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-gate-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&d).unwrap();
        d.canonicalize().unwrap()
    }
    /// A throwaway repository with one commit. Every git write in these tests lands here.
    fn repo(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let d = tmpdir(name);
        for a in [&["init", "-q"][..], &["config", "user.email", "t@t"], &["config", "user.name", "t"], &["config", "commit.gpgsign", "false"]] {
            git(&d, a).unwrap();
        }
        std::fs::write(d.join("README.md"), "toy\n").unwrap();
        git(&d, &["add", "README.md"]).unwrap();
        git(&d, &["commit", "-qm", "init"]).unwrap();
        d
    }
    fn spec(id: &str, owns: &[&str]) -> LaneSpec {
        LaneSpec { id: id.into(), title: id.into(), items: vec![], owns: owns.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(), depends_on: vec![], checks: vec![] }
    }

    #[test]
    fn checks_report_exit_codes_and_tails() {
        let d = tmpdir("exit");
        let r = run_check(&d, &Check { name: "t".into(), command: "echo hi; echo oops 1>&2; exit 3".into() }, &[], Duration::from_secs(10));
        assert_eq!(r.exit, Some(3));
        assert!(!r.timed_out);
        assert!(r.tail.contains("hi") && r.tail.contains("oops"), "{}", r.tail);
        assert_eq!(run_check(&d, &Check { name: "ok".into(), command: "true".into() }, &[], Duration::from_secs(10)).exit, Some(0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_check_that_never_exits_is_killed_with_its_children() {
        let d = tmpdir("hang");
        let c = Check { name: "hang".into(), command: "echo started; sleep 30 & echo $! > child.pid; wait".into() };
        let t = Instant::now();
        let r = run_check(&d, &c, &[], Duration::from_secs(1));
        assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(5), "killed at the timeout, not after the sleep");
        assert!(r.timed_out);
        assert_eq!(r.exit, None, "counted as failed");
        assert!(r.tail.contains("started"), "the partial output is kept: {}", r.tail);
        assert!(r.tail.contains("[stopped after 1 second]"), "{}", r.tail);
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300));
        let pid: i32 = std::fs::read_to_string(d.join("child.pid")).unwrap().trim().parse().unwrap();
        assert_ne!(unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) }, 0, "the background child died with its group");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_scratch_copy_checks_a_commit_outside_the_repo() {
        let r = repo("scratch");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(r.join("node_modules/x")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join(".gitignore"), "node_modules\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("a.txt"), "one").unwrap();
        git(&r, &["add", "a.txt", ".gitignore"]).unwrap();
        git(&r, &["commit", "-qm", "a"]).unwrap();
        let c1 = git(&r, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("a.txt"), "two").unwrap(); // the user's own unsaved edit
        let s = tmpdir("scratch-copy").join("copy");
        let checks = vec![Check { name: "test".into(), command: "test \"$(cat a.txt)\" = one && test -d node_modules/x".into() }];
        let out = run_checks_at(&r, &s, &c1, &checks);
        assert_eq!(out.results[0].exit, Some(0), "{}", out.results[0].tail);
        assert!(!out.main_tree && out.notice.is_none());
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["status", "--porcelain"]).unwrap(), "M a.txt", "the user's tree is untouched");
        git(&r, &["commit", "-qam", "b"]).unwrap();
        let c2 = git(&r, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(run_checks_at(&r, &s, &c2, &checks).results[0].exit, Some(1), "the copy moves to the next commit");
        let bad = run_checks_at(&r, Path::new("/dev/null/cannot"), &c2, &checks);
        assert!(bad.main_tree, "no scratch copy: the checks run in the project (spec §11)");
        assert!(bad.notice.unwrap().contains("ran in your project while the lanes waited"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_save_commits_only_its_paths() {
        let r = repo("save");
        std::fs::write(r.join("old.txt"), "x").unwrap();
        git(&r, &["add", "old.txt"]).unwrap();
        git(&r, &["commit", "-qm", "old"]).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(r.join("a")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("a/new.txt"), "lane").unwrap();
        std::fs::remove_file(r.join("old.txt")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("other.txt"), "not the lane's").unwrap();
        let Saved::Commit(c) = save_paths(&r, &["a/new.txt".into(), "old.txt".into(), "a/ghost.txt".into()], "lane a").unwrap() else { panic!("expected a commit") };
        let mut files: Vec<String> = git(&r, &["show", "--name-only", "--format=", &c]).unwrap().lines().map(String::from).collect();
        files.sort();
        assert_eq!(files, vec!["a/new.txt", "old.txt"], "a deletion is saved; a path that never existed is skipped");
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["status", "--porcelain"]).unwrap(), "?? other.txt", "everything else stays as it was");
        assert_eq!(save_paths(&r, &["a/new.txt".into()], "again").unwrap(), Saved::AlreadySaved(c.clone()));
        assert_eq!(save_paths(&r, &[], "none").unwrap(), Saved::Nothing);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_save_waits_out_the_users_index_lock_and_leaves_their_staged_files_alone() {
        let r = repo("race");
        std::fs::write(r.join("mine.txt"), "the user's work").unwrap();
        git(&r, &["add", "mine.txt"]).unwrap(); // staged by the user, not yet committed
        std::fs::write(r.join("lane.txt"), "lane a's work").unwrap();
        let lock = r.join(".git/index.lock");
        std::fs::write(&lock, "").unwrap(); // the user's own git is mid-commit
        let l2 = lock.clone();
        let t = std::thread::spawn(move || { std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(800)); std::fs::remove_file(l2).unwrap(); });
        let saved = save_paths(&r, &["lane.txt".into()], "lane a").unwrap();
        t.join().unwrap();
        let Saved::Commit(c) = saved else { panic!("expected a commit once the lock lifted") };
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["show", "--name-only", "--format=", &c]).unwrap(), "lane.txt");
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["diff", "--cached", "--name-only"]).unwrap(), "mine.txt", "the user's staged file is still staged, and still theirs");
    }

    #[test]
    fn changes_split_into_owned_files_and_leaks() {
        let map = LaneMap { items: vec![], lanes: vec![spec("a", &["src/a/**"]), spec("b", &["src/b/**"]), spec("c", &["src/c/**"])] };
        let changed: Vec<String> = ["src/a/x.ts", "src/b/own.ts", "src/b/sneaky.ts", "src/c/y.ts", "README.md", ".chronicle/journal.jsonl"].iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
        let writes = HashMap::from([("b".to_string(), HashSet::from(["src/b/own.ts".to_string()]))]);
        let (owned, leaks) = split_changes(&changed, "a", &map, &["b".to_string()], &writes);
        assert_eq!(owned, vec!["src/a/x.ts"]);
        assert_eq!(leaks, vec![
            Leak { path: "src/b/sneaky.ts".into(), owner: Some("b".into()), by: None },
            Leak { path: "src/c/y.ts".into(), owner: Some("c".into()), by: None },
            Leak { path: "README.md".into(), owner: None, by: None },
        ], "b's own tool write while b was working is b's; .chronicle/ is the app's");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_close_commit_is_empty_and_leaves_the_index_alone() {
        let r = repo("close");
        std::fs::write(r.join("mine.txt"), "staged by the user").unwrap();
        git(&r, &["add", "mine.txt"]).unwrap();
        let c = close_commit(&r, 10).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]).unwrap(), c);
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["show", "--name-only", "--format=", &c]).unwrap(), "", "no files in the close commit");
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["log", "-1", "--format=%(trailers:key=Chronicle-Phase,valueonly)"]).unwrap(), "FX-10 done");
        assert_eq!(git(&r, &["diff", "--cached", "--name-only"]).unwrap(), "mine.txt");
    }

    #[test]
    fn discard_puts_back_only_the_lanes_tracked_files() {
        let r = repo("discard");
        std::fs::write(r.join("README.md"), "changed by the lane").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("keep.txt"), "the user's").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(discard_paths(&r, &["README.md".into(), "never-tracked.txt".into()]).unwrap(), 1);
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(r.join("README.md")).unwrap(), "toy\n");
        assert!(r.join("keep.txt").exists());
    }

    #[test]
    fn dirty_paths_reads_porcelain_without_losing_the_first_letter() {
        let r = repo("dirty");
        std::fs::write(r.join("README.md"), "edited").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(r.join("new.txt"), "").unwrap();
        let mut d = dirty_paths(&r);
        d.sort();
        assert_eq!(d, vec!["README.md", "new.txt"]);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The gate**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/gate.rs`:

```rust
//! The gate (spec §3, §4, §11). After a lane's turn: what it changed is split
//! into its own files and leaks; its own files are saved by explicit path
//! under one git mutex; the save is checked in a scratch worktree outside the
//! repo (CARGO_TARGET_DIR on the main tree's target, node_modules linked);
//! exit codes are law; a check is killed with its whole process group at ten
//! minutes. Leaks are reported, never reverted. Only Rust writes git, here.
use super::checks::{cargo_dir, Check};
use super::lane_map::compile;
use super::record::{CheckResult, LaneMap, Leak};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Every git write a run makes goes through this one lock (spec §4, "Index").
pub static GIT: Mutex<()> = Mutex::new(());
pub const CHECK_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(600);
pub const BUSY: &str = "Git is busy";
const TAIL_BYTES: usize = 6000;
const DIFF_CAP: usize = 60_000;
static NEXT_LOG: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

fn lock() -> std::sync::MutexGuard<'static, ()> { GIT.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()) }

pub fn git_raw(repo: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> Result<String, String> {
    let out = Command::new("git").arg("-C").arg(repo).args(args).stdin(Stdio::null()).output()
        .map_err(|e| format!("couldn't run git: {e}"))?;
    if out.status.success() { Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned()) }
    else { Err(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).trim().to_string()) }
}
pub fn git(repo: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> Result<String, String> { git_raw(repo, args).map(|s| s.trim().to_string()) }
fn git_ok(repo: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> bool {
    Command::new("git").arg("-C").arg(repo).args(args).stdin(Stdio::null()).stdout(Stdio::null()).stderr(Stdio::null())
        .status().map(|s| s.success()).unwrap_or(false)
}

/// `~/Library/Application Support/Chronicle/scratch/<repo-hash>/` (spec §15.8).
pub fn scratch_dir(repo: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    let mut h = Sha256::new();
    h.update(repo.to_string_lossy().as_bytes());
    crate::config_dir().join("scratch").join(&format!("{:x}", h.finalize())[..16])
}

/// The scratch worktree, detached at `commit`. Made once, moved after.
pub fn prepare_scratch(repo: &Path, scratch: &Path, commit: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let _g = lock();
    if scratch.join(".git").exists() {
        git(scratch, &["checkout", "--detach", "--force", "-q", commit])?;
        // Chronicle's own copy, never the user's tree: the last check's leftovers go
        git(scratch, &["clean", "-fdq", "-e", "node_modules"])?;
    } else {
        let _ = git(repo, &["worktree", "prune"]);
        if let Some(parent) = scratch.parent() { std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?; }
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(scratch);
        git(repo, &["worktree", "add", "--detach", "--force", "-q", &scratch.to_string_lossy(), commit])?;
    }
    let nm = repo.join("node_modules");
    let link = scratch.join("node_modules");
    if nm.is_dir() && std::fs::symlink_metadata(&link).is_err() {
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&nm, &link).map_err(|e| format!("couldn't link node_modules: {e}"))?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub fn check_env(repo: &Path) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
    let mut env = vec![("PATH".to_string(), crate::setup::tool_env_path()), ("CI".to_string(), "1".to_string())];
    if let Some(d) = cargo_dir(repo) { env.push(("CARGO_TARGET_DIR".into(), d.join("target").to_string_lossy().into_owned())); }
    env
}

fn human(d: Duration) -> String {
    match d.as_secs() { 1 => "1 second".into(), s if s >= 60 => format!("{} minutes", s / 60), s => format!("{s} seconds") }
}

fn tail_of(p: &Path) -> String {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(p).unwrap_or_default();
    let start = bytes.len().saturating_sub(TAIL_BYTES);
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[start..]).trim_end().to_string()
}

/// One check, in its own process group, output to a file (a child that keeps a
/// pipe open can never wedge us), killed with the whole group at `timeout`.
pub fn run_check(cwd: &Path, check: &Check, env: &[(String, String)], timeout: Duration) -> CheckResult {
    use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
    let started = Instant::now();
    let log = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-check-{}-{}.log", std::process::id(), NEXT_LOG.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst)));
    let fail = |tail: String| CheckResult { name: check.name.clone(), exit: None, ms: 0, timed_out: false, tail };
    let out = match std::fs::File::create(&log) { Ok(f) => f, Err(e) => return fail(format!("couldn't open a log for the check: {e}")) };
    let out2 = match out.try_clone() { Ok(f) => f, Err(e) => return fail(e.to_string()) };
    let mut cmd = Command::new("/bin/sh");
    cmd.arg("-c").arg(&check.command).current_dir(cwd).stdin(Stdio::null()).stdout(out).stderr(out2).process_group(0);
    for (k, v) in env { cmd.env(k, v); }
    let mut child = match cmd.spawn() { Ok(c) => c, Err(e) => return fail(format!("couldn't start the check: {e}")) };
    let pgid = child.id() as i32;
    let (exit, timed_out) = loop {
        match child.try_wait() {
            Ok(Some(st)) => break (st.code(), false),
            Ok(None) if started.elapsed() >= timeout => {
                unsafe { libc::killpg(pgid, libc::SIGKILL); }
                let _ = child.wait();
                break (None, true);
            }
            Ok(None) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)),
            Err(_) => break (None, false),
        }
    };
    // anything the check left running dies with its group
    unsafe { libc::killpg(pgid, libc::SIGKILL); }
    let mut tail = tail_of(&log);
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&log);
    if timed_out { tail.push_str(&format!("\n[stopped after {}]", human(timeout))); }
    CheckResult { name: check.name.clone(), exit, ms: started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64, timed_out, tail }
}

pub struct Checked { pub results: Vec<CheckResult>, pub notice: Option<String>, pub main_tree: bool }

/// Run `checks` against `commit` in the scratch copy; without one, in the
/// project itself with a notice, and the caller pauses lanes while they run.
pub fn run_checks_at(repo: &Path, scratch: &Path, commit: &str, checks: &[Check]) -> Checked {
    if checks.is_empty() { return Checked { results: vec![], notice: None, main_tree: false }; }
    let env = check_env(repo);
    match prepare_scratch(repo, scratch, commit) {
        Ok(()) => Checked { results: checks.iter().map(|c| run_check(scratch, c, &env, CHECK_TIMEOUT)).collect(), notice: None, main_tree: false },
        Err(e) => Checked {
            results: checks.iter().map(|c| run_check(repo, c, &env, CHECK_TIMEOUT)).collect(),
            notice: Some(format!("Chronicle couldn't make its scratch copy ({e}), so the checks ran in your project while the lanes waited.")),
            main_tree: true,
        },
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Saved { Commit(String), AlreadySaved(String), Nothing }

fn with_index_retry<T>(mut f: impl FnMut() -> Result<T, String>) -> Result<T, String> {
    for _ in 0..40 {
        match f() {
            Err(e) if e.contains("index.lock") => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(250)),
            r => return r,
        }
    }
    Err(format!("{BUSY}: another git command holds the index. Chronicle will save this lane on its next try."))
}

/// Save exactly `paths` (stage by explicit path, `commit --only` so nothing
/// else the index holds — the user's own staged files — rides along).
pub fn save_paths(repo: &Path, paths: &[String], message: &str) -> Result<Saved, String> {
    if paths.is_empty() { return Ok(Saved::Nothing); }
    let _g = lock();
    let mut ls = vec!["ls-files", "-z", "--"];
    ls.extend(paths.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()));
    let tracked: HashSet<String> = git_raw(repo, &ls).unwrap_or_default().split('\0').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).map(String::from).collect();
    let live: Vec<&str> = paths.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).filter(|p| repo.join(p).exists() || tracked.contains(*p)).collect();
    if live.is_empty() { return Ok(Saved::Nothing); }
    let mut add = vec!["add", "--"];
    add.extend(live.iter().copied());
    with_index_retry(|| git(repo, &add))?;
    let mut diff = vec!["diff", "--cached", "--quiet", "HEAD", "--"];
    diff.extend(live.iter().copied());
    if git_ok(repo, &diff) { return Ok(Saved::AlreadySaved(git(repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"])?)); }
    let mut commit = vec!["commit", "--only", "-q", "-m", message, "--"];
    commit.extend(live.iter().copied());
    with_index_retry(|| git(repo, &commit))?;
    Ok(Saved::Commit(git(repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"])?))
}

pub fn changed_between(repo: &Path, from_tree: &str, to_tree: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    git(repo, &["diff-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "--no-renames", from_tree, to_tree]).unwrap_or_default()
        .lines().filter(|l| !l.is_empty()).map(String::from).collect()
}

/// After a restart there is no turn snapshot: everything that differs from HEAD.
pub fn dirty_paths(repo: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    git_raw(repo, &["status", "--porcelain=v1", "--untracked-files=all", "--no-renames"]).unwrap_or_default()
        .lines().filter_map(|l| l.get(3..)).map(|p| p.trim_matches('"').to_string()).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()).collect()
}

/// The lane's own files, and the rest as leaks (spec §4, the shell hole). A
/// path another lane owns is that lane's when that lane was working too AND
/// wrote it through a tool Chronicle allowed; otherwise it is a leak. The
/// app's own `.chronicle/` writes are never anyone's.
pub fn split_changes(changed: &[String], lane: &str, map: &LaneMap, active: &[String], tool_writes: &HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<Leak>) {
    let globs: Vec<(String, Vec<super::lane_map::Glob>)> = map.lanes.iter().map(|l| (l.id.clone(), compile(&l.owns))).collect();
    let mut owned = vec![];
    let mut leaks = vec![];
    for p in changed {
        if p.starts_with(".chronicle/") { continue; }
        match globs.iter().find(|(_, gs)| gs.iter().any(|g| g.matches(p))).map(|(id, _)| id.clone()) {
            Some(id) if id == lane => owned.push(p.clone()),
            Some(id) => {
                let theirs = active.contains(&id) && tool_writes.get(&id).map(|s| s.contains(p)).unwrap_or(false);
                if !theirs { leaks.push(Leak { path: p.clone(), owner: Some(id), by: None }); }
            }
            None => leaks.push(Leak { path: p.clone(), owner: None, by: None }),
        }
    }
    (owned, leaks)
}

pub fn diff_for(repo: &Path, commit: &str) -> String {
    let d = git_raw(repo, &["show", "--format=", "--patch", "--no-color", commit]).unwrap_or_default();
    if d.len() <= DIFF_CAP { return d; }
    let mut end = DIFF_CAP;
    while !d.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
    format!("{}\n[the diff is longer; the rest was cut]", &d[..end])
}

pub fn files_in(repo: &Path, commit: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    git(repo, &["show", "--format=", "--name-only", commit]).unwrap_or_default().lines().filter(|l| !l.is_empty()).map(String::from).collect()
}

/// The user's Discard on a stopped lane (spec §3, Stop): its tracked files go
/// back to HEAD. Nothing untracked is deleted. Returns how many were put back.
pub fn discard_paths(repo: &Path, paths: &[String]) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let _g = lock();
    let mut ls = vec!["ls-files", "-z", "--"];
    ls.extend(paths.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()));
    let tracked: Vec<String> = git_raw(repo, &ls)?.split('\0').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).map(String::from).collect();
    if tracked.is_empty() { return Ok(0); }
    let mut co = vec!["checkout", "--"];
    co.extend(tracked.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()));
    with_index_retry(|| git(repo, &co))?;
    Ok(tracked.len())
}

/// The round's closing trailer as an empty commit made with plumbing, so the
/// index — and anything the user has staged — is never part of it.
pub fn close_commit(repo: &Path, round: u64) -> Result<String, String> {
    let _g = lock();
    let head = git(repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"])?;
    let tree = git(repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD^{tree}"])?;
    let id = format!("FX-{round}");
    let commit = git(repo, &["commit-tree", &tree, "-p", &head, "-m", &format!("Close {id}"), "-m", &format!("Chronicle-Phase: {id} done")])?;
    git(repo, &["update-ref", "-m", &format!("chronicle: close {id}"), "HEAD", &commit, &head])?;
    Ok(commit)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The seam, and the fake the tests use**

Create `src-tauri/src/runs/oracle.rs`:

```rust
//! The one seam between runs and the provider layer (agents spec §9–§10). The
//! judge and the reviewer see only `Oracle`; `ProviderOracle` puts Plan C's
//! `Calls` behind it; `Fallback` falls to the user's agent for the rest of the
//! run the first time a provider fails (spec §11), with one notice.
use crate::providers::{Decision, DecisionRequest};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

pub trait Oracle: Send + Sync {
    fn decide(&self, req: DecisionRequest) -> Result<Decision, String>;
    /// The schema-checked JSON answer, and the model that gave it.
    fn complete_json(&self, system: &str, prompt: &str, schema: &Value) -> Result<(Value, String), String>;
}

pub struct ProviderOracle { calls: Box<dyn crate::providers::Calls + Send + Sync>, label: String }

impl ProviderOracle {
    pub fn new(calls: Box<dyn crate::providers::Calls + Send + Sync>, label: &str) -> Self { ProviderOracle { calls, label: label.into() } }
}

impl Oracle for ProviderOracle {
    fn decide(&self, req: DecisionRequest) -> Result<Decision, String> { self.calls.decide(req) }
    fn complete_json(&self, system: &str, prompt: &str, schema: &Value) -> Result<(Value, String), String> {
        let v = self.calls.complete_json(crate::providers::CompleteRequest {
            system: system.to_string(), prompt: prompt.to_string(), schema: schema.clone(), model: None,
        })?;
        Ok((v, self.label.clone()))
    }
}

pub const DECISIONS_FALLBACK: &str = "The decision model couldn’t be reached, so your agent answers the run’s small questions from here on.";
pub const REVIEW_FALLBACK: &str = "The review model couldn’t be reached, so your agent reviews the lanes from here on.";

pub struct Fallback {
    primary: Arc<dyn Oracle>,
    fallback: Arc<dyn Oracle>,
    degraded: AtomicBool,
    notice: Mutex<Option<String>>,
    what: &'static str,
}

impl Fallback {
    pub fn new(primary: Arc<dyn Oracle>, fallback: Arc<dyn Oracle>, what: &'static str) -> Self {
        Fallback { primary, fallback, degraded: AtomicBool::new(false), notice: Mutex::new(None), what }
    }
    fn degrade(&self) {
        if !self.degraded.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst) {
            if let Ok(mut g) = self.notice.lock() { *g = Some(self.what.to_string()); }
        }
    }
    pub fn decide(&self, mk: &dyn Fn() -> DecisionRequest) -> Result<Decision, String> {
        if !self.degraded.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            match self.primary.decide(mk()) { Ok(d) => return Ok(d), Err(_) => self.degrade() }
        }
        self.fallback.decide(mk())
    }
    pub fn complete_json(&self, system: &str, prompt: &str, schema: &Value) -> Result<(Value, String), String> {
        if !self.degraded.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            match self.primary.complete_json(system, prompt, schema) { Ok(v) => return Ok(v), Err(_) => self.degrade() }
        }
        self.fallback.complete_json(system, prompt, schema)
    }
    /// The one notice for the run card, once.
    pub fn take_notice(&self) -> Option<String> { self.notice.lock().ok()?.take() }
}

/// The project's Decisions and Review roles (agents spec §9), each falling back
/// to the user's agent — which is also what both roles are when no key is set.
pub fn for_project(project: &Path) -> (Fallback, Fallback) {
    use crate::providers::{agent_calls, for_role, Role};
    let agent = || -> Arc<dyn Oracle> { Arc::new(ProviderOracle::new(agent_calls(project), "agent")) };
    let decisions = Fallback::new(Arc::new(ProviderOracle::new(for_role(Role::Decisions, project), "decisions")), agent(), DECISIONS_FALLBACK);
    let review = Fallback::new(Arc::new(ProviderOracle::new(for_role(Role::Review, project), "review")), agent(), REVIEW_FALLBACK);
    (decisions, review)
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) mod fake {
    use super::*;
    use crate::providers::{Answer, Decision};
    use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};
    use std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize;

    /// Answers queued by the test, in order; an empty queue is an unreachable provider.
    #[derive(Default)]
    pub(crate) struct FakeOracle {
        pub decides: Mutex<VecDeque<Result<Decision, String>>>,
        pub completes: Mutex<VecDeque<Result<Value, String>>>,
        pub decide_calls: AtomicUsize,
        pub complete_calls: AtomicUsize,
        pub model: String,
    }
    impl FakeOracle {
        pub fn named(model: &str) -> Arc<Self> { Arc::new(FakeOracle { model: model.into(), ..Default::default() }) }
        pub fn decide_with(&self, d: Decision) { self.decides.lock().unwrap().push_back(Ok(d)); }
        pub fn complete_with(&self, v: Value) { self.completes.lock().unwrap().push_back(Ok(v)); }
        pub fn fail_next_decide(&self) { self.decides.lock().unwrap().push_back(Err("503 Service Unavailable".into())); }
    }
    impl Oracle for FakeOracle {
        fn decide(&self, _req: DecisionRequest) -> Result<Decision, String> {
            self.decide_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
            self.decides.lock().unwrap().pop_front().unwrap_or_else(|| Err("unreachable".into()))
        }
        fn complete_json(&self, _s: &str, _p: &str, _schema: &Value) -> Result<(Value, String), String> {
            self.complete_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
            self.completes.lock().unwrap().pop_front().unwrap_or_else(|| Err("unreachable".into())).map(|v| (v, self.model.clone()))
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn choice(name: &str, pick: &str, probs: &[(&str, f32)], confidence: f32) -> (String, Answer) {
        (name.into(), Answer::Choice { choice: pick.into(), probabilities: probs.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), *v)).collect::<HashMap<_, _>>(), confidence })
    }
    pub(crate) fn noul(name: &str, yes: f32) -> (String, Answer) { (name.into(), Answer::Noul { yes }) }
    pub(crate) fn score(name: &str, value: i32, confidence: f32) -> (String, Answer) { (name.into(), Answer::Score { value, confidence }) }
    pub(crate) fn decision(answers: Vec<(String, Answer)>, model: &str, self_reported: bool) -> Decision {
        Decision { answers: answers.into_iter().collect(), model: model.into(), self_reported }
    }
    /// A fallback over two fakes — the shape every runs test builds its judge from.
    pub(crate) fn fallback(primary: &Arc<FakeOracle>, agent: &Arc<FakeOracle>) -> Fallback {
        Fallback::new(primary.clone(), agent.clone(), DECISIONS_FALLBACK)
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::oracle::fake::*;
    use crate::runs::oracle::DECISIONS_FALLBACK;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn owns() -> Vec<String> { vec!["src/lib/**".into()] }

        ShellAsk { lane: "b", command: "npx vitest run src/lib", owns, item: "T-143: Stream the reply through streamdown", last_calls: last }

    #[test]
    fn the_bands_start_at_point_75_and_point_45() {
        assert_eq!(band(0.75, false), Band::Act);
        assert_eq!(band(0.7499, false), Band::SecondOpinion);
        assert_eq!(band(0.45, false), Band::SecondOpinion);
        assert_eq!(band(0.4499, false), Band::Strict);
        assert_eq!(band(0.95, true), Band::SecondOpinion, "a self-reported confidence is one band stricter");
        assert_eq!(band(0.5, true), Band::Strict);

    #[test]
    fn a_confident_allow_for_a_reading_command_runs() {
        let p = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("action", "allow", &[("allow", 0.93), ("confirm", 0.05), ("refuse", 0.02)], 0.93), noul("writes_outside", 0.04)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));
        let (o, l) = (owns(), vec![]);
        let (allow, reason, logs) = j.shell(&shell_ask(&o, &l));
        assert!(allow);
        assert_eq!(reason, "it reads only");
        assert_eq!(logs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!((logs[0].kind.as_str(), logs[0].band.as_str(), logs[0].action.as_str()), ("shell", "act", "allow"));
        assert_eq!(logs[0].model, "jev-1.13.0");
        assert_eq!(logs[0].subject, "npx vitest run src/lib");
        assert_eq!(logs[0].lane.as_deref(), Some("b"));
        assert!((logs[0].confidence - 0.93).abs() < 1e-6);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_allow_that_writes_outside_asks_one_second_opinion() {
        let p = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("action", "allow", &[("allow", 0.9)], 0.9), noul("writes_outside", 0.8)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.complete_with(json!({ "choice": "refuse", "reason": "it rewrites package.json." }));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));
        let (o, l) = (owns(), vec![]);

        assert!(!allow);
        assert_eq!(reason, "it rewrites package.json");
        assert_eq!(logs[0].choice, "confirm");
        assert_eq!(logs[1].band, "second-opinion");

        let p = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("action", "allow", &[("allow", 0.4)], 0.4), noul("writes_outside", 0.1)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));
        let (o, l) = (owns(), vec![]);
        let (allow, _, logs) = j.shell(&shell_ask(&o, &l));
        assert!(!allow);
        assert_eq!(logs[0].band, "strict");
        assert_eq!(p.complete_calls.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 0);

    #[test]
    fn a_block_claim_is_parked_retried_or_given_a_second_opinion() {
        let p = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![noul("impossible_as_written", 0.86)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![noul("impossible_as_written", 0.1)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![noul("impossible_as_written", 0.6)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.complete_with(json!({ "impossible": false, "reason": "Rename the file the item names instead." }));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));

        assert_eq!(j.block("d", "T-147: Pre-warm the session", "needs src/App.tsx", "", &o).0, BlockOutcome::Park { reading: Some(0.86) });
        assert!(matches!(j.block("d", "T-147", "hard", "", &o).0, BlockOutcome::Retry { .. }));
        match j.block("d", "T-147", "missing file", "", &o).0 {
            BlockOutcome::Retry { pushback } => assert!(pushback.contains("Rename the file")),
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),

    #[test]
    fn triage_picks_a_lane_or_none() {
        let lanes = vec![
            TriageLane { id: "a".into(), title: "Pane store batching".into(), items: vec!["T-141".into()], files: vec!["src/lib/agent-session.ts".into()] },
            TriageLane { id: "b".into(), title: "Streaming reply renderer".into(), items: vec!["T-143".into()], files: vec!["src/lib/mini-md.tsx".into()] },
        ];

        p.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("lane", "b", &[("a", 0.1), ("b", 0.85), ("none", 0.05)], 0.85)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("lane", "none", &[("none", 0.9)], 0.9)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("lane", "zz", &[("zz", 0.9)], 0.9)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));
        assert_eq!(j.triage("TS2339", &lanes).0.as_deref(), Some("b"));
        assert_eq!(j.triage("TS2339", &lanes).0, None);
        assert_eq!(j.triage("TS2339", &lanes).0, None, "a lane that isn't in the run is never reopened");

        let p = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![score("lanes", 5, 0.9)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        p.decide_with(decision(vec![score("lanes", 5, 0.2)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));
        assert_eq!(j.sizing("items and files", 3).0, 3);
        assert_eq!(j.sizing("items and files", 6).0, 4, "low confidence: fewer lanes");
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unreachable_provider_falls_back_once_and_then_fails_strict() {
        let primary = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        primary.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("action", "allow", &[("allow", 0.95)], 0.95), noul("writes_outside", 0.05)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        primary.fail_next_decide();
        let agent = FakeOracle::named("agent");
        agent.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("action", "allow", &[("allow", 0.9)], 0.9), noul("writes_outside", 0.1)], "agent", true));
        agent.complete_with(json!({ "choice": "allow", "reason": "it only runs the tests" }));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&primary, &agent));
        let (o, l) = (owns(), vec![]);
        let (a1, _, l1) = j.shell(&shell_ask(&o, &l));
        assert!(a1);
        assert_eq!(l1[0].model, "jev-1.13.0");
        assert_eq!(j.take_notice(), None);
        let (a2, _, l2) = j.shell(&shell_ask(&o, &l));
        assert!(a2, "the agent answered in its place");
        assert_eq!(l2[0].model, "agent", "the log says which model answered");
        assert!(l2[0].self_reported);
        assert_eq!(l2[0].band, "second-opinion", "and its self-reported confidence counts one band stricter");
        assert_eq!(j.take_notice().as_deref(), Some(DECISIONS_FALLBACK));
        assert_eq!(j.take_notice(), None, "one notice for the run");
        let (a3, _, l3) = j.shell(&shell_ask(&o, &l));
        assert_eq!(primary.decide_calls.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 2, "never asked again once it failed");
        assert!(!a3, "both unreachable: refuse");
        assert_eq!(l3[0].model, "none");
        assert_eq!(j.block("a", "T-1", "why", "", &o).0, BlockOutcome::Park { reading: None }, "both unreachable: park");
        assert_eq!(j.triage("out", &[]).0, None, "both unreachable: none");
        assert_eq!(j.sizing("table", 3).0, 1, "both unreachable: one lane");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lanes_question_gets_an_answer_or_an_honest_default() {
        let p = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        p.complete_with(json!({ "answer": "Use the existing token, --dur-fast." }));
        let j = Judge::new(fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")));
        let (a, logs) = j.ask("c", "Which duration for the chevron?", "Lane C: Scroll anchoring");
        assert_eq!(a, "Use the existing token, --dur-fast.");
        assert_eq!(logs[0].kind, "ask");
        let (a2, _) = j.ask("c", "again?", "");
        assert_eq!(a2, NO_ANSWER);
    }
}
```

```rust
        p.complete_with(json!({ "verdict": "really" }));

        p.complete_with(json!({ "pass": false, "findings": [] }));
        let r = review(&fallback(&p, &FakeOracle::named("agent")), &[], "diff", &[]);
        assert_eq!(r.findings.len(), 1);
        assert!(r.findings[0].what.contains("named nothing specific"));
    }
```
