- [ ] **Step 4: The judge**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/judge.rs`:

```rust
//! The judge (spec §6): four bounded decisions on the provider layer's
//! `decide`, acted on by confidence band — high acts, middle asks one second
//! opinion (`complete_json`), low takes the stricter option — and a lane's
//! question (`ask`). Never delegated: ownership, git by path, push and release,
//! exit codes, the review verdict. The judge sees command text, plan excerpts,
//! titles and failing output — never whole files. Every call is logged.
use super::oracle::Fallback;
use super::record::{iso_now, DecisionLog};
use crate::providers::{Answer, Criterion, Decision, DecisionRequest};
use serde_json::json;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub const ACT: f32 = 0.75;
pub const SECOND: f32 = 0.45;
const STATE_CAP: usize = 24_000;

pub const NO_JUDGE: &str = "Chronicle couldn't check this command, so it wasn't run.";
pub const NO_ANSWER: &str = "Chronicle couldn't answer that right now. Make the most reasonable choice within your lane's files and say which you made in your report.";
const PUSHBACK: &str = "Chronicle read your reason and thinks the item can be done as written. Try it again; if it truly can't be, say exactly what stops it.";
const SHELL_INSTRUCTIONS: &str = "A coding agent working on one lane of a plan wants to run a shell command. The lane may change only the files it owns. Choose allow if the command only reads, builds or tests, or writes only inside the owned files; confirm if you are unsure; refuse if it could change files outside them, the network, or the machine. Also answer whether the command writes outside the owned files.";
const BLOCK_INSTRUCTIONS: &str = "A coding agent says an item of a plan cannot be done as written. Decide whether the item truly cannot be done as written (yes), or whether the agent should try again (no).";
const TRIAGE_INSTRUCTIONS: &str = "Every lane passed its own checks, but the whole project's check failed after all were saved. Choose the lane whose changes most likely broke it, or none if no lane's changes explain the failure.";
const SIZING_INSTRUCTIONS: &str = "Items of a plan and the files each names are listed, with the pairs that share files. Score how many parallel lanes the work splits into without two lanes touching the same file (1 to 6).";
const SECOND_SYSTEM: &str = "You give a short second opinion for a coding orchestrator. You may read the repository. Answer only in the JSON shape asked for.";
const ASK_SYSTEM: &str = "You answer a coding agent's question about its lane of a plan in one or two sentences, using the plan and the repository. Answer only in the JSON shape asked for.";

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Band { Act, SecondOpinion, Strict }

impl Band {
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str { match self { Band::Act => "act", Band::SecondOpinion => "second-opinion", Band::Strict => "strict" } }
}

/// REFLEX's three bands. A self-reported confidence (an emulated `decide`,
/// agents spec §9) counts one band stricter.
pub fn band(confidence: f32, self_reported: bool) -> Band {
    let b = if confidence >= ACT { Band::Act } else if confidence >= SECOND { Band::SecondOpinion } else { Band::Strict };
    if !self_reported { return b; }
    if b == Band::Act { Band::SecondOpinion } else { Band::Strict }
}

pub struct ShellAsk<'a> { pub lane: &'a str, pub command: &'a str, pub owns: &'a [String], pub item: &'a str, pub last_calls: &'a [String] }

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum BlockOutcome { Park { reading: Option<f32> }, Retry { pushback: String } }

pub struct TriageLane { pub id: String, pub title: String, pub items: Vec<String>, pub files: Vec<String> }

fn cap(s: String) -> String {
    if s.len() <= STATE_CAP { return s; }
    let mut end = STATE_CAP;
    while !s.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
    format!("{}\n[…cut]", &s[..end])
}
fn choice_of(d: &Decision, name: &str) -> Option<(String, BTreeMap<String, f32>, f32)> {
    match d.answers.get(name)? {
        Answer::Choice { choice, probabilities, confidence } => Some((choice.clone(), probabilities.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), *v)).collect(), *confidence)),
        _ => None,
    }
}
fn noul_of(d: &Decision, name: &str) -> Option<f32> { match d.answers.get(name)? { Answer::Noul { yes } => Some(*yes), _ => None } }
fn score_of(d: &Decision, name: &str) -> Option<(i32, f32)> { match d.answers.get(name)? { Answer::Score { value, confidence } => Some((*value, *confidence)), _ => None } }
fn half(s: &str) -> String { s.trim().trim_end_matches('.').to_string() }

pub struct Judge { oracle: Fallback }

impl Judge {
    pub fn new(oracle: Fallback) -> Self { Judge { oracle } }
    pub fn take_notice(&self) -> Option<String> { self.oracle.take_notice() }

    /// Decision 1: a shell command no rule decides.
    pub fn shell(&self, a: &ShellAsk) -> (bool, String, Vec<DecisionLog>) {
        let state = cap(format!("Command: {}\nThe lane owns: {}\nIts item: {}\nIts last two tool calls:\n{}", a.command, a.owns.join(", "), a.item, a.last_calls.join("\n")));
        let mk = || DecisionRequest { state: state.clone(), instructions: SHELL_INSTRUCTIONS.into(), criteria: vec![
            Criterion::Choice { name: "action".into(), options: vec!["allow".into(), "confirm".into(), "refuse".into()] },
            Criterion::Noul { name: "writes_outside".into() },
        ] };
        let base = DecisionLog { at: iso_now(), kind: "shell".into(), lane: Some(a.lane.into()), subject: a.command.into(), ..Default::default() };
        let d = match self.oracle.decide(&mk) {
            Ok(d) => d,
            Err(_) => return (false, NO_JUDGE.into(), vec![DecisionLog { model: "none".into(), choice: "refuse".into(), band: "strict".into(), action: "refuse".into(), reason: "no decision model could be reached".into(), ..base }]),
        };
        let (mut choice, probabilities, confidence) = choice_of(&d, "action").unwrap_or(("refuse".into(), BTreeMap::new(), 0.0));
        let writes = noul_of(&d, "writes_outside").unwrap_or(1.0);
        if choice == "allow" && writes >= 0.5 { choice = "confirm".into(); }
        let b = band(confidence, d.self_reported);
        let first = DecisionLog { model: d.model.clone(), choice: choice.clone(), probabilities, confidence, band: b.as_str().into(), self_reported: d.self_reported, ..base.clone() };
        let (allow, reason, extra) = match (b, choice.as_str()) {
            (Band::Strict, _) => (false, "it wasn’t clear the command stays inside the lane’s files".to_string(), vec![]),
            (Band::Act, "allow") => (true, if writes < 0.2 { "it reads only".to_string() } else { "it stays inside the lane’s files".to_string() }, vec![]),
            (Band::Act, "refuse") => (false, "it could change files the lane doesn’t own".to_string(), vec![]),
            _ => self.second_shell(&state, &base),
        };
        let mut out = vec![DecisionLog { action: if allow { "allow".into() } else { "refuse".into() }, reason: reason.clone(), ..first }];
        out.extend(extra);
        (allow, reason, out)
    }

    fn second_shell(&self, state: &str, base: &DecisionLog) -> (bool, String, Vec<DecisionLog>) {
        let schema = json!({ "type": "object", "required": ["choice", "reason"], "properties": { "choice": { "enum": ["allow", "refuse"] }, "reason": { "type": "string" } } });
        match self.oracle.complete_json(SECOND_SYSTEM, &format!("{state}\n\nMay this lane run the command? Answer allow or refuse, with a half-sentence reason."), &schema) {
            Ok((v, model)) => {
                let allow = v["choice"] == "allow";
                let mut reason = half(v["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));
                if reason.is_empty() { reason = if allow { "a second opinion allowed it".into() } else { "a second opinion refused it".into() }; }
                let log = DecisionLog { model, choice: v["choice"].as_str().unwrap_or("refuse").into(), confidence: 1.0, band: "second-opinion".into(),
                    action: if allow { "allow".into() } else { "refuse".into() }, reason: reason.clone(), ..base.clone() };
                (allow, reason, vec![log])
            }
            Err(_) => (false, "the second opinion couldn’t be reached".into(), vec![]),
        }
    }

    /// Decision 2: a lane's `block` claim on an item.
    pub fn block(&self, lane: &str, item: &str, reason: &str, plan_excerpt: &str, owns: &[String]) -> (BlockOutcome, Vec<DecisionLog>) {
        let state = cap(format!("The item as written:\n{item}\n\nThe lane's reason it can't be done:\n{reason}\n\nFrom the plan:\n{plan_excerpt}\n\nFiles the lane owns: {}", owns.join(", ")));
        let mk = || DecisionRequest { state: state.clone(), instructions: BLOCK_INSTRUCTIONS.into(), criteria: vec![Criterion::Noul { name: "impossible_as_written".into() }] };
        let base = DecisionLog { at: iso_now(), kind: "block".into(), lane: Some(lane.into()), subject: item.into(), ..Default::default() };
        let d = match self.oracle.decide(&mk) {
            Ok(d) => d,
            Err(_) => return (BlockOutcome::Park { reading: None }, vec![DecisionLog { model: "none".into(), choice: "park".into(), band: "strict".into(), action: "park".into(), ..base }]),
        };
        let yes = noul_of(&d, "impossible_as_written").unwrap_or(0.5);
        let conf = yes.max(1.0 - yes);
        let b = band(conf, d.self_reported);
        let first = DecisionLog { model: d.model.clone(), choice: if yes >= 0.5 { "impossible".into() } else { "possible".into() },
            probabilities: BTreeMap::from([("yes".to_string(), yes), ("no".to_string(), 1.0 - yes)]), confidence: conf, band: b.as_str().into(),
            self_reported: d.self_reported, ..base.clone() };
        let (outcome, extra) = match b {
            Band::Act if yes >= 0.5 => (BlockOutcome::Park { reading: Some(yes) }, vec![]),
            Band::Act => (BlockOutcome::Retry { pushback: PUSHBACK.into() }, vec![]),
            Band::Strict => (BlockOutcome::Park { reading: Some(yes) }, vec![]),
            Band::SecondOpinion => {
                let schema = json!({ "type": "object", "required": ["impossible", "reason"], "properties": { "impossible": { "type": "boolean" }, "reason": { "type": "string" } } });
                match self.oracle.complete_json(SECOND_SYSTEM, &format!("{state}\n\nCan the item truly not be done as written? If it can, say in one sentence how."), &schema) {
                    Ok((v, model)) => {
                        let impossible = v["impossible"].as_bool().unwrap_or(true);
                        let log = DecisionLog { model, choice: if impossible { "impossible".into() } else { "possible".into() }, confidence: 1.0, band: "second-opinion".into(),
                            action: if impossible { "park".into() } else { "retry".into() }, ..base.clone() };
                        if impossible { (BlockOutcome::Park { reading: Some(yes) }, vec![log]) }
                        else { (BlockOutcome::Retry { pushback: v["reason"].as_str().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()).unwrap_or(PUSHBACK).to_string() }, vec![log]) }
                    }
                    Err(_) => (BlockOutcome::Park { reading: Some(yes) }, vec![]),
                }
            }
        };
        let action = match &outcome { BlockOutcome::Park { .. } => "park", BlockOutcome::Retry { .. } => "retry" };
        let mut out = vec![DecisionLog { action: action.into(), ..first }];
        out.extend(extra);
        (outcome, out)
    }

    /// Decision 3: the head check failed with every lane green.
    pub fn triage(&self, failing: &str, lanes: &[TriageLane]) -> (Option<String>, Vec<DecisionLog>) {
        let table = lanes.iter().map(|l| format!("Lane {}: {} — items {} — changed {}", l.id, l.title, l.items.join(", "), l.files.join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let state = cap(format!("Failing output:\n{failing}\n\nLanes:\n{table}"));
        let mut options: Vec<String> = lanes.iter().map(|l| l.id.clone()).collect();
        options.push("none".into());
        let mk = || DecisionRequest { state: state.clone(), instructions: TRIAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.into(), criteria: vec![Criterion::Choice { name: "lane".into(), options: options.clone() }] };
        let base = DecisionLog { at: iso_now(), kind: "triage".into(), subject: "the whole-project check".into(), ..Default::default() };
        let d = match self.oracle.decide(&mk) {
            Ok(d) => d,
            Err(_) => return (None, vec![DecisionLog { model: "none".into(), choice: "none".into(), band: "strict".into(), action: "none".into(), ..base }]),
        };
        let (choice, probabilities, confidence) = choice_of(&d, "lane").unwrap_or(("none".into(), BTreeMap::new(), 0.0));
        let valid = |c: &str| c != "none" && lanes.iter().any(|l| l.id == c);
        let b = band(confidence, d.self_reported);
        let pick = match b {
            Band::Act => Some(choice.clone()).filter(|c| valid(c)),
            Band::Strict => None,
            Band::SecondOpinion => {
                let schema = json!({ "type": "object", "required": ["lane"], "properties": { "lane": { "enum": options.clone() } } });
                self.oracle.complete_json(SECOND_SYSTEM, &format!("{state}\n\nWhich lane should get one fix pass, or none?"), &schema).ok()
                    .and_then(|(v, _)| v["lane"].as_str().map(String::from)).filter(|c| valid(c))
            }
        };
        let action = pick.as_ref().map(|p| format!("reopen {p}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "none".into());
        (pick, vec![DecisionLog { model: d.model, choice, probabilities, confidence, band: b.as_str().into(), self_reported: d.self_reported, action, ..base }])
    }

    /// Decision 4: how many lanes to aim for, before the planner is prompted.
    pub fn sizing(&self, table: &str, max_lanes: u32) -> (u32, Vec<DecisionLog>) {
        let max = max_lanes.max(1);
        let state = cap(table.to_string());
        let mk = || DecisionRequest { state: state.clone(), instructions: SIZING_INSTRUCTIONS.into(), criteria: vec![Criterion::Score { name: "lanes".into(), min: 1, max: 6 }] };
        let base = DecisionLog { at: iso_now(), kind: "sizing".into(), subject: "lane count".into(), ..Default::default() };
        let d = match self.oracle.decide(&mk) {
            Ok(d) => d,
            Err(_) => return (1, vec![DecisionLog { model: "none".into(), choice: "1".into(), band: "strict".into(), action: "aim for 1 lane".into(), ..base }]),
        };
        let (value, confidence) = score_of(&d, "lanes").unwrap_or((1, 0.0));
        let value = value.clamp(1, 6) as u32;
        let b = band(confidence, d.self_reported);
        let n = match b {
            Band::Act => value,
            Band::Strict => value.saturating_sub(1).max(1),
            Band::SecondOpinion => {
                let schema = json!({ "type": "object", "required": ["lanes"], "properties": { "lanes": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 6 } } });
                self.oracle.complete_json(SECOND_SYSTEM, &format!("{state}\n\nHow many lanes?"), &schema).ok()
                    .and_then(|(v, _)| v["lanes"].as_u64()).map(|x| (x as u32).clamp(1, 6)).unwrap_or(value.saturating_sub(1).max(1))
            }
        }.min(max).max(1);
        (n, vec![DecisionLog { model: d.model, choice: value.to_string(), confidence, band: b.as_str().into(), self_reported: d.self_reported, action: format!("aim for {n} lanes"), ..base }])
    }

    /// A lane's question (`chronicle_run_ask`): answered here, never by the user.
    pub fn ask(&self, lane: &str, question: &str, context: &str) -> (String, Vec<DecisionLog>) {
        let schema = json!({ "type": "object", "required": ["answer"], "properties": { "answer": { "type": "string" } } });
        let base = DecisionLog { at: iso_now(), kind: "ask".into(), lane: Some(lane.into()), subject: question.into(), band: "act".into(), ..Default::default() };
        match self.oracle.complete_json(ASK_SYSTEM, &cap(format!("{context}\n\nThe lane asks:\n{question}")), &schema) {
            Ok((v, model)) => {
                let a = v["answer"].as_str().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string();
                let a = if a.is_empty() { NO_ANSWER.to_string() } else { a };
                (a, vec![DecisionLog { model, choice: "answered".into(), confidence: 1.0, action: "answered".into(), ..base }])
            }
            Err(_) => (NO_ANSWER.into(), vec![DecisionLog { model: "none".into(), choice: "none".into(), action: "default answer".into(), ..base }]),
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The review verdict (spec §3): the saved diff and the check output, read by
//! the Review role (agents spec §9), answered as schema-checked JSON. A
//! verdict that can't be read is never guessed: the save counts as not
//! reviewed, and the lane gets a fix pass that says so.
use super::oracle::Fallback;
use super::record::{CheckResult, Finding, Review};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

pub const UNREVIEWED: &str = "The review's answer couldn't be read, so this save counts as not reviewed. Re-check the work against the items.";
const REVIEW_SYSTEM: &str = "You review one lane of a coding plan: its items, its saved diff and its check output. Pass it only if the diff does what the items ask, correctly and completely. Otherwise list findings with file, line and what is wrong. Answer only in the JSON shape asked for.";

pub fn review_schema() -> Value {
    json!({ "type": "object", "required": ["pass", "findings"], "properties": {
        "pass": { "type": "boolean" },
        "findings": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "object", "required": ["file", "line", "what"],
            "properties": { "file": { "type": "string" }, "line": { "type": "integer" }, "what": { "type": "string" } } } } } })
}

pub fn parse_review(v: &Value, model: &str) -> Option<Review> {
    let pass = v.get("pass")?.as_bool()?;
    let findings = v.get("findings")?.as_array()?.iter().filter_map(|f| Some(Finding {
        file: f.get("file")?.as_str()?.to_string(),
        line: f.get("line").and_then(|l| l.as_u64()).unwrap_or(0) as u32,
        what: f.get("what")?.as_str()?.to_string(),
    })).collect();
    Some(Review { model: model.to_string(), pass, findings })
}

pub fn review(oracle: &Fallback, items: &[String], diff: &str, checks: &[CheckResult]) -> Review {
    let checks_text = if checks.is_empty() { "No automated checks for this lane.".to_string() } else {
        checks.iter().map(|c| format!("{}: exit {}", c.name, c.exit.map(|e| e.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| "none".into()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n")
    };
    let prompt = format!("The lane's items:\n{}\n\nThe checks:\n{checks_text}\n\nThe saved diff:\n{diff}", items.join("\n"));
    for _ in 0..2 {
        match oracle.complete_json(REVIEW_SYSTEM, &prompt, &review_schema()) {
            Ok((v, model)) => if let Some(mut r) = parse_review(&v, &model) {
                if !r.pass && r.findings.is_empty() {
                    r.findings.push(Finding { file: String::new(), line: 0, what: "The review didn't pass this save but named nothing specific; re-check the work against the items.".into() });
                }
                return r;
            },
            Err(_) => break,
        }
    }
    Review { model: "none".into(), pass: false, findings: vec![Finding { file: String::new(), line: 0, what: UNREVIEWED.into() }] }
}
```

### Task 7: The scheduler (pure)

**Files:**
- Create: `src-tauri/src/runs/scheduler.rs`
- Modify: `src-tauri/src/runs/mod.rs` (`pub mod scheduler;`)
- Test: `#[cfg(test)]` in `scheduler.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `record::{RunRecord, RunState, LaneState, LaneRecord, NeedsYou}` (Task 1).
- Produces (in `crate::runs::scheduler`):
  - consts `MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3`, `MAX_FIX_PASSES = 2`, `STALL_MS = 300_000`
  - `backoff_ms(attempt: u32) -> u64` = `min(10_000 × 2^(attempt−1), 300_000)`
  - `#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Clone)] pub enum Action { Start(String), Gate(String), Stall(String), Park { lane: String, reason: String }, HeadCheck, Close, NeedsYou }`
  - `pub struct Tick<'a> { pub now_ms: u64, pub last_wire: &'a HashMap<String, u64>, pub max_lanes: usize, pub main_tree: bool }`
  - `next_actions(rec: &RunRecord, t: &Tick) -> Vec<Action>` (only for a `Running` run)
  - `blocking(rec: &RunRecord) -> usize` (needs-you rows that hold the run: parked, exhausted, head)
  - `resume(rec: &RunRecord) -> (RunRecord, Vec<Action>)` — normalises an `interrupted` record by its `interrupted_from`

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn run(lanes: &[(&str, &[&str])]) -> RunRecord {

        r.lane_map = Some(LaneMap { items: vec![], lanes: lanes.iter().map(|(id, deps)| LaneSpec {

    #[test]
    fn backoff_doubles_from_ten_seconds_to_a_five_minute_cap() {
        assert_eq!(backoff_ms(1), 10_000);
        assert_eq!(backoff_ms(2), 20_000);
        assert_eq!(backoff_ms(3), 40_000);
        assert_eq!(backoff_ms(6), 300_000);
        assert_eq!(backoff_ms(40), 300_000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn lanes_start_when_their_dependencies_closed_and_a_slot_is_free() {
        let w = HashMap::new();
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[]), ("b", &[]), ("c", &[]), ("d", &[]), ("e", &["a"])]);
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::Start("a".into()), Action::Start("b".into()), Action::Start("c".into())], "three slots by default");
        for id in ["a", "b", "c"] { set(&mut r, id, LaneState::Streaming); }
        assert!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)).is_empty(), "every slot is busy");
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Closed);
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::Start("d".into())], "d first: map order; e's dependency a has closed but only one slot is free");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_silent_for_five_minutes_is_stalled() {
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Streaming);
        let w = HashMap::from([("a".to_string(), 1_000u64)]);
        assert!(next_actions(&r, &tick(1_000 + STALL_MS, &w)).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(1_001 + STALL_MS, &w)), vec![Action::Stall("a".into())]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_attempt_retries_after_its_backoff_and_parks_after_three() {
        let w = HashMap::new();
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Failed);
        r.lane_mut("a").unwrap().attempt = 1;
        r.lane_mut("a").unwrap().retry_at_ms = Some(10_000);
        assert!(next_actions(&r, &tick(9_999, &w)).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(10_000, &w)), vec![Action::Start("a".into())]);
        r.lane_mut("a").unwrap().attempt = 3;
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(10_000, &w)), vec![Action::Park { lane: "a".into(), reason: "Lane A didn’t finish after three tries.".into() }]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_finished_turn_is_gated_and_in_main_tree_mode_waits_for_quiet() {
        let w = HashMap::new();
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[]), ("b", &[]), ("c", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Finishing);
        set(&mut r, "b", LaneState::Streaming);
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::Gate("a".into()), Action::Start("c".into())]);
        let quiet = Tick { main_tree: true, ..tick(0, &w) };
        assert!(next_actions(&r, &quiet).is_empty(), "checks in the project wait for b's turn, and nothing new starts");
        set(&mut r, "b", LaneState::Finishing);
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &quiet), vec![Action::Gate("a".into()), Action::Gate("b".into())]);
        r.lane_mut("a").unwrap().retry_at_ms = Some(5_000);
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::Gate("b".into()), Action::Start("c".into())], "a save that met a busy index waits its retry");
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_lane_closed_means_the_head_check_and_a_parked_item_means_needs_you() {
        let w = HashMap::new();
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[]), ("b", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Closed);
        set(&mut r, "b", LaneState::Closed);
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::HeadCheck]);
        r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "refused".into(), lane: Some("a".into()), item: None, reason: "git push".into(), reading: None });
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::HeadCheck], "a refused publish never holds the run");
        r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "parked".into(), lane: Some("b".into()), item: Some("T-147".into()), reason: "x".into(), reading: Some(0.86) });
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::NeedsYou]);
        assert_eq!(blocking(&r), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_whose_dependency_is_parked_waits_and_the_run_needs_you() {
        let w = HashMap::new();
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[]), ("b", &["a"])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Parked);
        r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "exhausted".into(), lane: Some("a".into()), item: None, reason: "three tries".into(), reading: None });
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&r, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::NeedsYou]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn resume_from_gating_runs_the_head_check_again() {
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[]), ("b", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Closed);
        set(&mut r, "b", LaneState::Closed);
        r.head_check = Some(HeadCheck { commit: "abc".into(), checks: vec![], reopened: None });
        r.state = RunState::Interrupted;
        r.interrupted_from = Some(RunState::Gating);
        let (back, acts) = resume(&r);
        assert_eq!(back.state, RunState::Gating);
        assert_eq!(acts, vec![Action::HeadCheck]);
        assert_eq!(back.interrupted_from, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn resume_rechecks_a_lane_caught_mid_gate() {
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[]), ("b", &[]), ("c", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Closed);
        set(&mut r, "b", LaneState::Gating);
        r.lane_mut("b").unwrap().commit = Some("b1".into());
        r.lane_mut("b").unwrap().gates.push(Gate { commit: "b1".into(), ..Default::default() });
        set(&mut r, "c", LaneState::Streaming);
        r.lane_mut("c").unwrap().attempt = 2;
        r.state = RunState::Interrupted;
        r.interrupted_from = Some(RunState::Running);
        let (back, acts) = resume(&r);
        assert_eq!(back.state, RunState::Running);
        assert_eq!(back.lane("a").unwrap().state, LaneState::Closed, "a closed lane stays closed");
        assert_eq!(back.lane("b").unwrap().state, LaneState::Finishing, "re-gated on the commit it has");
        assert_eq!(back.lane("c").unwrap().state, LaneState::Waiting, "a turn cut off mid-way starts again");
        assert_eq!(back.lane("c").unwrap().attempt, 2, "an interruption is not the lane's failure");
        assert!(acts.is_empty(), "the next tick gates b and starts c");
        let w = HashMap::new();
        assert_eq!(next_actions(&back, &tick(0, &w)), vec![Action::Gate("b".into()), Action::Start("c".into())]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn resume_from_closing_closes() {
        let mut r = run(&[("a", &[])]);
        set(&mut r, "a", LaneState::Closed);
        r.state = RunState::Interrupted;
        r.interrupted_from = Some(RunState::Closing);
        assert_eq!(resume(&r), ({ let mut b = r.clone(); b.state = RunState::Closing; b.interrupted_from = None; b }, vec![Action::Close]));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The scheduler**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/scheduler.rs`:

```rust
//! What a running run does next (spec §3), as a pure function of its record
//! and the clock — the controller performs what this returns. Symphony's
//! attempt machine: start a lane when its dependencies closed and a slot is
//! free; stall after five minutes without a wire message; back off
//! `min(10 s × 2^(n−1), 300 s)`; three attempts, then parked.
use super::record::{LaneRecord, LaneState, NeedsYou, RunRecord, RunState};
use std::collections::HashMap;

pub const MAX_ATTEMPTS: u32 = 3;
pub const MAX_FIX_PASSES: u32 = 2;
pub const STALL_MS: u64 = 300_000;

pub fn backoff_ms(attempt: u32) -> u64 {
    let a = attempt.clamp(1, 20);
    (10_000u64 << (a - 1)).min(300_000)
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Clone)]
pub enum Action { Start(String), Gate(String), Stall(String), Park { lane: String, reason: String }, HeadCheck, Close, NeedsYou }

pub struct Tick<'a> { pub now_ms: u64, pub last_wire: &'a HashMap<String, u64>, pub max_lanes: usize, pub main_tree: bool }

fn holds(n: &NeedsYou) -> bool { matches!(n.kind.as_str(), "parked" | "exhausted" | "head") }
pub fn blocking(rec: &RunRecord) -> usize { rec.needs_you.iter().filter(|n| holds(n)).count() }

pub fn next_actions(rec: &RunRecord, t: &Tick) -> Vec<Action> {
    use LaneState::*;
    if rec.state != RunState::Running { return vec![]; }
    let Some(map) = &rec.lane_map else { return vec![] };
    let state_of = |id: &str| rec.lane(id).map(|l| l.state);
    let mut out = vec![];
    let busy = rec.lanes.iter().filter(|l| l.state.busy()).count();
    let mut free = t.max_lanes.saturating_sub(busy);
    let working = rec.lanes.iter().any(|l| matches!(l.state, Preparing | BuildingPrompt | Launching | Initialising | Streaming | Fixing));
    let gating = rec.lanes.iter().any(|l| l.state == Gating);
    // checks running in the project itself: nothing new starts, and a gate waits for quiet
    let hold_starts = t.main_tree && rec.lanes.iter().any(|l| matches!(l.state, Finishing | Gating));
    for l in &rec.lanes {
        if matches!(l.state, Initialising | Streaming | Fixing) {
            let last = t.last_wire.get(&l.id).copied().unwrap_or(t.now_ms);
            if t.now_ms.saturating_sub(last) > STALL_MS { out.push(Action::Stall(l.id.clone())); }
        }
    }
    for l in &rec.lanes {
        let due = l.retry_at_ms.map(|at| at <= t.now_ms).unwrap_or(true);
        if l.state == Finishing && due && !(t.main_tree && (working || gating)) { out.push(Action::Gate(l.id.clone())); }
    }
    for l in &rec.lanes {
        if !l.state.between_tries() { continue; }
        if l.attempt >= MAX_ATTEMPTS {
            out.push(Action::Park { lane: l.id.clone(), reason: format!("Lane {} didn’t finish after three tries.", l.id.to_uppercase()) });
        } else if !hold_starts && free > 0 && l.retry_at_ms.map(|at| at <= t.now_ms).unwrap_or(true) {
            out.push(Action::Start(l.id.clone()));
            free -= 1;
        }
    }
    if !hold_starts {
        for spec in &map.lanes {
            if free == 0 { break; }
            if state_of(&spec.id) != Some(Waiting) { continue; }
            if spec.depends_on.iter().all(|d| state_of(d) == Some(Closed)) {
                out.push(Action::Start(spec.id.clone()));
                free -= 1;
            }
        }
    }
    let all_closed = !rec.lanes.is_empty() && rec.lanes.iter().all(|l| l.state == Closed);
    if all_closed && blocking(rec) == 0 && out.is_empty() { out.push(Action::HeadCheck); }
    let moving = rec.lanes.iter().any(|l: &LaneRecord| l.state.busy() || l.state.between_tries())
        || out.iter().any(|a| matches!(a, Action::Start(_) | Action::Park { .. } | Action::HeadCheck));
    if !moving && blocking(rec) > 0 { out.push(Action::NeedsYou); }
    out
}

/// An interrupted run, set back on its feet from what the record says
/// (spec §3, Resume). Closed lanes stay closed; a lane caught mid-gate is
/// re-gated on what it has (the save finds nothing new to commit); a turn cut
/// off mid-way starts again without spending an attempt.
pub fn resume(rec: &RunRecord) -> (RunRecord, Vec<Action>) {
    use LaneState::*;
    let mut r = rec.clone();
    let from = rec.interrupted_from.unwrap_or(RunState::Running);
    r.interrupted_from = None;
    for l in r.lanes.iter_mut() {
        l.state = match l.state {
            Finishing | Gating => Finishing,
            Preparing | BuildingPrompt | Launching | Initialising | Streaming | Fixing | Cancelled => Waiting,
            s => s,
        };
        if l.state == Waiting { l.retry_at_ms = None; }
    }
    match from {
        RunState::Planning => { r.state = RunState::Planning; (r, vec![]) }
        RunState::Gating => { r.state = RunState::Gating; (r, vec![Action::HeadCheck]) }
        RunState::Closing => { r.state = RunState::Closing; (r, vec![Action::Close]) }
        _ => { r.state = RunState::Running; (r, vec![]) }
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::record::{Finding, Review};

    fn checks() -> Vec<Check> { vec![Check { name: "test".into(), command: "npm run test".into() }, Check { name: "cargo test".into(), command: "cargo test".into() }] }

    #[test]
    fn the_planner_writes_the_plan_as_today_and_declares_lanes() {
        let p = planner_notes("You are turning a queue of user-written notes … fixes/phase_10_fixes_plan.md …", 3, &checks());
        assert!(p.starts_with("You are turning a queue"), "today's head comes first, unchanged");
        assert!(p.contains("chronicle_run_declare"));
        assert!(p.contains("aim for 3 lanes"));
        assert!(p.contains("this project's check list: test, cargo test"));
        assert!(p.contains("No lane may own anything under .chronicle/, fixes/, or chronicle.json."));
        assert!(p.contains("Do not start the work yourself"));
        assert!(planner_notes("h", 2, &[]).contains("which is empty — leave checks empty"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_plan_run_never_rewrites_the_plan_and_a_prompt_run_writes_one() {
        let p = planner_plan(9, "fixes/phase_9_fixes_plan.md", "fixes/phase_9_fixes_prompt.md", 2, &checks());
        assert!(p.contains("Do not change either file"));
        assert!(p.contains("fixes/phase_9_fixes_plan.md"));
        let q = planner_prompt(11, "Make the pane faster", 2, &checks());
        assert!(q.contains("fixes/phase_11_fixes_plan.md") && q.contains("fixes/phase_11_fixes_prompt.md"));
        assert!(q.contains("Make the pane faster"));
        assert!(q.contains("Round kind: bug fixes"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_is_told_its_files_its_items_and_the_rules_but_not_to_commit_or_flip_notes() {
        let lane = LaneSpec { id: "c".into(), title: "Scroll anchoring".into(), items: vec!["T-144".into()],
            owns: vec!["src/screens/agent/thread-scroll.ts".into(), "src/index.css".into()], depends_on: vec![], checks: vec!["test".into(), "typecheck".into()] };
        let items = vec![Item { id: "T-144".into(), title: "Scroll anchoring on a bottom sentinel".into(), note: None, rewrite: None }];
        let p = lane_prompt(10, &lane, &items, "fixes/phase_10_fixes_plan.md");
        assert!(p.starts_with("You are lane C of round 10: Scroll anchoring."));
        assert!(p.contains("- T-144: Scroll anchoring on a bottom sentinel"));
        assert!(p.contains("src/screens/agent/thread-scroll.ts, src/index.css"));
        assert!(p.contains("checks it: test, typecheck"));
        assert!(p.contains("chronicle_run_block") && p.contains("chronicle_run_ask"));
        assert!(!p.contains("status: done") && !p.contains("Chronicle-Phase"), "Chronicle flips the notes and writes the trailer (spec §3, Closing)");
        let rewritten = vec![Item { rewrite: Some("Warm the session from the pane instead".into()), ..items[0].clone() }];
        assert!(lane_prompt(10, &lane, &rewritten, "p").contains("- T-144: Warm the session from the pane instead (rewritten by the user)"));
    }

        let gate = Gate { commit: "abc".into(), fix: 0, notice: None,
            checks: vec![CheckResult { name: "typecheck".into(), exit: Some(2), ms: 2100, timed_out: false, tail: "error TS2339: Property 'anchor' does not exist".into() },
                         CheckResult { name: "test".into(), exit: Some(0), ms: 4200, timed_out: false, tail: String::new() }],
            review: Some(Review { model: "review".into(), pass: false, findings: vec![Finding { file: "src/a.ts".into(), line: 12, what: "the anchor is never released".into() }] }) };
        let p = fix_prompt(1, &gate);
        assert!(p.contains("fix 1 of 2"));
        assert!(p.contains("`typecheck` failed (exit 2)") && p.contains("TS2339"));
        assert!(!p.contains("`test` failed"));
        assert!(p.contains("- src/a.ts:12 — the anchor is never released"));
        assert!(head_fix_prompt("boom").contains("one pass"));

        let timed = vec![CheckResult { name: "build".into(), exit: None, ms: 600_000, timed_out: true, tail: "…".into() }];
        assert!(failing_text(&timed).contains("`build` didn't finish in 10 minutes"));
        assert!(nudge_prompt(&["Use --dur-fast".into()]).contains("Use --dur-fast"));

        assert!(p.contains("Item T-3 isn't in any lane.") && p.contains("both own"));
        assert!(p.contains("chronicle_run_declare"));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The prompts**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/prompts.rs`:

```rust
//! What the planner and the lanes are told (spec §3, §5 amended). The planner
//! is today's FIXES_PROMPT_HEAD plus the lane-map instructions; lanes carry
//! their rules in the prompt (no plugin — agents spec §8). Neither is asked to
//! flip notes or write the trailer commit: Chronicle does both when it closes.
use super::checks::Check;
use super::record::{CheckResult, Gate, Item, LaneSpec};

pub const LANE_MAP_INSTRUCTIONS: &str = "Then split the plan into lanes and declare them with the chronicle_run_declare tool. Do not start the work yourself: Chronicle runs each lane as its own agent once the map is accepted.\n\n- items: every item of the plan, once, as {id, title, note}. id is the note's id from its front matter if it has one (like T-141), otherwise a short id you choose (I-1, I-2, …); note is the note's path from the notes file when the item came from a note.\n- lanes: aim for {LANES} lanes, fewer if the work does not split. Each lane is {id, title, items, owns, depends_on, checks}: id is one lowercase letter (a, b, c, …); title is a few words; items are item ids; owns are the files the lane may change, as paths or globs relative to the project root (src/lib/x.ts, src/screens/agent/**); depends_on are lane ids that must finish first; checks are names from {CHECKS}.\n- Owned paths must not overlap between lanes. A file two items both need belongs to one lane; the other lane depends on it.\n- No lane may own anything under .chronicle/, fixes/, or chronicle.json.\n- If the tool says the map is invalid, fix what it names and call it again.\n\nEnd your turn once the map is accepted, with one sentence saying how many items went into how many lanes.";

fn lane_map(lanes: u32, checks: &[Check]) -> String {
    let list = if checks.is_empty() { "this project's check list, which is empty — leave checks empty".to_string() }
        else { format!("this project's check list: {}", checks.iter().map(|c| c.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) };
    LANE_MAP_INSTRUCTIONS.replace("{LANES}", &lanes.to_string()).replace("{CHECKS}", &list)
}

pub fn planner_notes(fixes_head: &str, lanes: u32, checks: &[Check]) -> String {
    format!("{fixes_head}\n{}", lane_map(lanes, checks))
}

pub fn planner_plan(n: u64, plan_path: &str, prompt_path: &str, lanes: u32, checks: &[Check]) -> String {
    format!("The plan for round {n} is already written at {plan_path}, with its instructions at {prompt_path}. Read both. Do not change either file.\n\n{}", lane_map(lanes, checks))
}

pub fn planner_prompt(n: u64, text: &str, lanes: u32, checks: &[Check]) -> String {
    format!("You are turning the user's request below into an executable plan for this project. Write exactly two files, creating the fixes/ folder if needed:\n\n1. fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md — the request expanded into precise, unambiguous, actionable items a coding agent can execute without questions, referencing concrete files where inferable from the repo. THE FIRST LINE of this file must be exactly `Round kind: bug fixes` or `Round kind: feature additions`.\n\n2. fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md — the execution instructions to paste into Claude Code or Codex: read the plan, execute every item, verify each like a shipping change, report per-item outcomes honestly.\n\nDo not change any other file. The request:\n\n{text}\n\n{}", lane_map(lanes, checks))
}

pub fn planner_retry(reasons: &str) -> String {
    format!("Chronicle couldn't accept the lane map yet:\n{reasons}\n\nFix exactly these and call chronicle_run_declare again with the whole map. Do not start the work yourself.")
}

pub fn lane_prompt(n: u64, lane: &LaneSpec, items: &[Item], plan_path: &str) -> String {
    let id = lane.id.to_uppercase();
    let list = lane.items.iter().map(|i| match items.iter().find(|x| &x.id == i) {
        Some(Item { rewrite: Some(r), .. }) => format!("- {i}: {r} (rewritten by the user)"),
        Some(x) => format!("- {i}: {}", x.title),
        None => format!("- {i}"),
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
    let checks = if lane.checks.is_empty() { "there are no automated checks for this lane, but a reviewer reads your diff".to_string() } else { lane.checks.join(", ") };
    format!("You are lane {id} of round {n}: {}.\n\nRead {plan_path} for the whole plan. Your items:\n{list}\n\nYou may change only these files: {}. Chronicle refuses any other write, and anything a shell command changes elsewhere is reported to the user and left unsaved.\n\nRules:\n- When your turn ends Chronicle saves your work and checks it: {checks}. Do not run git commands that change anything; git status, diff, log and show are fine.\n- Never push, tag, publish or release anything.\n- Do not change notes, .chronicle/, fixes/ or chronicle.json. Chronicle marks the items done itself.\n- If an item cannot be done as written, call chronicle_run_block with the item id and the exact reason, then carry on with your other items.\n- If you need a decision the plan doesn't settle, call chronicle_run_ask.\n\nWhen every item is done and verified, end your turn with a short report, one line per item.", lane.title, lane.owns.join(", "))
}

pub fn failing_text(checks: &[CheckResult]) -> String {
    checks.iter().filter(|c| c.exit != Some(0)).map(|c| {
        let head = if c.timed_out { format!("`{}` didn't finish in {} minutes", c.name, c.ms / 60_000) }
            else { format!("`{}` failed (exit {})", c.name, c.exit.map(|e| e.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| "none".into())) };
        format!("{head}:\n```\n{}\n```", c.tail)
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n\n")
}

pub fn fix_prompt(fix: u32, gate: &Gate) -> String {
    let mut parts = vec![format!("Chronicle saved your work and checked it, and it did not pass yet. This is fix {fix} of 2.")];
    let failing = failing_text(&gate.checks);
    if !failing.is_empty() { parts.push(failing); }
    if let Some(r) = gate.review.as_ref().filter(|r| !r.pass) {
        let f = r.findings.iter().map(|f| if f.file.is_empty() { format!("- {}", f.what) } else { format!("- {}:{} — {}", f.file, f.line, f.what) }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        parts.push(format!("The review found:\n{f}"));
    }
    parts.push("Fix exactly this, within your files, then end your turn. Same rules as before.".into());
    parts.join("\n\n")
}

pub fn head_fix_prompt(failing: &str) -> String {
    format!("Every lane passed on its own, but the whole project's check failed once all lanes were saved. Chronicle picked your lane to fix it — one pass.\n\n{failing}\n\nFix it within your files, then end your turn. Same rules as before.")
}

pub fn nudge_prompt(texts: &[String]) -> String {
    format!("The user added this while you worked:\n\n{}\n\nTake it into account, finish your items, then end your turn.", texts.join("\n\n"))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests and the fake host**

At the bottom of a new `src-tauri/src/runs/runner.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) mod testkit {
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::oracle::fake::{fallback, FakeOracle};
    use crate::runs::oracle::{Fallback, REVIEW_FALLBACK};

    /// What a fake lane does when prompted: files it writes, and whether each
    /// went through a tool Chronicle allowed (true) or a shell command (false).
    pub(crate) type Work = Box<dyn Fn(&str, &str) -> Vec<(String, String, bool)> + Send + Sync>;

    pub(crate) struct FakeHost {
        work: Work,
        end_turns: bool,
        policies: Mutex<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn LanePolicy>>>,
        pub started: Mutex<Vec<String>>,
        pub stopped: Mutex<Vec<String>>,
    }
    impl FakeHost {
        pub fn new(work: Work) -> Arc<Self> { Arc::new(FakeHost { work, end_turns: true, policies: Default::default(), started: Default::default(), stopped: Default::default() }) }
        pub fn hanging(work: Work) -> Arc<Self> { Arc::new(FakeHost { work, end_turns: false, policies: Default::default(), started: Default::default(), stopped: Default::default() }) }
    }
    impl LaneHost for FakeHost {
        fn start(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, _k: u16, policy: Arc<dyn LanePolicy>) -> Result<(), String> {
            lk(&self.started).push(lane.into());
            lk(&self.policies).insert(lane.into(), policy);
            let _ = ctl.tx.send(Ev::Ready { lane: lane.into(), session: format!("s-{lane}") });
            Ok(())
        }
        fn prompt(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, _k: u16, text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
            let policy = lk(&self.policies).get(lane).cloned();
            for (rel, body, via_tool) in (self.work)(lane, text) {
                let abs = ctl.repo.join(&rel);
                if via_tool && policy.as_ref().map(|p| p.may_write(&abs).is_err()).unwrap_or(true) { continue; }
                std::fs::create_dir_all(abs.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
                std::fs::write(abs, body).unwrap();
            }
            if self.end_turns { let _ = ctl.tx.send(Ev::TurnEnd { lane: lane.into(), stop: Some("end_turn".into()), error: false }); }
            Ok(())
        }
        fn cancel(&self, _: &Arc<RunCtl>, _: &str, _: u16) {}
        fn stop(&self, _: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, _: u16) { lk(&self.stopped).push(lane.into()); }
    }

    /// A throwaway repository, `.chronicle/` ignored as the app's own, with round `n` ready.
    pub(crate) fn repo(name: &str, n: u64) -> PathBuf {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-runner-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&d).unwrap();
        let d = d.canonicalize().unwrap();
        for a in [&["init", "-q"][..], &["config", "user.email", "t@t"], &["config", "user.name", "t"], &["config", "commit.gpgsign", "false"]] { gate::git(&d, a).unwrap(); }
        std::fs::write(d.join("README.md"), "toy\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(d.join(".gitignore"), ".chronicle/\n").unwrap();
        gate::git(&d, &["add", "README.md", ".gitignore"]).unwrap();
        gate::git(&d, &["commit", "-qm", "init"]).unwrap();
        crate::notes::rounds::save(&d, &[crate::notes::rounds::Round { n, state: "ready".into(), kind: None, task_ids: vec![], note_paths: vec![],
            created_at: 0, plan_path: format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md"), prompt_path: format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md") }]).unwrap();
        d
    }

    /// Lanes a and b, owning a/** and b/**, with the checks each names.
    pub(crate) fn two_lanes(n: u64, checks_a: &[&str], checks_b: &[&str]) -> RunRecord {
        let mut r = RunRecord::new(n, RunKind::Prompt, Some(n), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md"), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md"), "toy");
        let v = |xs: &[&str]| xs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        r.lane_map = Some(LaneMap {
            items: vec![Item { id: "I-1".into(), title: "the a work".into(), note: None, rewrite: None }, Item { id: "I-2".into(), title: "the b work".into(), note: None, rewrite: None }],
            lanes: vec![
                LaneSpec { id: "a".into(), title: "Lane a work".into(), items: v(&["I-1"]), owns: v(&["a/**"]), depends_on: vec![], checks: v(checks_a) },
                LaneSpec { id: "b".into(), title: "Lane b work".into(), items: v(&["I-2"]), owns: v(&["b/**"]), depends_on: vec![], checks: v(checks_b) },
            ],
        });
        r.lanes = vec![LaneRecord::new("a"), LaneRecord::new("b")];
        r.state = RunState::Running;
        r
    }

    pub(crate) struct Kit { pub ctl: Arc<RunCtl>, pub rx: Option<Receiver<Ev>>, pub decide: Arc<FakeOracle>, pub published: Arc<Mutex<Vec<RunRecord>>> }

    pub(crate) fn kit(dir: &Path, rec: RunRecord, checks: Vec<Check>, host: Arc<dyn LaneHost>) -> Kit {
        let decide = FakeOracle::named("jev-1.13.0");
        let review = FakeOracle::named("review");
        for _ in 0..8 { review.complete_with(serde_json::json!({ "pass": true, "findings": [] })); }
        let published: Arc<Mutex<Vec<RunRecord>>> = Default::default();
        let sink = published.clone();
        let (ctl, rx) = RunCtl::new(CtlParts {
            dir: dir.to_path_buf(), repo: dir.to_path_buf(),
            scratch: std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-runner-scratch-{}-{}", dir.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy(), std::process::id())),
            rec, checks, max_lanes: 3,
            judge: Judge::new(fallback(&decide, &FakeOracle::named("agent"))),
            reviewer: Fallback::new(review.clone(), review, REVIEW_FALLBACK),
            host, publish: Arc::new(move |_d: &Path, r: &RunRecord| lk(&sink).push(r.clone())),
        });
        Kit { ctl, rx: Some(rx), decide, published }
    }

    pub(crate) fn wait_until(ctl: &RunCtl, secs: u64, pred: impl Fn(&RunRecord) -> bool) -> RunRecord {
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(secs);
        loop {
            let r = ctl.snapshot();
            if pred(&r) { return r; }
            if std::time::Instant::now() > deadline { panic!("the run never got there: {r:#?}"); }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50));
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::testkit::*;
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::oracle::fake::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn check(name: &str, command: &str) -> Check { Check { name: name.into(), command: command.into() } }

    #[test]
    fn two_lanes_run_gate_and_close_and_a_shell_leak_is_left_unsaved() {
        let r = repo("close", 10);
        let host = FakeHost::new(Box::new(|lane, _text| {
            let mut w = vec![(format!("{lane}/work.txt"), lane.to_string(), true)];
            if lane == "a" { w.push(("NOTES.txt".into(), "a shell wrote this".into(), false)); }
            w
        }));
        let mut k = kit(&r, two_lanes(10, &["test"], &["test"]), vec![check("test", "test -f README.md")], host);
        spawn(k.ctl.clone(), k.rx.take().unwrap(), vec![]);
        let done = wait_until(&k.ctl, 60, |x| x.state.terminal());
        assert_eq!(done.state, RunState::Done, "{:#?}", done);
        for id in ["a", "b"] {
            let l = done.lane(id).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(l.state, LaneState::Closed);
            assert!(l.commit.is_some());
            assert_eq!(l.gates.len(), 1);
            assert!(l.gates[0].passed());
        }
        assert_eq!(done.lane("a").unwrap().leak, vec![Leak { path: "NOTES.txt".into(), owner: None, by: None }], "b's own write while both worked is b's");
        assert!(done.lane("b").unwrap().leak.is_empty());
        let log = gate::git(&r, &["log", "--format=%s"]).unwrap();
        let subjects: Vec<&str> = log.lines().collect();
        assert_eq!(subjects[0], "Close FX-10");
        assert!(subjects.contains(&"Lane a work") && subjects.contains(&"Lane b work"), "{log}");
        assert_eq!(gate::git(&r, &["status", "--porcelain"]).unwrap(), "?? NOTES.txt", "the leak is left for the user, never committed or reverted");
        assert_eq!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&r).unwrap()[0].state, "done");
        let journal = std::fs::read_to_string(r.join(".chronicle/journal.jsonl")).unwrap();
        assert!(journal.contains("Round 10 · lane A finished.") && journal.contains("Round 10 is saved: 2 items done."), "{journal}");
        assert!(k.published.lock().unwrap().len() > 5, "every change was published");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_check_gets_a_fix_pass_with_its_output_and_then_closes() {
        let r = repo("fix", 11);
        let host = FakeHost::new(Box::new(|lane, text| {
            if lane == "b" && text.contains("fix 1 of 2") && text.contains("`fixed` failed") { vec![("b/fixed.txt".into(), "ok".into(), true)] }
            else { vec![(format!("{lane}/work.txt"), lane.to_string(), true)] }
        }));
        let mut k = kit(&r, two_lanes(11, &[], &["fixed"]), vec![check("fixed", "test -f b/fixed.txt")], host);
        spawn(k.ctl.clone(), k.rx.take().unwrap(), vec![]);
        let done = wait_until(&k.ctl, 60, |x| x.state.terminal());
        assert_eq!(done.state, RunState::Done);
        let b = done.lane("b").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(b.gates.len(), 2);
        assert!(!b.gates[0].passed());
        assert_eq!(b.gates[0].checks[0].exit, Some(1));
        assert_eq!(b.gates[1].fix, 1);
        assert!(b.gates[1].passed());
    }

    #[test]
    fn fixes_that_never_pass_park_the_lane_after_two() {
        let r = repo("park", 12);
        let host = FakeHost::new(Box::new(|lane, _| vec![(format!("{lane}/work.txt"), "x".into(), true)]));
        let mut k = kit(&r, two_lanes(12, &[], &["never"]), vec![check("never", "exit 1")], host);
        spawn(k.ctl.clone(), k.rx.take().unwrap(), vec![]);
        let done = wait_until(&k.ctl, 60, |x| x.state == RunState::NeedsYou);
        let b = done.lane("b").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(b.state, LaneState::Parked);
        assert_eq!(b.gates.len(), 3, "the save and two fix passes");
        assert_eq!(done.needs_you[0].kind, "exhausted");
        assert_eq!(done.lane("a").unwrap().state, LaneState::Closed);
        k.ctl.tx.send(Ev::Stop).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn stop_cancels_the_lanes_and_reverts_nothing() {
        let r = repo("stop", 13);
        let host = FakeHost::hanging(Box::new(|lane, _| vec![(format!("{lane}/half.txt"), "half done".into(), true)]));
        let mut k = kit(&r, two_lanes(13, &[], &[]), vec![], host.clone());
        spawn(k.ctl.clone(), k.rx.take().unwrap(), vec![]);
        wait_until(&k.ctl, 20, |x| x.lanes.iter().all(|l| l.state == LaneState::Streaming));
        k.ctl.tx.send(Ev::Stop).unwrap();
        let done = wait_until(&k.ctl, 20, |x| x.state.terminal());
        assert_eq!(done.state, RunState::Stopped);
        assert!(done.lanes.iter().all(|l| l.state == LaneState::Cancelled));
        assert!(r.join("a/half.txt").exists() && r.join("b/half.txt").exists(), "nothing is reverted");
        assert_eq!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&r).unwrap()[0].state, "failed", "the round's notes unlock");
        assert_eq!(host.stopped.lock().unwrap().len(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn resuming_a_run_caught_in_its_final_check_saves_nothing_twice() {
        let r = repo("resume", 14);
        let mut rec = two_lanes(14, &[], &[]);
        for id in ["a", "b"] {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(r.join(id)).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(r.join(id).join("work.txt"), id).unwrap();
            let gate::Saved::Commit(c) = gate::save_paths(&r, &[format!("{id}/work.txt")], &format!("Lane {id} work")).unwrap() else { panic!() };
            let l = rec.lane_mut(id).unwrap();
            l.state = LaneState::Closed;
            l.commit = Some(c.clone());
            l.gates.push(Gate { commit: c, ..Default::default() });
        }
        rec.head_check = Some(HeadCheck { commit: "x".into(), checks: vec![], reopened: None });
        rec.state = RunState::Interrupted;
        rec.interrupted_from = Some(RunState::Gating);
        let before: u32 = gate::git(&r, &["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"]).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        let (back, first) = scheduler::resume(&rec);
        let host = FakeHost::new(Box::new(|_, _| vec![]));
        let mut k = kit(&r, back, vec![check("test", "test -f a/work.txt")], host.clone());
        spawn(k.ctl.clone(), k.rx.take().unwrap(), first);
        let done = wait_until(&k.ctl, 60, |x| x.state.terminal());
        assert_eq!(done.state, RunState::Done);
        let after: u32 = gate::git(&r, &["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"]).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(after, before + 1, "only the close commit");
        assert!(host.started.lock().unwrap().is_empty(), "no lane was started again");
        assert_eq!(done.head_check.unwrap().checks[0].exit, Some(0), "the head check ran again");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_block_claim_parks_its_item_and_the_round_waits_for_you() {
        let r = repo("block", 15);
        let host = FakeHost::new(Box::new(|lane, _| vec![(format!("{lane}/work.txt"), "x".into(), true)]));
        let mut k = kit(&r, two_lanes(15, &[], &[]), vec![], host);
        k.decide.decide_with(decision(vec![noul("impossible_as_written", 0.9)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        k.decide.decide_with(decision(vec![noul("impossible_as_written", 0.1)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let said = k.ctl.block("a", "I-1", "the file it names doesn't exist").unwrap();
        assert!(said.starts_with("Accepted"), "{said}");
        assert!(k.ctl.block("a", "I-1", "still").unwrap().contains("already"), "a parked item isn't parked twice");
        assert!(k.ctl.block("b", "I-2", "hard").unwrap().starts_with("Not accepted yet"));
        assert!(k.ctl.block("a", "I-2", "x").is_err(), "a lane can only block its own items");
        spawn(k.ctl.clone(), k.rx.take().unwrap(), vec![]);
        let held = wait_until(&k.ctl, 60, |x| x.state == RunState::NeedsYou);
        let row = &held.needs_you[0];
        assert_eq!((row.kind.as_str(), row.lane.as_deref(), row.item.as_deref()), ("parked", Some("a"), Some("I-1")));
        assert_eq!(row.reading, Some(0.9));
        assert!(held.lanes.iter().all(|l| l.state == LaneState::Closed), "both lanes finished their other work");
        assert_eq!(held.decisions.iter().filter(|d| d.kind == "block").count(), 2);
        k.ctl.tx.send(Ev::Stop).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_lane_policy_refuses_a_publish_judges_a_command_and_fences_writes() {
        let r = repo("policy", 16);
        let k = kit(&r, two_lanes(16, &[], &[]), vec![], FakeHost::new(Box::new(|_, _| vec![])));
        let p = RunPolicy::new(&k.ctl, "a");
        let bash = |c: &str| json!({ "toolCall": { "toolCallId": "t", "name": "Bash", "kind": "execute", "rawInput": { "command": c } }, "options": [] });
        let a = p.answer(&bash("git push origin main"));
        assert!(!a.allow);
        assert_eq!(a.reason, crate::runs::fence::PUBLISH_REFUSAL);
        assert_eq!(k.ctl.snapshot().needs_you[0].kind, "refused");
        k.decide.decide_with(decision(vec![choice("action", "allow", &[("allow", 0.93)], 0.93), noul("writes_outside", 0.05)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let j = p.answer(&bash("npx vitest run src/lib"));
        assert!(j.allow);
        assert_eq!(j.decision.unwrap()["confidence"], json!(0.93f32));
        assert_eq!(k.ctl.snapshot().decisions[0].subject, "npx vitest run src/lib");
        let w = |path: &Path| json!({ "toolCall": { "toolCallId": "w", "name": "Write", "kind": "edit", "rawInput": { "file_path": path.to_string_lossy() } }, "options": [] });
        assert!(p.answer(&w(&r.join("a/x.ts"))).allow);
        assert!(!p.answer(&w(&r.join("b/x.ts"))).allow);
        assert!(p.may_write(&r.join("b/y.ts")).is_err());
    }
}
```
