- [ ] **Step 3: The controller**

Above the test modules in `src-tauri/src/runs/runner.rs`:

```rust
//! The run controller (spec §3, §5 amended): one thread per live run. It asks
//! the scheduler what to do, starts each lane as its own session through a
//! `LaneHost` (ACP in the app, a fake in tests), answers the lane's asks with
//! `RunPolicy`, gates each finished turn, runs the head check and triage, and
//! closes the round. Every record change goes through `update` — saved,
//! journalled, published — so Rust stays the only writer.
use super::checks::Check;
use super::fence::{tool_name, write_target, Fence, Verdict, PUBLISH_REFUSAL, WRITE_TOOLS};
use super::gate::{self, Saved};
use super::judge::{BlockOutcome, Judge, ShellAsk, TriageLane};
use super::lane_map;
use super::oracle::Fallback;
use super::prompts;
use super::record::*;
use super::review;
use super::scheduler::{self, Action, Tick, MAX_FIX_PASSES};
use crate::acp::{LanePolicy, PolicyAnswer};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::mpsc::{Receiver, RecvTimeoutError, Sender};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, Weak};
use std::time::Duration;

pub trait LaneHost: Send + Sync {
    /// Start lane `lane`'s session. Its lifecycle comes back as `Ev`s on
    /// `ctl.tx`; `Ready` always arrives through the channel, never by a call.
    fn start(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, k: u16, policy: Arc<dyn LanePolicy>) -> Result<(), String>;
    fn prompt(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, k: u16, text: &str) -> Result<(), String>;
    fn cancel(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, k: u16);
    fn stop(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, k: u16);
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Ev {
    Ready { lane: String, session: String },
    Wire { lane: String },
    Usage { lane: String, usd: f64 },
    TurnEnd { lane: String, stop: Option<String>, error: bool },
    Died { lane: String, reason: String },
    Kick,
    Stop,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
enum PromptKind { Work, Fix }

pub type Publish = Arc<dyn Fn(&Path, &RunRecord) + Send + Sync>;

fn now_ms() -> u64 { crate::epoch_ms() }
fn lk<T>(m: &Mutex<T>) -> std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, T> { m.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()) }

pub struct CtlParts {
    pub dir: PathBuf, pub repo: PathBuf, pub scratch: PathBuf, pub rec: RunRecord,
    pub checks: Vec<Check>, pub max_lanes: usize, pub judge: Judge, pub reviewer: Fallback,
    pub host: Arc<dyn LaneHost>, pub publish: Publish,
}

pub struct RunCtl {
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    pub repo: PathBuf,
    pub n: u64,
    pub scratch: PathBuf,
    pub checks: Vec<Check>,
    pub max_lanes: usize,
    pub judge: Judge,
    pub reviewer: Fallback,
    pub tx: Sender<Ev>,
    host: Arc<dyn LaneHost>,
    publish: Publish,
    rec: Mutex<RunRecord>,
    /// one gate (or head check) at a time: one scratch copy holds one checkout
    gate_lock: Mutex<()>,
    main_tree: AtomicBool,
    stopping: AtomicBool,
    last_wire: Mutex<HashMap<String, u64>>,
    tool_writes: Mutex<HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>>,
    snapshots: Mutex<HashMap<String, String>>,
    active: Mutex<HashMap<String, HashSet<String>>>,
    pending: Mutex<HashMap<String, (PromptKind, String)>>,
    cost_base: Mutex<HashMap<String, f64>>,
    nudges: Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<String>>>,
    recent: Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<String>>>,
}

impl RunCtl {
    pub fn new(p: CtlParts) -> (Arc<RunCtl>, Receiver<Ev>) {
        let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        let ctl = Arc::new(RunCtl {
            n: p.rec.n, dir: p.dir, repo: p.repo, scratch: p.scratch, checks: p.checks, max_lanes: p.max_lanes,
            judge: p.judge, reviewer: p.reviewer, tx, host: p.host, publish: p.publish, rec: Mutex::new(p.rec),
            gate_lock: Mutex::new(()), main_tree: AtomicBool::new(false), stopping: AtomicBool::new(false),
            last_wire: Default::default(), tool_writes: Default::default(), snapshots: Default::default(), active: Default::default(),
            pending: Default::default(), cost_base: Default::default(), nudges: Default::default(), recent: Default::default(),
        });
        (ctl, rx)
    }

    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> RunRecord { lk(&self.rec).clone() }

    /// Every change to the record: applied, saved, journalled, published.
    pub fn update(&self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut RunRecord)) -> RunRecord {
        let mut g = lk(&self.rec);
        let before = g.clone();
        f(&mut g);
        g.cost = g.total_cost();
        if let Err(e) = save(&self.dir, &mut g) { eprintln!("run {}: couldn't save its record: {e}", self.n); }
        let after = g.clone();
        drop(g);
        for line in transition_lines(&before, &after) { crate::journal_append_in(&self.dir, "run", &line); }
        (self.publish)(&self.dir, &after);
        after
    }

    fn k_of(&self, lane: &str) -> u16 {
        lk(&self.rec).lane_map.as_ref().and_then(|m| m.lanes.iter().position(|l| l.id == lane)).unwrap_or(0) as u16
    }
    fn spec(&self, lane: &str) -> Option<LaneSpec> { lk(&self.rec).lane_map.as_ref()?.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == lane).cloned() }
    fn set_lane(&self, lane: &str, to: LaneState) { self.update(|r| { let _ = r.set_lane_state(lane, to); }); }

    fn tick(self: &Arc<Self>) -> bool {
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        if rec.state.terminal() { return false; }
        let wire = lk(&self.last_wire).clone();
        let t = Tick { now_ms: now_ms(), last_wire: &wire, max_lanes: self.max_lanes, main_tree: self.main_tree.load(Ordering::SeqCst) };
        for a in scheduler::next_actions(&rec, &t) { self.perform(a); }
        true
    }

    fn perform(self: &Arc<Self>, a: Action) {
        match a {
            Action::Start(id) => self.start_lane(&id),
            Action::Gate(id) => {
                self.set_lane(&id, LaneState::Gating);
                let me = self.clone();
                std::thread::spawn(move || { me.gate(&id); let _ = me.tx.send(Ev::Kick); });
            }
            Action::Stall(id) => self.fail_lane(&id, LaneState::Stalled),
            Action::Park { lane, reason } => {
                self.update(|r| {
                    let _ = r.set_lane_state(&lane, LaneState::Parked);
                    if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(&lane) { l.parked = Some(reason.clone()); }
                    r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "exhausted".into(), lane: Some(lane.clone()), item: None, reason, reading: None });
                });
            }
            Action::HeadCheck => {
                self.update(|r| { let _ = r.set_state(RunState::Gating); });
                let me = self.clone();
                std::thread::spawn(move || { me.head(); let _ = me.tx.send(Ev::Kick); });
            }
            Action::Close => {
                self.update(|r| { let _ = r.set_state(RunState::Closing); });
                let me = self.clone();
                std::thread::spawn(move || { me.close(); let _ = me.tx.send(Ev::Kick); });
            }
            Action::NeedsYou => { self.update(|r| { let _ = r.set_state(RunState::NeedsYou); }); }
        }
    }

    fn handle(self: &Arc<Self>, ev: Ev) {
        match ev {
            Ev::Wire { lane } => { lk(&self.last_wire).insert(lane, now_ms()); }
            Ev::Usage { lane, usd } => {
                let base = lk(&self.cost_base).get(&lane).copied().unwrap_or(0.0);
                lk(&self.last_wire).insert(lane.clone(), now_ms());
                self.update(|r| if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(&lane) { l.cost_usd = base + usd; });
            }
            Ev::Ready { lane, session } => self.on_ready(&lane, session),
            Ev::TurnEnd { lane, stop, error } => self.on_turn_end(&lane, stop.as_deref(), error),
            Ev::Died { lane, .. } => {
                let working = self.snapshot().lane(&lane)
                    .map(|l| matches!(l.state, LaneState::Preparing | LaneState::BuildingPrompt | LaneState::Launching | LaneState::Initialising | LaneState::Streaming | LaneState::Fixing))
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                if working && !self.stopping.load(Ordering::SeqCst) { self.fail_lane(&lane, LaneState::Failed); }
            }
            Ev::Kick | Ev::Stop => {}
        }
    }

    fn start_lane(self: &Arc<Self>, id: &str) {
        let k = self.k_of(id);
        let retry = self.snapshot().lane(id).map(|l| l.state.between_tries()).unwrap_or(false);
        if retry { self.host.stop(self, id, k); }
        self.update(|r| {
            if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(id) {
                l.attempt = if retry { l.attempt + 1 } else { l.attempt.max(1) };
                l.retry_at_ms = None;
                l.fixes = 0;
            }
            let _ = r.set_lane_state(id, LaneState::Preparing);
        });
        let policy: Arc<dyn LanePolicy> = Arc::new(RunPolicy::new(self, id));
        self.set_lane(id, LaneState::BuildingPrompt);
        let text = self.lane_prompt(id);
        lk(&self.pending).insert(id.to_string(), (PromptKind::Work, text));
        self.set_lane(id, LaneState::Launching);
        lk(&self.last_wire).insert(id.to_string(), now_ms());
        match self.host.start(self, id, k, policy) {
            Ok(()) => self.set_lane(id, LaneState::Initialising),
            Err(_) => self.fail_lane(id, LaneState::Failed),
        }
    }

    fn lane_prompt(&self, id: &str) -> String {
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        let map = rec.lane_map.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        let spec = map.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == id).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        prompts::lane_prompt(rec.round_n(), &spec, &map.items, &rec.plan_path)
    }

    fn on_ready(self: &Arc<Self>, lane: &str, session: String) {
        let cost = self.snapshot().lane(lane).map(|l| l.cost_usd).unwrap_or(0.0);
        lk(&self.cost_base).insert(lane.to_string(), cost);
        self.update(|r| if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(lane) { l.session = Some(session.clone()); });
        let next = lk(&self.pending).remove(lane);
        if let Some((kind, text)) = next { self.send_turn(lane, kind, &text); }
    }

    /// Snapshot the tree, note who else is working, then prompt the lane.
    fn send_turn(self: &Arc<Self>, lane: &str, kind: PromptKind, text: &str) {
        if let Ok(tree) = crate::acp::checkpoint::snapshot_tree_as(&self.repo, &format!("run-{}-{lane}", self.n)) {
            lk(&self.snapshots).insert(lane.to_string(), tree);
        }
        let others: HashSet<String> = self.snapshot().lanes.iter().filter(|l| l.id != lane && l.state.busy()).map(|l| l.id.clone()).collect();
        {
            let mut act = lk(&self.active);
            for o in &others { act.entry(o.clone()).or_default().insert(lane.to_string()); }
            act.insert(lane.to_string(), others);
        }
        lk(&self.last_wire).insert(lane.to_string(), now_ms());
        self.set_lane(lane, if kind == PromptKind::Fix { LaneState::Fixing } else { LaneState::Streaming });
        if self.host.prompt(self, lane, self.k_of(lane), text).is_err() { self.fail_lane(lane, LaneState::Failed); }
    }

    fn on_turn_end(self: &Arc<Self>, lane: &str, stop: Option<&str>, error: bool) {
        if self.stopping.load(Ordering::SeqCst) { return; }
        let st = self.snapshot().lane(lane).map(|l| l.state);
        if !matches!(st, Some(LaneState::Streaming | LaneState::Fixing)) { return; }
        if error || stop == Some("cancelled") { return self.fail_lane(lane, LaneState::Failed); }
        self.set_lane(lane, LaneState::Finishing);
        let nudges = lk(&self.nudges).remove(lane).unwrap_or_default();
        if !nudges.is_empty() { self.send_turn(lane, PromptKind::Work, &prompts::nudge_prompt(&nudges)); }
    }

    fn fail_lane(self: &Arc<Self>, lane: &str, st: LaneState) {
        self.host.stop(self, lane, self.k_of(lane));
        lk(&self.pending).remove(lane);
        self.update(|r| if r.set_lane_state(lane, st).is_ok() {
            if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(lane) { l.retry_at_ms = Some(now_ms() + scheduler::backoff_ms(l.attempt)); }
        });
    }

    fn gate(self: &Arc<Self>, id: &str) {
        let _g = lk(&self.gate_lock);
        if self.stopping.load(Ordering::SeqCst) { return; }
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        let Some(map) = rec.lane_map.clone() else { return };
        let Some(spec) = map.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == id).cloned() else { return };
        let lane = rec.lane(id).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| LaneRecord::new(id));
        // 1 · what the turn changed: the lane's own files, and leaks
        let from = lk(&self.snapshots).get(id).cloned();
        let to = crate::acp::checkpoint::snapshot_tree_as(&self.repo, &format!("run-{}-{id}", self.n)).ok();
        let changed = match (from, to) { (Some(f), Some(t)) => gate::changed_between(&self.repo, &f, &t), _ => gate::dirty_paths(&self.repo) };
        let active: Vec<String> = lk(&self.active).get(id).map(|s| s.iter().cloned().collect()).unwrap_or_default();
        let writes = lk(&self.tool_writes).clone();
        let (owned, leaks) = gate::split_changes(&changed, id, &map, &active, &writes);
        // 2 · save the lane's own files, by explicit path
        let titles: Vec<String> = spec.items.iter().map(|i| map.items.iter().find(|x| &x.id == i).map(|x| format!("{i}: {}", x.title)).unwrap_or_else(|| i.clone())).collect();
        let message = format!("{}\n\nRound {} · lane {}: {}\n\nChronicle-Run: {}/{}", spec.title, rec.round_n(), id.to_uppercase(), spec.items.join(", "), rec.n, id);
        let head = || gate::git(&self.repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]).unwrap_or_default();
        let (commit, save_failure) = match gate::save_paths(&self.repo, &owned, &message) {
            Ok(Saved::Commit(c)) => (c, None),
            Ok(Saved::AlreadySaved(h)) => (lane.commit.clone().unwrap_or(h), None),
            Ok(Saved::Nothing) => (lane.commit.clone().unwrap_or_else(head), None),
            Err(e) if e.starts_with(gate::BUSY) => {
                // the index stayed busy: this gate runs again shortly, nothing lost
                self.update(|r| { let _ = r.set_lane_state(id, LaneState::Finishing); if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(id) { l.retry_at_ms = Some(now_ms() + 10_000); } });
                return;
            }
            Err(e) => (lane.commit.clone().unwrap_or_else(head), Some(e)),
        };
        // 3 · the checks, in the scratch copy; 4 · the review, once they pass
        let checks: Vec<Check> = self.checks.iter().filter(|c| spec.checks.contains(&c.name)).cloned().collect();
        let mut checked = gate::run_checks_at(&self.repo, &self.scratch, &commit, &checks);
        if checked.main_tree { self.main_tree.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst); }
        if let Some(e) = save_failure { checked.results.insert(0, CheckResult { name: "save".into(), exit: Some(1), ms: 0, timed_out: false, tail: e }); }
        let review = checked.results.iter().all(|c| c.exit == Some(0))
            .then(|| review::review(&self.reviewer, &titles, &gate::diff_for(&self.repo, &commit), &checked.results));
        let notice = self.reviewer.take_notice();
        let g = Gate { commit: commit.clone(), checks: checked.results, review, notice: checked.notice, fix: lane.fixes };
        let passed = g.passed();
        let fixes = lane.fixes;
        if self.stopping.load(Ordering::SeqCst) { return; }
        self.update(|r| {
            if let Some(n) = notice { r.notices.push(n); }
            for leak in &leaks {
                if let Some(owner) = leak.owner.as_ref().filter(|o| o.as_str() != id) {
                    let mirrored = Leak { path: leak.path.clone(), owner: Some(owner.clone()), by: Some(id.to_string()) };
                    if let Some(o) = r.lane_mut(owner) { if !o.leak.contains(&mirrored) { o.leak.push(mirrored); } }
                }
            }
            if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(id) {
                l.commit = Some(commit.clone());
                l.retry_at_ms = None;
                for leak in &leaks { if !l.leak.contains(leak) { l.leak.push(leak.clone()); } }
                l.gates.push(g.clone());
            }
            if passed { let _ = r.set_lane_state(id, LaneState::Closed); }
            else if fixes >= MAX_FIX_PASSES {
                let _ = r.set_lane_state(id, LaneState::Parked);
                if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(id) { l.parked = Some("Its checks still failed after two fixes.".into()); }
                r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "exhausted".into(), lane: Some(id.into()), item: None,
                    reason: format!("Lane {}’s checks still failed after two fixes.", id.to_uppercase()), reading: None });
            } else {
                if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(id) { l.fixes += 1; }
                let _ = r.set_lane_state(id, LaneState::Fixing);
            }
        });
        if passed { self.host.stop(self, id, self.k_of(id)); }
        else if fixes < MAX_FIX_PASSES { self.send_turn(id, PromptKind::Fix, &prompts::fix_prompt(fixes + 1, &g)); }
    }

    fn head(self: &Arc<Self>) {
        let _g = lk(&self.gate_lock);
        if self.stopping.load(Ordering::SeqCst) { return; }
        let head = gate::git(&self.repo, &["rev-parse", "HEAD"]).unwrap_or_default();
        let names: Vec<String> = self.checks.iter().map(|c| c.name.clone()).collect();
        self.update(|r| r.head_pending = names);
        let checked = gate::run_checks_at(&self.repo, &self.scratch, &head, &self.checks);
        if checked.main_tree { self.main_tree.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst); }
        let ok = checked.results.iter().all(|c| c.exit == Some(0));
        let reopened = self.snapshot().head_check.and_then(|h| h.reopened);
        let results = checked.results.clone();
        self.update(|r| { r.head_pending.clear(); r.head_check = Some(HeadCheck { commit: head.clone(), checks: results, reopened: reopened.clone() }); });
        if ok {
            self.update(|r| { let _ = r.set_state(RunState::Closing); });
            return self.close();
        }
        let failing = prompts::failing_text(&checked.results);
        let needs = |why: &str| { self.update(|r| {
            r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "head".into(), lane: None, item: None, reason: why.to_string(), reading: None });
            let _ = r.set_state(RunState::NeedsYou);
        }); };
        if reopened.is_some() { return needs(&failing); }
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        let map = rec.lane_map.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        let lanes: Vec<TriageLane> = map.lanes.iter().map(|s| TriageLane {
            id: s.id.clone(), title: s.title.clone(), items: s.items.clone(),
            files: rec.lane(&s.id).and_then(|l| l.commit.clone()).map(|c| gate::files_in(&self.repo, &c)).unwrap_or_default(),
        }).collect();
        let (pick, logs) = self.judge.triage(&failing, &lanes);
        let notice = self.judge.take_notice();
        self.update(|r| { r.decisions.extend(logs); if let Some(n) = notice { r.notices.push(n); } });
        let Some(lane) = pick else { return needs(&failing) };
        self.update(|r| {
            if let Some(h) = r.head_check.as_mut() { h.reopened = Some(lane.clone()); }
            if let Some(l) = r.lane_mut(&lane) { l.fixes = MAX_FIX_PASSES; } // exactly one pass
            let _ = r.set_lane_state(&lane, LaneState::Fixing);
            let _ = r.set_state(RunState::Running);
        });
        lk(&self.pending).insert(lane.clone(), (PromptKind::Fix, prompts::head_fix_prompt(&failing)));
        lk(&self.last_wire).insert(lane.clone(), now_ms());
        let policy: Arc<dyn LanePolicy> = Arc::new(RunPolicy::new(self, &lane));
        if self.host.start(self, &lane, self.k_of(&lane), policy).is_err() { needs(&failing); }
    }

    fn close(self: &Arc<Self>) {
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        let round = rec.round_n();
        if let Some(map) = &rec.lane_map { flip_notes(&self.dir, round, map, &rec); }
        let closing = gate::close_commit(&self.repo, round);
        for l in &rec.lanes { self.host.stop(self, &l.id, self.k_of(&l.id)); }
        set_round_state(&self.dir, round, "done");
        self.update(|r| {
            if let Err(e) = &closing { r.notices.push(format!("The closing save didn’t happen ({e}); each lane’s own save is in place.")); }
            let _ = r.set_state(RunState::Done);
        });
    }

    /// Stop (spec §3): every lane's turn cancelled and its session ended.
    /// Nothing is reverted; Discard is per lane, on the user's click.
    pub fn stop_all(self: &Arc<Self>) {
        self.stopping.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        if rec.state.terminal() { return; }
        for l in &rec.lanes {
            if l.state.busy() || l.state.between_tries() {
                let k = self.k_of(&l.id);
                self.host.cancel(self, &l.id, k);
                self.host.stop(self, &l.id, k);
            }
        }
        self.update(|r| {
            for l in r.lanes.iter_mut() {
                if l.state.busy() || l.state.between_tries() { l.state = LaneState::Cancelled; l.ended = Some(iso_now()); }
            }
            let _ = r.set_state(RunState::Stopped);
        });
        set_round_state(&self.dir, rec.round_n(), "failed");
    }

    /// `chronicle_run_block` (spec §3, §6 decision 2).
    pub fn block(self: &Arc<Self>, lane: &str, item: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let rec = self.snapshot();
        let map = rec.lane_map.as_ref().ok_or("This round has no lanes yet.")?;
        let spec = map.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == lane).ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no lane {lane} in this round."))?;
        if !spec.items.iter().any(|i| i == item) {
            return Err(format!("Item {item} isn't one of lane {}'s items ({}).", lane.to_uppercase(), spec.items.join(", ")));
        }
        if rec.needs_you.iter().any(|x| x.kind == "parked" && x.item.as_deref() == Some(item)) {
            return Ok(format!("{item} is already parked for the user; carry on with your other items."));
        }
        let title = map.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == item).map(|i| i.title.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let excerpt = plan_excerpt(&self.dir.join(&rec.plan_path), item);
        let (outcome, logs) = self.judge.block(lane, &format!("{item}: {title}"), reason, &excerpt, &spec.owns);
        let notice = self.judge.take_notice();
        self.update(|r| { r.decisions.extend(logs); if let Some(n) = notice { r.notices.push(n); } });
        match outcome {
            BlockOutcome::Park { reading } => {
                self.update(|r| r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "parked".into(), lane: Some(lane.into()), item: Some(item.into()), reason: reason.into(), reading }));
                Ok(format!("Accepted: leave {item} as it is and carry on with your other items. Chronicle shows it to the user."))
            }
            BlockOutcome::Retry { pushback } => Ok(format!("Not accepted yet: {pushback}")),
        }
    }

    /// `chronicle_run_ask`: the judge answers, never the user.
    pub fn ask(self: &Arc<Self>, lane: &str, question: &str) -> String {
        let spec = self.spec(lane).unwrap_or_default();
        let ctx = format!("Lane {}: {} — owns {}", lane.to_uppercase(), spec.title, spec.owns.join(", "));
        let (answer, logs) = self.judge.ask(lane, question, &ctx);
        let notice = self.judge.take_notice();
        self.update(|r| { r.decisions.extend(logs); if let Some(n) = notice { r.notices.push(n); } });
        answer
    }

    /// The lane view's composer: delivered when the lane's turn ends.
    pub fn nudge(&self, lane: &str, text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let st = self.snapshot().lane(lane).map(|l| l.state).ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no lane {lane} in this round."))?;
        if !(st.busy() || st == LaneState::Waiting || st.between_tries()) {
            return Err(format!("Lane {} has finished; there's nothing to nudge.", lane.to_uppercase()));
        }
        lk(&self.nudges).entry(lane.to_string()).or_default().push(text.to_string());
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The lane's last two tool calls (what the judge is shown), then this one.
    fn remember(&self, lane: &str, call: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut g = lk(&self.recent);
        let v = g.entry(lane.to_string()).or_default();
        let last = v.clone();
        v.push(call.to_string());
        if v.len() > 2 { v.remove(0); }
        last
    }

    fn note_write(&self, lane: &str, abs: &Path) {
        let (Some(c), Ok(root)) = (crate::acp::canonical_deep(abs), self.repo.canonicalize()) else { return };
        if let Ok(rel) = c.strip_prefix(&root) {
            lk(&self.tool_writes).entry(lane.to_string()).or_default().insert(rel.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        }
    }
}

fn plan_excerpt(path: &Path, item: &str) -> String {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap_or_default();
    let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
    let start = lines.iter().position(|l| l.contains(item)).unwrap_or(0);
    let mut s = lines[start.min(lines.len())..lines.len().min(start + 30)].join("\n");
    if s.len() > 4000 { let mut e = 4000; while !s.is_char_boundary(e) { e -= 1; } s.truncate(e); }
    s
}

/// The run loop: events first, then one scheduler pass, until the record is terminal.
pub fn spawn(ctl: Arc<RunCtl>, rx: Receiver<Ev>, first: Vec<Action>) -> std::thread::JoinHandle<()> {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        for a in first { ctl.perform(a); }
        loop {
            match rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(500)) {
                Ok(Ev::Stop) => { ctl.stop_all(); return; }
                Ok(ev) => ctl.handle(ev),
                Err(RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => {}
                Err(RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => return,
            }
            while let Ok(ev) = rx.try_recv() {
                if ev == Ev::Stop { ctl.stop_all(); return; }
                ctl.handle(ev);
            }
            if !ctl.tick() { return; }
        }
    })
}

pub fn set_round_state(dir: &Path, n: u64, state: &str) {
    let Ok(mut rounds) = crate::notes::rounds::load(dir) else { return };
    if let Some(r) = rounds.iter_mut().find(|r| r.n == n) {
        r.state = state.into();
        let _ = crate::notes::rounds::save(dir, &rounds);
    }
}

/// Closing (spec §3): Chronicle, not the agent, marks the notes done — every
/// item except one still parked or skipped by the user.
pub fn flip_notes(dir: &Path, round: u64, map: &LaneMap, rec: &RunRecord) {
    let parked: HashSet<&str> = rec.needs_you.iter().filter(|x| x.kind == "parked").filter_map(|x| x.item.as_deref()).collect();
    for item in &map.items {
        if parked.contains(item.id.as_str()) || rec.skipped.contains(&item.id) { continue; }
        if let Some(note) = &item.note { let _ = crate::notes::rounds::set_status(dir, note, Some("done"), Some(round)); }
    }
}

/// Chronicle answering a lane's asks (spec §4): the fence for writes, the git
/// guard, publish refused and shown, the judge for any other command.
pub struct RunPolicy { ctl: Weak<RunCtl>, lane: String, fence: Fence }

impl RunPolicy {
    pub fn new(ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str) -> Self {
        let map = ctl.snapshot().lane_map.unwrap_or_default();
        let owns = map.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == lane).map(|l| lane_map::compile(&l.owns)).unwrap_or_default();
        let others = map.lanes.iter().filter(|l| l.id != lane).map(|l| (l.id.clone(), lane_map::compile(&l.owns))).collect();
        RunPolicy { ctl: Arc::downgrade(ctl), lane: lane.into(), fence: Fence::Lane { repo: ctl.repo.clone(), lane: lane.into(), owns, others } }
    }
}

impl LanePolicy for RunPolicy {
    fn answer(&self, params: &Value) -> PolicyAnswer {
        let refuse = |path: String, reason: String| PolicyAnswer { allow: false, path, reason, decision: None };
        let Some(ctl) = self.ctl.upgrade() else { return refuse(String::new(), "The round is over.".into()) };
        let name = tool_name(params).unwrap_or("");
        let call = params.get("toolCall").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        let summary = format!("{name} {}", call.pointer("/rawInput/command").or_else(|| call.pointer("/rawInput/file_path")).and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or(""));
        let last = ctl.remember(&self.lane, summary.trim());
        match self.fence.verdict(params) {
            Verdict::Allow { what } => {
                let kind = call.get("kind").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                if WRITE_TOOLS.contains(&name) || matches!(kind, "edit" | "delete" | "move") { ctl.note_write(&self.lane, Path::new(write_target(&call))); }
                PolicyAnswer { allow: true, path: what, reason: String::new(), decision: None }
            }
            Verdict::Refuse { path, reason } => refuse(path, reason),
            Verdict::NotMine => refuse(String::new(), "Chronicle has no rule for that ask, so it wasn't used.".into()),
            Verdict::Publish { command } => {
                ctl.update(|r| r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "refused".into(), lane: Some(self.lane.clone()), item: None, reason: command.clone(), reading: None }));
                refuse(command, PUBLISH_REFUSAL.into())
            }
            Verdict::Judge { command } => {
                let rec = ctl.snapshot();
                let map = rec.lane_map.clone().unwrap_or_default();
                let spec = map.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == self.lane).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
                let item = spec.items.first().and_then(|i| map.items.iter().find(|x| &x.id == i)).map(|x| format!("{}: {}", x.id, x.title)).unwrap_or_default();
                let (allow, reason, logs) = ctl.judge.shell(&ShellAsk { lane: &self.lane, command: &command, owns: &spec.owns, item: &item, last_calls: &last });
                let decision = logs.first().map(|l| json!({ "choice": l.choice, "confidence": l.confidence, "model": l.model, "band": l.band }));
                let notice = ctl.judge.take_notice();
                ctl.update(|r| { r.decisions.extend(logs); if let Some(n) = notice { r.notices.push(n); } });
                PolicyAnswer { allow, path: command, reason, decision }
            }
        }
    }
    fn may_write(&self, abs: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.fence.may_write(abs)?;
        if let Some(ctl) = self.ctl.upgrade() { ctl.note_write(&self.lane, abs); }
        Ok(())
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::runs::oracle::fake::*;
    use crate::runs::runner::testkit::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn project(name: &str) -> crate::Project {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-tools-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        for p in ["src/lib", "src-tauri/src", ".chronicle/notes/Tasks"] { std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join(p)).unwrap(); }
        std::fs::write(d.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{"test":"vitest run"}}"#).unwrap();
        crate::Project::bare(&d.canonicalize().unwrap())
    }
    fn planning(p: &crate::Project, n: u64, notes: &[&str]) {
        crate::notes::rounds::save(&p.dir, &[crate::notes::rounds::Round { n, state: "generating".into(), kind: None, task_ids: vec![],
            note_paths: notes.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(), created_at: 0, plan_path: String::new(), prompt_path: String::new() }]).unwrap();
        let mut r = RunRecord::new(n, RunKind::Notes, Some(n), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md"), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md"), "");
        save(&p.dir, &mut r).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_planner_declares_a_map_and_hears_what_to_fix() {
        let p = project("declare");
        planning(&p, 3, &["Tasks/A.md"]);
        let runs = Runs::new();
        let bad = json!({ "items": [{ "id": "T-1", "title": "x" }], "lanes": [{ "id": "a", "title": "A", "items": ["T-1"], "owns": ["src/lib/**"], "checks": ["lint"] }] });
        let e = declare(&runs, &p, &bad).unwrap_err();
        assert!(e.contains("The note Tasks/A.md isn't covered") && e.contains("names the check \"lint\""), "{e}");
        let rec = load(&p.dir, 3).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(rec.lane_map.is_none());
        assert!(rec.planner_error.unwrap().contains("isn't covered"), "kept for the retry prompt");
        let good = json!({ "items": [{ "id": "T-1", "title": "x", "note": "Tasks/A.md" }, { "id": "T-2", "title": "y", "note": "Tasks/A.md" }],
            "lanes": [{ "id": "a", "title": "A", "items": ["T-1"], "owns": ["src/lib/"], "checks": ["test"] },
                      { "id": "b", "title": "B", "items": ["T-2"], "owns": ["src-tauri/src/**"], "depends_on": ["a"] }] });
        let (summary, _) = declare(&runs, &p, &good).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(summary, "Lane map accepted: 2 items in 2 lanes.");
        let rec = load(&p.dir, 3).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rec.lane_map.unwrap().lanes[0].owns, vec!["src/lib/**"]);
        assert_eq!(rec.lanes.iter().map(|l| (l.id.as_str(), l.state)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec![("a", LaneState::Waiting), ("b", LaneState::Waiting)]);
        assert!(declare(&runs, &p, &good).unwrap_err().contains("already accepted"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn declare_with_no_round_being_planned_says_so() {
        let p = project("none");
        assert!(declare(&Runs::new(), &p, &json!({ "items": [], "lanes": [] })).unwrap_err().contains("No round is being planned"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn scopes_come_from_the_mcp_flags_and_lanes_carry_their_identity() {
        let a = |xs: &[&str]| xs.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(matches!(RunScope::from_args(&a(&[".", "--planner"])), Some(RunScope::Planner)));
        assert!(matches!(RunScope::from_args(&a(&[".", "--run", "10", "--lane", "c"])), Some(RunScope::Lane { n: 10, ref lane }) if lane == "c"));
        assert!(RunScope::from_args(&a(&["."])).is_none());
        assert!(RunScope::from_args(&a(&[".", "--run", "x", "--lane", "c"])).is_none());
        let s = RunScope::Lane { n: 10, lane: "c".into() };
        assert_eq!(scoped_args(Some(&s), &json!({ "item": "T-1" })), json!({ "item": "T-1", "_run": 10, "_lane": "c" }));
        assert_eq!(scoped_args(Some(&s), &json!({ "_lane": "a" }))["_lane"], "c", "a lane can't speak for another");
        let srv = lane_server(Path::new("/p"), Path::new("/x/chronicle"), 10, "c");
        assert_eq!(srv["name"], "chronicle-run");
        assert_eq!(srv["args"], json!(["--mcp", "/p", "--run", "10", "--lane", "c"]));
        assert_eq!(planner_server(Path::new("/p"), Path::new("/x/chronicle"))["args"], json!(["--mcp", "/p", "--planner"]));
    }

    #[test]
    fn block_ask_and_state_reach_the_live_run() {
        let r = repo("tools-live", 20);
        let k = kit(&r, two_lanes(20, &[], &[]), vec![], FakeHost::new(Box::new(|_, _| vec![])));
        k.decide.decide_with(decision(vec![noul("impossible_as_written", 0.9)], "jev-1.13.0", false));
        let runs = Runs::new();
        runs.insert(k.ctl.clone());
        let p = crate::Project::bare(&r);
        let rep = handle(&runs, &p, "run.block", &json!({ "_run": 20, "_lane": "a", "item": "I-1", "reason": "the file doesn't exist" }));
        assert!(rep.ok && rep.summary.starts_with("Accepted"), "{rep:?}");
        let st = handle(&runs, &p, "run.state", &json!({ "_run": 20, "_lane": "b" }));
        assert!(st.ok);
        assert_eq!(st.data.unwrap()["lane"]["owns"], json!(["b/**"]));
        let no = handle(&runs, &p, "run.block", &json!({ "_run": 99, "_lane": "a", "item": "I-1", "reason": "x" }));
        assert!(!no.ok && no.summary.contains("isn't running"));
        let missing = handle(&runs, &p, "run.ask", &json!({ "_run": 20, "_lane": "a" }));
        assert!(!missing.ok && missing.summary.contains("question is required"));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The registry**

Append to `src-tauri/src/runs/mod.rs`:

```rust
pub mod runner;
pub mod tools;

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

/// The live runs, and how a record reaches the frontend (`run-update`).
pub struct Runs {
    ctls: Mutex<HashMap<(PathBuf, u64), Arc<runner::RunCtl>>>,
    emit: Mutex<Option<runner::Publish>>,
}

impl Runs {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Runs { ctls: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()), emit: Mutex::new(None) } }
    pub fn set_emit(&self, f: runner::Publish) { if let Ok(mut g) = self.emit.lock() { *g = Some(f); } }
    pub fn publish(&self, dir: &Path, rec: &record::RunRecord) {
        if let Some(f) = self.emit.lock().ok().and_then(|g| g.clone()) { f(dir, rec); }
    }
    /// What a controller publishes through (the emitter set at app start).
    pub fn publisher(&self) -> runner::Publish {
        let f = self.emit.lock().ok().and_then(|g| g.clone());
        Arc::new(move |d: &Path, r: &record::RunRecord| if let Some(f) = &f { f(d, r) })
    }
    pub fn insert(&self, ctl: Arc<runner::RunCtl>) {
        if let Ok(mut g) = self.ctls.lock() {
            g.retain(|_, c| !c.snapshot().state.terminal());
            g.insert((ctl.dir.clone(), ctl.n), ctl);
        }
    }
    pub fn get(&self, dir: &Path, n: u64) -> Option<Arc<runner::RunCtl>> {
        self.ctls.lock().ok()?.get(&(dir.to_path_buf(), n)).cloned()
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The tools**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/tools.rs`:

```rust
//! The run tools a planner and its lanes call (spec §5a's list, trimmed for
//! the amended engine: gating and closing are Rust-internal now). They reach
//! the app over the bridge socket and are answered here, in Rust — never by
//! the frontend — so the record keeps one writer.
use super::checks::repo_checks;
use super::lane_map::validate;
use super::record::*;
use super::Runs;
use crate::bridge::Reply;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::path::Path;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum RunScope { Planner, Lane { n: u64, lane: String } }

impl RunScope {
    /// `chronicle --mcp <dir> --planner` or `--run <n> --lane <id>`.
    pub fn from_args(args: &[String]) -> Option<RunScope> {
        if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--planner") { return Some(RunScope::Planner); }
        let after = |flag: &str| args.iter().position(|a| a == flag).and_then(|i| args.get(i + 1)).cloned();
        let n = after("--run")?.parse().ok()?;
        let lane = after("--lane").filter(|l| l.len() == 1 && l.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase()))?;
        Some(RunScope::Lane { n, lane })
    }
}

/// A lane's calls carry its run and lane, set by its own server — never by the agent.
pub fn scoped_args(scope: Option<&RunScope>, args: &Value) -> Value {
    let mut a = if args.is_object() { args.clone() } else { json!({}) };
    if let Some(RunScope::Lane { n, lane }) = scope {
        a["_run"] = json!(n);
        a["_lane"] = json!(lane);
    }
    a
}

fn server(dir: &Path, exe: &Path, extra: &[&str]) -> Value {
    let mut args = vec!["--mcp".to_string(), dir.to_string_lossy().into_owned()];
    args.extend(extra.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()));
    let mut env = vec![json!({ "name": "PATH", "value": crate::setup::tool_env_path() })];
    // the bridge's test seam travels to the server too (the gated test, Task 17)
    for k in ["CHRONICLE_BRIDGE_SOCKET", "CHRONICLE_BRIDGE_TOKEN"] {
        if let Ok(v) = std::env::var(k) { env.push(json!({ "name": k, "value": v })); }
    }
    json!({ "name": super::fence::RUN_SERVER, "command": exe.to_string_lossy(), "args": args, "env": env })
}
pub fn planner_server(dir: &Path, exe: &Path) -> Value { server(dir, exe, &["--planner"]) }
pub fn lane_server(dir: &Path, exe: &Path, n: u64, lane: &str) -> Value { server(dir, exe, &["--run", &n.to_string(), "--lane", lane]) }

pub fn handle(runs: &Runs, p: &crate::Project, action: &str, args: &Value) -> Reply {
    let r = match action {
        "run.declare" => declare(runs, p, args),
        "run.block" => lane_call(runs, p, args).and_then(|(ctl, lane)| {
            let item = args.get("item").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()).ok_or("item is required.")?;
            let reason = args.get("reason").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()).ok_or("reason is required.")?;
            ctl.block(&lane, item, reason).map(|s| (s, json!({})))
        }),
        "run.ask" => lane_call(runs, p, args).and_then(|(ctl, lane)| {
            let q = args.get("question").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()).ok_or("question is required.")?;
            Ok((ctl.ask(&lane, q), json!({})))
        }),
        "run.state" => state(runs, p, args),
        other => Err(format!("No action named {other}.")),
    };
    match r {
        Ok((summary, data)) => Reply { ok: true, summary, data: Some(data) },
        Err(summary) => Reply { ok: false, summary, data: None },
    }
}

fn lane_call(runs: &Runs, p: &crate::Project, args: &Value) -> Result<(std::sync::Arc<super::runner::RunCtl>, String), String> {
    let n = args.get("_run").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()).ok_or("This tool is for a round's lanes.")?;
    let lane = args.get("_lane").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).ok_or("This tool is for a round's lanes.")?.to_string();
    let ctl = runs.get(&p.dir, n).filter(|c| !c.snapshot().state.terminal()).ok_or_else(|| format!("Round {n} isn't running."))?;
    Ok((ctl, lane))
}

/// `chronicle_run_declare`: validate and store the planner's lane map. Every
/// reason goes back at once; the last ones are kept for the retry prompt.
pub fn declare(runs: &Runs, p: &crate::Project, args: &Value) -> Result<(String, Value), String> {
    let mut rec = list(&p.dir).into_iter().rev().find(|r| r.state == RunState::Planning)
        .ok_or("No round is being planned right now, so there's no lane map to declare.")?;
    if rec.lane_map.is_some() { return Err("This round's lane map is already accepted. End your turn.".into()); }
    let map: LaneMap = serde_json::from_value(args.clone())
        .map_err(|e| format!("The map isn't in the expected shape ({e}). Send {{\"items\": [...], \"lanes\": [...]}}."))?;
    let checks = repo_checks(&p.repo, p.manifest.as_ref());
    let notes: Vec<String> = if rec.kind == RunKind::Notes {
        crate::notes::rounds::load(&p.dir).unwrap_or_default().into_iter().find(|r| r.n == rec.round_n()).map(|r| r.note_paths).unwrap_or_default()
    } else { vec![] };
    match validate(&map, &p.repo, &checks, &notes) {
        Ok(ok) => {
            let (items, lanes) = (ok.items.len(), ok.lanes.len());
            rec.lanes = ok.lanes.iter().map(|l| LaneRecord::new(&l.id)).collect();
            rec.lane_map = Some(ok);
            rec.planner_error = None;
            save(&p.dir, &mut rec)?;
            runs.publish(&p.dir, &rec);
            Ok((format!("Lane map accepted: {items} items in {lanes} lanes."), json!({})))
        }
        Err(reasons) => {
            let text = format!("- {}", reasons.join("\n- "));
            rec.planner_error = Some(text.clone());
            save(&p.dir, &mut rec)?;
            runs.publish(&p.dir, &rec);
            Err(format!("The lane map isn't valid yet:\n{text}"))
        }
    }
}

/// `chronicle_run_state`: the asking lane's own spec, and which lanes have
/// closed — enough for a lane that resumes to see where it stands.
fn state(runs: &Runs, p: &crate::Project, args: &Value) -> Result<(String, Value), String> {
    let rec = match args.get("_run").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()) {
        Some(n) => runs.get(&p.dir, n).map(|c| c.snapshot()).or_else(|| load(&p.dir, n).ok().flatten()),
        None => list(&p.dir).into_iter().rev().find(|r| !r.state.terminal()),
    }.ok_or("No round is running.")?;
    let lane = args.get("_lane").and_then(|v| v.as_str())
        .and_then(|id| rec.lane_map.as_ref()?.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == id).cloned());
    let closed: Vec<&str> = rec.lanes.iter().filter(|l| l.state == LaneState::Closed).map(|l| l.id.as_str()).collect();
    Ok((format!("Round {} is {:?}; {} of {} lanes finished.", rec.round_n(), rec.state, closed.len(), rec.lanes.len()),
        json!({ "round": rec.round_n(), "state": rec.state, "lane": lane, "closed": closed })))
}
```

In `src-tauri/src/mcp.rs`:

```rust
pub(crate) struct Session { dir: PathBuf, initialized: bool, designs_only: bool, scope: Option<crate::runs::tools::RunScope> }
impl Session {
    pub(crate) fn new(dir: PathBuf) -> Self { Self { dir, initialized: false, designs_only: false, scope: None } }
    /// The design session's server (`--designs-only`): only `chronicle.designs.*` is visible or callable.
    pub(crate) fn designs_only(mut self, on: bool) -> Self { self.designs_only = on; self }
    /// A run's server (`--planner`, or `--run <n> --lane <id>`): only its run tools.
    pub(crate) fn with_scope(mut self, scope: Option<crate::runs::tools::RunScope>) -> Self { self.scope = scope; self }
    fn visible(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        use crate::runs::tools::RunScope;
        match &self.scope {
            Some(RunScope::Planner) => matches!(name, "chronicle.run.declare" | "chronicle.run.state"),
            Some(RunScope::Lane { .. }) => matches!(name, "chronicle.run.block" | "chronicle.run.ask" | "chronicle.run.state"),
            None => !name.starts_with("chronicle.run.")
                && (!self.designs_only || ["list", "read", "capture", "lint"].iter().any(|v| name == format!("chronicle.designs.{v}"))),
        }
    }
}
```

- Test: `#[cfg(test)]` in `host.rs` and `commands.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; from Plan C (assumed names, header table): `crate::agents::project_profile`, `crate::setup::agent_launch`, the `profile` argument of `acp::start`; `oracle::for_project`; `crate::{round_plan_begin_in, round_plan_cancel_in, FIXES_PROMPT_HEAD, project_for, load_project, OpenRoots, Project}`.
- Produces:
  - `host::lane_event(lane: &str, v: &Value) -> Ev`; `host::AcpHost { pub app: tauri::AppHandle }` implementing `LaneHost`; `host::RunsScreen { pub dir: PathBuf, pub record: Arc<dyn Fn(&Path, NeedsYou) -> bool + Send + Sync> }` implementing `AskScreen`; `host::RunsScreen::for_app(app: &AppHandle, dir: &Path) -> RunsScreen`
  - `commands::Source` (serde, tagged by `kind`: `{ "kind": "notes" }` | `{ "kind": "plan", "path": … }` | `{ "kind": "prompt", "text": … }`)
  - `commands::NeedsAct` (serde, tagged by `kind`: `skip { item }` | `rewrite { item, text }` | `retry { lane }` | `recheck` | `dismiss { index }`)
  - `commands::begin_in(p: &Project, source: &Source, judge: Option<&Judge>) -> Result<Value, String>` → `{ n, total, prompt }`
  - `commands::enum Settle { Launch(RunRecord), Retry(String), Failed(String) }`; `settle_in(p, n, planner_cost: f64) -> Result<Settle, String>`
  - `commands::cancel_planning_in(p, n)`, `recover_in(dir, live: &dyn Fn(u64) -> bool) -> Vec<RunRecord>`, `apply_needs_you(dir, &mut RunRecord, &NeedsAct) -> Result<(), String>`, `planner_prompt_for(p, &RunRecord) -> String`, `sizing_table(items: &[(String, String)]) -> String`
  - Tauri commands (frontend names): `run_begin(dir, source)`, `run_plan_settle(dir, n, plannerCost)`, `run_plan_cancel(dir, n)`, `runs_list(dir)`, `run_stop(dir, n)`, `run_resume(dir, n)`, `run_needs_you(dir, n, act)`, `run_lane_nudge(dir, n, lane, text)`, `run_lane_discard(dir, n, lane)`
  - the `run-update` event: `{ dir: string, record: RunRecord }`

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    fn wire(method: &str, params: Value) -> Value { json!({ "dir": "/p", "lane": "w/10-0", "message": { "method": method, "params": params } }) }

    #[test]
    fn a_lane_sessions_events_become_the_controllers() {
        assert_eq!(lane_event("a", &wire("_chronicle/session_state", json!({ "state": "ready", "sessionId": "s1" }))), Ev::Ready { lane: "a".into(), session: "s1".into() });
        assert!(matches!(lane_event("a", &wire("_chronicle/session_state", json!({ "state": "error", "message": "boom" }))), Ev::Died { ref reason, .. } if reason == "boom"));
        assert!(matches!(lane_event("a", &wire("_chronicle/session_state", json!({ "state": "needs-login" }))), Ev::Died { .. }));
        assert_eq!(lane_event("a", &wire("_chronicle/session_state", json!({ "state": "starting" }))), Ev::Wire { lane: "a".into() });
        assert_eq!(lane_event("a", &wire("_chronicle/turn_end", json!({ "stopReason": "end_turn", "usage": null }))), Ev::TurnEnd { lane: "a".into(), stop: Some("end_turn".into()), error: false });
        assert_eq!(lane_event("a", &wire("_chronicle/turn_end", json!({ "error": { "message": "x" } }))), Ev::TurnEnd { lane: "a".into(), stop: None, error: true });
        assert_eq!(lane_event("a", &wire("session/update", json!({ "update": { "sessionUpdate": "usage_update", "cost": { "amount": 0.42 } } }))), Ev::Usage { lane: "a".into(), usd: 0.42 });
        assert_eq!(lane_event("a", &wire("session/update", json!({ "update": { "sessionUpdate": "agent_message_chunk" } }))), Ev::Wire { lane: "a".into() });
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_chat_screen_refuses_a_publish_only_while_a_round_runs() {
        let taken: Arc<Mutex<Vec<NeedsYou>>> = Default::default();
        let sink = taken.clone();
        let live = Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(true));
        let l2 = live.clone();
        let screen = RunsScreen { dir: PathBuf::from("/p"), record: Arc::new(move |_d: &Path, row: NeedsYou| {
            if !l2.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst) { return false; }
            sink.lock().unwrap().push(row); true
        }) };
        let ask = |c: &str| json!({ "toolCall": { "toolCallId": "t", "name": "Bash", "rawInput": { "command": c } } });
        let a = screen.screen(&ask("git push origin main")).expect("refused during a round");
        assert!(!a.allow);
        assert_eq!(a.reason, crate::runs::fence::PUBLISH_REFUSAL);
        assert_eq!(taken.lock().unwrap()[0].kind, "refused");
        assert!(screen.screen(&ask("npm test")).is_none(), "only publish verbs are screened; the user answers the rest");
        live.store(false, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
        assert!(screen.screen(&ask("git push")).is_none(), "outside a round the chat lane behaves as today");
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn project(name: &str) -> crate::Project {
        let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-cmds-{}-{}", name, std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
        for p in ["src/lib", ".chronicle/notes/Tasks", "fixes"] { std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join(p)).unwrap(); }
        std::fs::write(d.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{"test":"vitest run","build":"vite build"}}"#).unwrap();
        crate::Project::bare(&d.canonicalize().unwrap())
    }
    fn note(p: &crate::Project, rel: &str, body: &str) {
        std::fs::write(p.dir.join(".chronicle/notes").join(rel), format!("---\nstatus: queued\n---\n\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
    }
    fn accept(p: &crate::Project, n: u64, map: LaneMap) {
        let mut r = load(&p.dir, n).unwrap().unwrap();
        r.lanes = map.lanes.iter().map(|l| LaneRecord::new(&l.id)).collect();
        r.lane_map = Some(map);
        save(&p.dir, &mut r).unwrap();
    }
    fn one_lane(note: Option<&str>) -> LaneMap {
        LaneMap { items: vec![Item { id: "T-1".into(), title: "x".into(), note: note.map(String::from), rewrite: None }],
                  lanes: vec![LaneSpec { id: "a".into(), title: "A".into(), items: vec!["T-1".into()], owns: vec!["src/lib/**".into()], depends_on: vec![], checks: vec![] }] }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_run_from_notes_freezes_them_into_a_round_and_prompts_the_planner() {
        let p = project("notes");
        note(&p, "Tasks/A.md", "Fix src/lib/a.ts");
        note(&p, "Tasks/B.md", "Fix src/lib/a.ts and src/lib/b.ts");
        let out = begin_in(&p, &Source::Notes, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((out["n"].as_u64(), out["total"].as_u64()), (Some(1), Some(2)));
        let prompt = out["prompt"].as_str().unwrap();
        assert!(prompt.starts_with("You are turning a queue of user-written notes"));
        assert!(prompt.contains("chronicle_run_declare") && prompt.contains("aim for 2 lanes") && prompt.contains("test, build"));
        let rec = load(&p.dir, 1).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((rec.kind, rec.state, rec.round), (RunKind::Notes, RunState::Planning, Some(1)));
        assert_eq!(rec.target_lanes, Some(2));
        assert_eq!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&p.dir).unwrap()[0].state, "generating");
        assert!(begin_in(&p, &Source::Notes, None).unwrap_err().contains("already running"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_run_from_a_plan_or_a_prompt_gets_its_own_round() {
        let p = project("plan");
        std::fs::write(p.dir.join("fixes/phase_4_fixes_plan.md"), "Round kind: bug fixes\n\n- I-1 do a\n- I-2 do b\n").unwrap();
        let out = begin_in(&p, &Source::Plan { path: "fixes/phase_4_fixes_plan.md".into() }, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(out["n"], 4);
        assert!(out["prompt"].as_str().unwrap().contains("Do not change either file"));
        assert!(begin_in(&p, &Source::Plan { path: "README.md".into() }, None).is_err());
        let mut r = load(&p.dir, 4).unwrap().unwrap();
        r.state = RunState::Done;
        save(&p.dir, &mut r).unwrap();
        let out2 = begin_in(&p, &Source::Prompt { text: "Make the pane faster".into() }, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(out2["n"], 5);
        assert!(out2["prompt"].as_str().unwrap().contains("Make the pane faster"));
        assert_eq!(load(&p.dir, 5).unwrap().unwrap().target_lanes, Some(1), "one item: one lane, still gated");
        assert!(begin_in(&p, &Source::Prompt { text: "  ".into() }, None).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn settling_launches_retries_twice_then_fails_and_requeues_the_notes() {
        let p = project("settle");
        note(&p, "Tasks/A.md", "x");
        begin_in(&p, &Source::Notes, None).unwrap();
        for _ in 0..2 { assert!(matches!(settle_in(&p, 1, 0.1).unwrap(), Settle::Retry(ref s) if s.contains("chronicle_run_declare"))); }
        match settle_in(&p, 1, 0.1).unwrap() { Settle::Failed(why) => assert!(why.contains("lane map")), _ => panic!("the third miss fails the run") }
        let rec = load(&p.dir, 1).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rec.state, RunState::Failed);
        assert!((rec.planner_cost_usd - 0.3).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert_eq!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&p.dir).unwrap()[0].state, "failed");
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(p.dir.join(".chronicle/notes/Tasks/A.md")).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("status: queued") && !text.contains("round:"), "{text}");

        let q = project("settle-ok");
        note(&q, "Tasks/A.md", "x");
        begin_in(&q, &Source::Notes, None).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(q.dir.join("fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md"), "Round kind: bug fixes\n").unwrap();
        accept(&q, 1, one_lane(Some("Tasks/A.md")));
        match settle_in(&q, 1, 0.26).unwrap() { Settle::Launch(r) => assert_eq!(r.state, RunState::Running), _ => panic!("an accepted map launches") }
        assert_eq!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&q.dir).unwrap()[0].state, "ready");
    }

    #[test]
    fn stopping_a_plan_puts_the_notes_back() {
        let p = project("cancel");
        note(&p, "Tasks/A.md", "x");
        begin_in(&p, &Source::Notes, None).unwrap();
        cancel_planning_in(&p, 1).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(load(&p.dir, 1).unwrap().unwrap().state, RunState::Stopped);
        assert!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&p.dir).unwrap().is_empty());
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(p.dir.join(".chronicle/notes/Tasks/A.md")).unwrap().contains("status: queued"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_run_nobody_is_driving_reads_as_interrupted() {
        let p = project("recover");
        let mut r = RunRecord::new(7, RunKind::Prompt, Some(7), "p", "q", "x");
        r.state = RunState::Gating;
        save(&p.dir, &mut r).unwrap();
        let out = recover_in(&p.dir, &|_| false);
        assert_eq!(out[0].state, RunState::Interrupted);
        assert_eq!(out[0].interrupted_from, Some(RunState::Gating));
        assert_eq!(recover_in(&p.dir, &|_| true)[0].state, RunState::Interrupted, "an interrupted run stays so until Resume");
    }

    #[test]
    fn needs_you_actions_skip_rewrite_retry_recheck_and_dismiss() {
        let p = project("needs");
        note(&p, "Tasks/A.md", "x");
        crate::notes::rounds::save(&p.dir, &[crate::notes::rounds::Round { n: 2, state: "ready".into(), kind: None, task_ids: vec![],
            note_paths: vec!["Tasks/A.md".into()], created_at: 0, plan_path: String::new(), prompt_path: String::new() }]).unwrap();
        let mut r = RunRecord::new(2, RunKind::Notes, Some(2), "p", "q", "");
        r.lane_map = Some(one_lane(Some("Tasks/A.md")));
        r.lanes = vec![LaneRecord::new("a")];
        r.lanes[0].state = LaneState::Closed;
        r.state = RunState::NeedsYou;
        r.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "parked".into(), lane: Some("a".into()), item: Some("T-1".into()), reason: "no".into(), reading: Some(0.86) });
        let mut skip = r.clone();
        apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut skip, &NeedsAct::Skip { item: "T-1".into() }).unwrap();
        assert!(skip.needs_you.is_empty() && skip.skipped == vec!["T-1"]);
        assert_eq!(skip.state, RunState::Running, "nothing holds the run any more");
        assert!(crate::notes::rounds::load(&p.dir).unwrap()[0].note_paths.is_empty(), "the skipped note leaves the round and unlocks");
        let mut rw = r.clone();
        apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut rw, &NeedsAct::Rewrite { item: "T-1".into(), text: "Do it from the pane".into() }).unwrap();
        let map = rw.lane_map.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(map.lanes[1].id, "b");
        assert_eq!(map.lanes[1].owns, vec!["src/lib/**"]);
        assert_eq!(map.lanes[1].depends_on, vec!["a"]);
        assert_eq!(map.items[0].rewrite.as_deref(), Some("Do it from the pane"));
        assert_eq!(rw.lanes[1].state, LaneState::Waiting);
        assert!(apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut r.clone(), &NeedsAct::Rewrite { item: "T-1".into(), text: " ".into() }).is_err());
        let mut ex = r.clone();
        ex.needs_you = vec![NeedsYou { kind: "exhausted".into(), lane: Some("a".into()), item: None, reason: "three tries".into(), reading: None }];
        ex.lanes[0].state = LaneState::Parked;
        ex.lanes[0].attempt = 3;
        apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut ex, &NeedsAct::Retry { lane: "a".into() }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((ex.lanes[0].state, ex.lanes[0].attempt), (LaneState::Waiting, 0));
        let mut hd = r.clone();
        hd.needs_you = vec![NeedsYou { kind: "head".into(), lane: None, item: None, reason: "boom".into(), reading: None }];
        hd.head_check = Some(HeadCheck::default());
        apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut hd, &NeedsAct::Recheck).unwrap();
        assert!(hd.head_check.is_none() && hd.state == RunState::Running);
        let mut rf = r.clone();
        rf.needs_you.push(NeedsYou { kind: "refused".into(), lane: Some("a".into()), item: None, reason: "git push".into(), reading: None });
        assert!(apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut rf.clone(), &NeedsAct::Dismiss { index: 0 }).is_err(), "only a refused row can be dismissed");
        apply_needs_you(&p.dir, &mut rf, &NeedsAct::Dismiss { index: 1 }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rf.needs_you.len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_sizing_table_names_files_and_the_items_that_share_them() {
        let t = sizing_table(&[("T-1".into(), "Fix src/lib/a.ts".into()), ("T-2".into(), "Touch src/lib/a.ts and src/index.css".into()), ("T-3".into(), "Docs only".into())]);
        assert!(t.contains("T-1: src/lib/a.ts"));
        assert!(t.contains("T-2: src/index.css, src/lib/a.ts"));
        assert!(t.contains("T-3: (no files named)"));
        assert!(t.contains("T-1 and T-2 share src/lib/a.ts"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn sources_and_actions_read_as_the_frontend_sends_them() {
        assert!(matches!(serde_json::from_value::<Source>(json!({ "kind": "notes" })).unwrap(), Source::Notes));
        assert!(matches!(serde_json::from_value::<Source>(json!({ "kind": "plan", "path": "fixes/phase_4_fixes_plan.md" })).unwrap(), Source::Plan { .. }));
        assert!(matches!(serde_json::from_value::<NeedsAct>(json!({ "kind": "rewrite", "item": "T-1", "text": "x" })).unwrap(), NeedsAct::Rewrite { .. }));
        assert!(matches!(serde_json::from_value::<NeedsAct>(json!({ "kind": "recheck" })).unwrap(), NeedsAct::Recheck));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The host and the screen**

Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/host.rs`:

```rust
//! The real `LaneHost`: each lane is an ACP session (`Lane::Worker`) on the
//! project's agent profile (agents spec §3, §12.7), carrying the lane's run
//! server and Chronicle's policy. Its acp-updates go to the pane as usual and
//! to the controller as `Ev`s. Plus the chat lane's screen: during a run, a
//! push, tag, publish or release from the chat session is refused and shown.
use super::fence::{classify, Shell, PUBLISH_REFUSAL};
use super::record::{list, save, NeedsYou};
use super::runner::{Ev, LaneHost, RunCtl};
use super::{tools, Runs};
use crate::acp::{self, AskScreen, LanePolicy, PolicyAnswer};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tauri::{Emitter, Manager};

pub fn lane_event(lane: &str, v: &Value) -> Ev {
    let m = &v["message"];
    let l = || lane.to_string();
    match m["method"].as_str().unwrap_or("") {
        "_chronicle/session_state" => match m["params"]["state"].as_str().unwrap_or("") {
            "ready" => Ev::Ready { lane: l(), session: m["params"]["sessionId"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string() },
            s @ ("error" | "ended" | "needs-login") => Ev::Died { lane: l(), reason: m["params"]["message"].as_str().map(String::from).unwrap_or_else(|| format!("the session is {s}")) },
            _ => Ev::Wire { lane: l() },
        },
        "_chronicle/turn_end" => Ev::TurnEnd { lane: l(), stop: m["params"]["stopReason"].as_str().map(String::from), error: !m["params"]["error"].is_null() },
        "session/update" if m["params"]["update"]["sessionUpdate"] == "usage_update" => match m["params"]["update"]["cost"]["amount"].as_f64() {
            Some(usd) => Ev::Usage { lane: l(), usd },
            None => Ev::Wire { lane: l() },
        },
        _ => Ev::Wire { lane: l() },
    }
}

pub struct AcpHost { pub app: tauri::AppHandle }

impl AcpHost {
    fn key(ctl: &RunCtl, k: u16) -> String { acp::Lane::Worker { run: ctl.n, k }.key(&ctl.dir.to_string_lossy()) }
    fn session(&self, ctl: &RunCtl, k: u16) -> Option<Arc<acp::AcpSession>> { self.app.state::<acp::AcpState>().get(&Self::key(ctl, k)) }
}

impl LaneHost for AcpHost {
    fn start(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, k: u16, policy: Arc<dyn LanePolicy>) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(old) = self.session(ctl, k) { old.stop(false); }
        let (app, tx, id) = (self.app.clone(), ctl.tx.clone(), lane.to_string());
        let emit: acp::Emit = Arc::new(move |v: Value| {
            let _ = tx.send(lane_event(&id, &v));
            let _ = app.emit("acp-update", v);
        });
        let exe = std::env::current_exe().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let extras = acp::LaneExtras {
            mcp_servers: vec![tools::lane_server(&ctl.dir, &exe, ctl.n, lane)],
            policy: Some(acp::PolicyHandle(policy)),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        launch_worker(&self.app.state::<acp::AcpState>(), emit, Self::key(ctl, k), acp::Lane::Worker { run: ctl.n, k }, &ctl.dir, extras)
    }
    fn prompt(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, _lane: &str, k: u16, text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let s = self.session(ctl, k).ok_or("the lane's session isn't running")?;
        // the thread shows the first line ("You are lane C of round 10: …"), the agent gets it all
        s.prompt(vec![json!({ "type": "text", "text": text })], text.lines().next().unwrap_or("").to_string())
    }
    fn cancel(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, _lane: &str, k: u16) { if let Some(s) = self.session(ctl, k) { let _ = s.cancel(); } }
    fn stop(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, _lane: &str, k: u16) { if let Some(s) = self.session(ctl, k) { s.stop(false); } }
}

/// The one place a worker session meets Plan C's names (header table): the
/// project's agent profile, its launch, and `acp::start`'s profile argument.
pub(crate) fn launch_worker(agents: &acp::AcpState, emit: acp::Emit, key: String, lane: acp::Lane, dir: &Path, extras: acp::LaneExtras) -> Result<(), String> {
    let p = crate::load_project(dir);
    let profile = crate::agents::project_profile(&p.dir);
    let launch = crate::setup::agent_launch(profile.id).ok_or("the project's agent isn't installed — open Setup & health")?;
    let mut jail = vec![p.repo.clone(), p.dir.clone()];
    jail.extend(p.extras.iter().map(|(_, b)| b.clone()));
    acp::start(agents, emit, key, lane, p.repo.clone(), p.dir.clone(), jail, None, extras, None, profile, acp::adapter_command_for(&launch)).map(|_| ())
}

/// The chat lane during a run (spec §4): publish verbs are refused there too,
/// and the refusal is a needs-you row on the live run.
pub struct RunsScreen {
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    /// adds the row to the live run; false when no run is live
    pub record: Arc<dyn Fn(&Path, NeedsYou) -> bool + Send + Sync>,
}

impl RunsScreen {
    pub fn for_app(app: &tauri::AppHandle, dir: &Path) -> RunsScreen {
        let app = app.clone();
        RunsScreen { dir: dir.to_path_buf(), record: Arc::new(move |dir: &Path, row: NeedsYou| {
            let Some(mut rec) = list(dir).into_iter().rev().find(|r| !r.state.terminal()) else { return false };
            let runs = app.state::<Runs>();
            match runs.get(dir, rec.n) {
                Some(ctl) => { ctl.update(|r| r.needs_you.push(row)); }
                None => { rec.needs_you.push(row); let _ = save(dir, &mut rec); runs.publish(dir, &rec); }
            }
            true
        }) }
    }
}

impl AskScreen for RunsScreen {
    fn screen(&self, params: &Value) -> Option<PolicyAnswer> {
        let cmd = params.pointer("/toolCall/rawInput/command").and_then(|v| v.as_str())?;
        if classify(cmd) != Shell::Publish { return None; }
        let row = NeedsYou { kind: "refused".into(), lane: None, item: None, reason: cmd.to_string(), reading: None };
        if !(self.record)(&self.dir, row) { return None; }
        Some(PolicyAnswer { allow: false, path: cmd.to_string(), reason: PUBLISH_REFUSAL.into(), decision: None })
    }
}
```
