Above the tests in `src-tauri/src/runs/commands.rs`:

```rust
//! The three entry points (spec §7) and the run card's buttons, as commands.
//! Each `_in` function works on disk and is tested; the `#[tauri::command]`
//! wrappers add the project allowlist and the live controller.
use super::checks::{max_lanes, repo_checks};
use super::gate;
use super::judge::Judge;
use super::oracle;
use super::prompts;
use super::record::*;
use super::runner::{self, CtlParts, RunCtl};
use super::scheduler::{self, Action};
use super::Runs;
use crate::{OpenRoots, Project};
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tauri::State;

#[derive(Deserialize, Debug, Clone)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum Source { Notes, Plan { path: String }, Prompt { text: String } }

#[derive(Deserialize, Debug, Clone)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum NeedsAct { Skip { item: String }, Rewrite { item: String, text: String }, Retry { lane: String }, Recheck, Dismiss { index: usize } }

pub enum Settle { Launch(RunRecord), Retry(String), Failed(String) }

fn plan_number(path: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    regex::Regex::new(r"^fixes/phase_(\d+)_fixes_plan\.md$").ok()?.captures(path)?.get(1)?.as_str().parse().ok()
}

/// Items, the files each names, and the pairs that share a file — the state
/// of the sizing decision (spec §6, decision 4). Never whole files.
pub fn sizing_table(items: &[(String, String)]) -> String {
    let re = regex::Regex::new(r"[A-Za-z0-9_\-./]+\.(?:tsx?|jsx?|rs|css|json|md|py|toml|html)").expect("pattern");
    let files: Vec<(String, BTreeSet<String>)> = items.iter().map(|(id, body)| (id.clone(), re.find_iter(body).map(|m| m.as_str().to_string()).collect())).collect();
    let mut out: Vec<String> = files.iter().map(|(id, f)| if f.is_empty() { format!("{id}: (no files named)") } else { format!("{id}: {}", f.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) }).collect();
    for i in 0..files.len() {
        for j in i + 1..files.len() {
            let shared: Vec<String> = files[i].1.intersection(&files[j].1).cloned().collect();
            if !shared.is_empty() { out.push(format!("{} and {} share {}", files[i].0, files[j].0, shared.join(", "))); }
        }
    }
    out.join("\n")
}

fn move_aside(dir: &Path, n: u64) {
    if let Ok(Some(old)) = load(dir, n) {
        if old.state.terminal() {
            let _ = std::fs::rename(path(dir, n), runs_dir(dir).join(format!("{n}.prev-{}.json", crate::epoch_ms())));
        }
    }
}

fn set_round(dir: &Path, n: u64, state: &str, plan: &str, prompt: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut rounds = crate::notes::rounds::load(dir)?;
    match rounds.iter_mut().find(|r| r.n == n) {
        Some(r) => r.state = state.into(),
        None => rounds.push(crate::notes::rounds::Round { n, state: state.into(), kind: None, task_ids: vec![], note_paths: vec![],
            created_at: crate::epoch_ms(), plan_path: plan.into(), prompt_path: prompt.into() }),
    }
    crate::notes::rounds::save(dir, &rounds)
}

/// The planner prompt for a record — at the start, and again on Resume.
pub fn planner_prompt_for(p: &Project, rec: &RunRecord) -> String {
    let checks = repo_checks(&p.repo, p.manifest.as_ref());
    let lanes = rec.target_lanes.unwrap_or(max_lanes(p.manifest.as_ref()) as u32);
    let n = rec.round_n();
    match rec.kind {
        RunKind::Notes => prompts::planner_notes(&crate::FIXES_PROMPT_HEAD.replace("{N}", &n.to_string()).replace("{TASKS}", &format!(".chronicle/round_{n}_notes.json")), lanes, &checks),
        RunKind::Plan => prompts::planner_plan(n, &rec.plan_path, &rec.prompt_path, lanes, &checks),
        RunKind::Prompt => prompts::planner_prompt(n, &rec.source, lanes, &checks),
    }
}

pub fn begin_in(p: &Project, source: &Source, judge: Option<&Judge>) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let dir = &p.dir;
    if list(dir).iter().any(|r| !r.state.terminal()) { return Err("A round is already running in this project. Stop it or let it finish first.".into()); }
    let max = max_lanes(p.manifest.as_ref()) as u32;
    let (n, total, kind, text, items): (u64, u64, RunKind, String, Vec<(String, String)>) = match source {
        Source::Notes => {
            let out = crate::round_plan_begin_in(dir)?;
            let n = out["n"].as_u64().ok_or("the round has no number")?;
            let notes: Vec<Value> = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(format!(".chronicle/round_{n}_notes.json"))).unwrap_or_default()).unwrap_or_default();
            let items = notes.iter().map(|v| (v["title"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(), v["body"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string())).collect();
            (n, out["total"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), RunKind::Notes, String::new(), items)
        }
        Source::Plan { path: plan } => {
            let n = plan_number(plan).ok_or("That isn't a fixes/phase_N_fixes_plan.md file.")?;
            let body = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(plan)).map_err(|_| format!("There's no plan at {plan}."))?;
            set_round(dir, n, "generating", plan, &plan.replace("_plan.md", "_prompt.md"))?;
            let total = crate::notes::rounds::load(dir)?.into_iter().find(|r| r.n == n).map(|r| r.note_paths.len() as u64).unwrap_or(0);
            let items = body.lines().filter(|l| { let t = l.trim_start(); t.starts_with("- ") || t.starts_with("## ") }).map(|l| (l.trim().to_string(), l.to_string())).collect();
            (n, total, RunKind::Plan, plan.clone(), items)
        }
        Source::Prompt { text } => {
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err("Write what the round should do first.".into()); }
            let n = crate::notes::rounds::load(dir)?.iter().map(|r| r.n).max().unwrap_or(0).max(list(dir).iter().map(|r| r.n).max().unwrap_or(0)) + 1;
            set_round(dir, n, "generating", &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md"), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md"))?;
            (n, 0, RunKind::Prompt, text.clone(), vec![("the request".into(), text.clone())])
        }
    };
    move_aside(dir, n);
    let mut rec = RunRecord::new(n, kind, Some(n), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_plan.md"), &format!("fixes/phase_{n}_fixes_prompt.md"), &text);
    let (lanes, logs) = match judge {
        Some(j) if items.len() > 1 => j.sizing(&sizing_table(&items), max),
        _ => ((items.len() as u32).clamp(1, max), vec![]),
    };
    rec.target_lanes = Some(lanes);
    rec.decisions.extend(logs);
    save(dir, &mut rec)?;
    let prompt = planner_prompt_for(p, &rec);
    Ok(json!({ "n": n, "total": total, "prompt": prompt }))
}

fn fail_round(dir: &Path, n: u64) {
    if let Ok(rounds) = crate::notes::rounds::load(dir) {
        if let Some(r) = rounds.iter().find(|r| r.n == n) {
            for rel in &r.note_paths { let _ = crate::notes::rounds::set_status(dir, rel, Some("queued"), None); }
        }
    }
    runner::set_round_state(dir, n, "failed");
}

/// The planner's turn ended: a declared map launches the run; no map is
/// retried twice with the reasons, then the run fails (spec §3, §11).
pub fn settle_in(p: &Project, n: u64, planner_cost: f64) -> Result<Settle, String> {
    let mut rec = load(&p.dir, n)?.ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no round {n} being planned."))?;
    if rec.state != RunState::Planning { return Err(format!("Round {n} isn't being planned any more.")); }
    rec.planner_cost_usd += planner_cost.max(0.0);
    if rec.lane_map.is_some() {
        rec.set_state(RunState::Running)?;
        save(&p.dir, &mut rec)?;
        runner::set_round_state(&p.dir, rec.round_n(), "ready");
        return Ok(Settle::Launch(rec));
    }
    rec.planner_tries += 1;
    if rec.planner_tries > 2 {
        rec.set_state(RunState::Failed)?;
        let why = rec.planner_error.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "The planner never declared a lane map.".into());
        rec.planner_error = Some(why.clone());
        save(&p.dir, &mut rec)?;
        fail_round(&p.dir, rec.round_n());
        return Ok(Settle::Failed(format!("No lane map Chronicle could accept after three tries. {why}")));
    }
    save(&p.dir, &mut rec)?;
    Ok(Settle::Retry(prompts::planner_retry(rec.planner_error.as_deref()
        .unwrap_or("- No lane map was declared. Call chronicle_run_declare with the items and the lanes."))))
}

/// The user stopped the planning turn (or Stop on a planning run).
pub fn cancel_planning_in(p: &Project, n: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut rec = load(&p.dir, n)?.ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no round {n}."))?;
    if rec.state != RunState::Planning && rec.state != RunState::Interrupted { return Ok(()); }
    rec.state = RunState::Stopped;
    save(&p.dir, &mut rec)?;
    match rec.kind {
        RunKind::Plan => runner::set_round_state(&p.dir, rec.round_n(), "ready"),
        _ => crate::round_plan_cancel_in(&p.dir)?,
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// After a restart: a run nobody drives is interrupted, remembering where.
pub fn recover_in(dir: &Path, live: &dyn Fn(u64) -> bool) -> Vec<RunRecord> {
    let mut out = list(dir);
    for r in out.iter_mut() {
        if !r.state.terminal() && r.state != RunState::Interrupted && !live(r.n) {
            r.interrupted_from = Some(r.state);
            r.state = RunState::Interrupted;
            let _ = save(dir, r);
        }
    }
    out
}

pub fn apply_needs_you(dir: &Path, rec: &mut RunRecord, act: &NeedsAct) -> Result<(), String> {
    let parked_at = |rec: &RunRecord, item: &str| rec.needs_you.iter().position(|x| x.kind == "parked" && x.item.as_deref() == Some(item)).ok_or_else(|| format!("{item} isn't waiting on you."));
    match act {
        NeedsAct::Skip { item } => {
            let i = parked_at(rec, item)?;
            rec.needs_you.remove(i);
            rec.skipped.push(item.clone());
            let note = rec.lane_map.as_ref().and_then(|m| m.items.iter().find(|x| &x.id == item)).and_then(|x| x.note.clone());
            if let Some(note) = note {
                crate::notes::rounds::set_status(dir, &note, Some("queued"), None)?;
                if let Ok(mut rounds) = crate::notes::rounds::load(dir) {
                    if let Some(r) = rounds.iter_mut().find(|r| r.n == rec.round_n()) { r.note_paths.retain(|p| p != &note); }
                    let _ = crate::notes::rounds::save(dir, &rounds);
                }
            }
        }
        NeedsAct::Rewrite { item, text } => {
            let text = text.trim();
            if text.is_empty() { return Err("Write what the item should say first.".into()); }
            let i = parked_at(rec, item)?;
            let old = rec.needs_you[i].lane.clone().unwrap_or_default();
            let map = rec.lane_map.as_mut().ok_or("This round has no lanes.")?;
            let spec = map.lanes.iter().find(|l| l.id == old).cloned().ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no lane {old}."))?;
            let id = (b'a'..=b'z').map(|c| (c as char).to_string()).find(|c| !map.lanes.iter().any(|l| &l.id == c)).ok_or("A round can have at most 26 lanes.")?;
            if let Some(it) = map.items.iter_mut().find(|x| &x.id == item) { it.rewrite = Some(text.to_string()); }
            if let Some(l) = map.lanes.iter_mut().find(|l| l.id == old) { l.items.retain(|x| x != item); }
            map.lanes.push(super::record::LaneSpec { id: id.clone(), title: format!("{item}, rewritten"), items: vec![item.clone()],
                owns: spec.owns.clone(), depends_on: vec![old.clone()], checks: spec.checks.clone() });
            rec.lanes.push(LaneRecord::new(&id));
            rec.needs_you.remove(i);
        }
        NeedsAct::Retry { lane } => {
            let i = rec.needs_you.iter().position(|x| x.kind == "exhausted" && x.lane.as_deref() == Some(lane)).ok_or_else(|| format!("Lane {lane} isn't waiting on you."))?;
            rec.needs_you.remove(i);
            let l = rec.lane_mut(lane).ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no lane {lane}."))?;
            l.state = LaneState::Waiting;
            l.attempt = 0;
            l.fixes = 0;
            l.parked = None;
            l.retry_at_ms = None;
        }
        NeedsAct::Recheck => {
            if !rec.needs_you.iter().any(|x| x.kind == "head") { return Err("There's no failed check waiting on you.".into()); }
            rec.needs_you.retain(|x| x.kind != "head");
            rec.head_check = None;
        }
        NeedsAct::Dismiss { index } => {
            if rec.needs_you.get(*index).map(|x| x.kind == "refused") != Some(true) { return Err("Only a refused publish can be dismissed.".into()); }
            rec.needs_you.remove(*index);
        }
    }
    if rec.state == RunState::NeedsYou && scheduler::blocking(rec) == 0 { rec.set_state(RunState::Running)?; }
    Ok(())
}

/// A controller for a running record, registered and started.
fn launch(app: &tauri::AppHandle, runs: &Runs, p: &Project, rec: RunRecord, first: Vec<Action>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let (decisions, review) = oracle::for_project(&p.dir);
    let (ctl, rx) = RunCtl::new(CtlParts {
        dir: p.dir.clone(), repo: p.repo.clone(), scratch: gate::scratch_dir(&p.repo), rec,
        checks: repo_checks(&p.repo, p.manifest.as_ref()), max_lanes: max_lanes(p.manifest.as_ref()),
        judge: Judge::new(decisions), reviewer: review,
        host: Arc::new(super::host::AcpHost { app: app.clone() }), publish: runs.publisher(),
    });
    runs.insert(ctl.clone());
    ctl.update(|_| {});
    runner::spawn(ctl, rx, first);
    Ok(())
}

fn live_ctl(runs: &Runs, p: &Project, n: u64) -> Result<Arc<RunCtl>, String> {
    runs.get(&p.dir, n).filter(|c| !c.snapshot().state.terminal()).ok_or_else(|| format!("Round {n} isn't running — resume it first."))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_begin(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, source: Source) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    let (decisions, _) = oracle::for_project(&p.dir);
    let out = begin_in(&p, &source, Some(&Judge::new(decisions)))?;
    if let Some(rec) = out["n"].as_u64().and_then(|n| load(&p.dir, n).ok().flatten()) { runs.publish(&p.dir, &rec); }
    Ok(out)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_plan_settle(app: tauri::AppHandle, roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64, planner_cost: Option<f64>) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    let out = match settle_in(&p, n, planner_cost.unwrap_or(0.0))? {
        Settle::Launch(rec) => { launch(&app, &runs, &p, rec, vec![])?; json!({ "state": "running" }) }
        Settle::Retry(prompt) => json!({ "state": "retry", "prompt": prompt }),
        Settle::Failed(reason) => json!({ "state": "failed", "reason": reason }),
    };
    if let Some(rec) = load(&p.dir, n)? { runs.publish(&p.dir, &rec); }
    Ok(out)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_plan_cancel(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    cancel_planning_in(&p, n)?;
    if let Some(rec) = load(&p.dir, n)? { runs.publish(&p.dir, &rec); }
    Ok(())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn runs_list(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String) -> Result<Vec<RunRecord>, String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    let live = |n: u64| runs.get(&p.dir, n).map(|c| !c.snapshot().state.terminal()).unwrap_or(false);
    Ok(recover_in(&p.dir, &live).into_iter().map(|r| runs.get(&p.dir, r.n).map(|c| c.snapshot()).unwrap_or(r)).collect())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_stop(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    if let Ok(ctl) = live_ctl(&runs, &p, n) { return ctl.tx.send(runner::Ev::Stop).map_err(|e| e.to_string()); }
    let rec = load(&p.dir, n)?.ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no round {n}."))?;
    match rec.state {
        RunState::Planning => cancel_planning_in(&p, n)?,
        RunState::Interrupted => {
            let mut r = rec;
            r.state = RunState::Stopped;
            save(&p.dir, &mut r)?;
            runner::set_round_state(&p.dir, r.round_n(), "failed");
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    if let Some(rec) = load(&p.dir, n)? { runs.publish(&p.dir, &rec); }
    Ok(())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_resume(app: tauri::AppHandle, roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    let rec = load(&p.dir, n)?.ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no round {n}."))?;
    if rec.state != RunState::Interrupted { return Err(format!("Round {n} wasn't interrupted.")); }
    let (mut back, first) = scheduler::resume(&rec);
    if back.state == RunState::Planning {
        save(&p.dir, &mut back)?;
        runs.publish(&p.dir, &back);
        return Ok(json!({ "state": "planning", "prompt": planner_prompt_for(&p, &back) }));
    }
    save(&p.dir, &mut back)?;
    launch(&app, &runs, &p, back, first)?;
    Ok(json!({ "state": "running" }))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_needs_you(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64, act: NeedsAct) -> Result<(), String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    let ctl = live_ctl(&runs, &p, n)?;
    let mut result = Ok(());
    ctl.update(|r| { result = apply_needs_you(&p.dir, r, &act); });
    let _ = ctl.tx.send(runner::Ev::Kick);
    result
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_lane_nudge(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64, lane: String, text: String) -> Result<(), String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    live_ctl(&runs, &p, n)?.nudge(&lane, &text)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn run_lane_discard(roots: State<'_, OpenRoots>, runs: State<'_, Runs>, dir: String, n: u64, lane: String) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let p = crate::project_for(&roots, &dir)?;
    let rec = runs.get(&p.dir, n).map(|c| c.snapshot()).or_else(|| load(&p.dir, n).ok().flatten()).ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no round {n}."))?;
    let l = rec.lane(&lane).ok_or_else(|| format!("There's no lane {lane}."))?;
    if l.state.busy() { return Err(format!("Lane {} is still working.", lane.to_uppercase())); }
    let globs = rec.lane_map.as_ref().and_then(|m| m.lanes.iter().find(|s| s.id == lane)).map(|s| super::lane_map::compile(&s.owns)).unwrap_or_default();
    let mine: Vec<String> = gate::dirty_paths(&p.repo).into_iter().filter(|f| globs.iter().any(|g| g.matches(f))).collect();
    gate::discard_paths(&p.repo, &mine)
}
```

- Consumes: the Tauri commands and the `run-update` event of Task 11; `notifyNative` from `./ipc`.
- Produces:
  - `runs-ipc.ts`: `type RunSource`, `type NeedsAct`, `runBegin(dir, source)`, `runPlanSettle(dir, n, plannerCost)`, `runPlanCancel(dir, n)`, `runsList(dir)`, `runStop(dir, n)`, `runResume(dir, n)`, `runNeedsYou(dir, n, act)`, `runLaneNudge(dir, n, lane, text)`, `runLaneDiscard(dir, n, lane)`, `onRunUpdate(cb)`. (Its own module, not `ipc.ts`, so the agent-session suites that mock `./ipc` wholesale never need run stubs.)
  - `runs-model.ts`: the record types (`RunRecord`, `LaneRecord`, `Gate`, `CheckResult`, `Leak`, `NeedsYou`, `DecisionLog`, `RunState`, `LaneState`, …) mirroring `record.rs`; `type Word = { word: string; tone: Tone; pulse: boolean }`; selectors `laneWord`, `tryLabel`, `runWord`, `runTitle`, `lanesSummary`, `fmtCost`, `fmtElapsed`, `elapsedMs`, `laneElapsed`, `runOver`, `laneBusy`, `runFooter`, `needsYouRows` (→ `NeedsRow[]`), `decisionLine`, `planRowText`, `runSubline`, `noteChip`, `finalCheckRows`, `checkRows`, `gateLabel`, `reviewLine`, `leakParts`, `laneItemTitles`, `ownsLabel`, `laneDivider`, `transitionNotice`, `countWord`
  - `runs-store.ts`: `ingest(dir, rec) -> { accepted: boolean; prev: RunRecord | null }`, `subscribeRuns(cb)`, `runsVersion()`, `runFor(dir, n)`, `liveRun(dir)`, `refreshRuns(dir): Promise<void>`

```ts
/*
 * Every word the run card, the lane view and the notes card say, pinned to the
 * approved boards (design/comps/runs/gen.py). The fixture is board R1; each
 * block patches it into the next board's state.
 */
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as M from "./runs-model";

const base = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "__fixtures__/run-record.json"), "utf8")) as M.RunRecord;
const clone = (): M.RunRecord => structuredClone(base);
const lane = (r: M.RunRecord, id: string) => r.lanes.find((l) => l.id === id)!;
const NOW = Date.parse("2026-10-15T09:04:12Z");
const ok = (name: string, ms: number): M.CheckResult => ({ name, exit: 0, ms, timed_out: false, tail: "" });

describe("the run card, working (board R1)", () => {
  const r = clone();
  it("says what the header says", () => {
    expect(M.runTitle(r)).toBe("Round 10 · 7 items");
    expect(M.runWord(r)).toEqual({ word: "running", tone: "neutral", pulse: true });
    expect(M.lanesSummary(r)).toBe("3 of 4 lanes");
    expect(M.fmtCost(r.cost.usd)).toBe("$1.84");
    expect(M.fmtElapsed(M.elapsedMs(r, NOW))).toBe("4m 12s");
  });
  it("words each lane as the board does", () => {
    expect(M.laneWord(r, lane(r, "a")).word).toBe("working");
    expect(M.laneWord(r, lane(r, "c")).word).toBe("being checked");
    expect(M.laneWord(r, lane(r, "d"))).toEqual({ word: "waiting", tone: "dim", pulse: false });
    expect(M.tryLabel(lane(r, "b"))).toBe("try 2 of 3");
    expect(M.tryLabel(lane(r, "a"))).toBeNull();
  });
  it("ends with the quiet sentence and shows the latest decision", () => {
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Nothing needs you. Chronicle saves each lane as it passes.");
    expect(M.decisionLine(r)).toEqual({ lead: "Lane B asked to run", command: "npx vitest run src/lib", tail: "— allowed, it reads only.", prob: "0.93" });
    expect(M.planRowText(r, 10, 7)).toBe("Planned round 10 · 7 items into 4 lanes");
    expect(M.planRowText(null, 10, 7)).toBe("Planning round 10 · 7 notes");
    expect(M.planRowText(null, 11, 0)).toBe("Planning round 11");
  });
  it("mirrors into the notes card", () => {
    expect(M.runSubline(r, 1, 7)).toBe("3 lanes working · 1 of 7 done · $1.84");
    expect(M.noteChip(r, "Tasks/T-144.md", "in_progress")).toEqual({ label: "checking", tone: "progress" });
    expect(M.noteChip(r, "Tasks/T-142.md", "in_progress")).toEqual({ label: "working", tone: "progress" });
    expect(M.noteChip(r, "Tasks/T-146.md", "in_progress")).toEqual({ label: "in round", tone: "unknown" });
    expect(M.noteChip(r, "Tasks/T-141.md", "done")).toEqual({ label: "done", tone: "done" });
    expect(M.noteChip(r, "Tasks/elsewhere.md", null)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("draws the lane view's header (board R2)", () => {
    expect(M.laneItemTitles(r, lane(r, "c"))).toBe("Scroll anchoring on a bottom sentinel · content-visibility on off-screen rows");
    expect(M.ownsLabel(r, lane(r, "c"))).toBe("Owns 2 files");
    expect(M.laneDivider(r, lane(r, "c"))).toBe("lane C · being checked");
    expect(M.fmtElapsed(M.laneElapsed(lane(r, "c"), Date.parse("2026-10-15T09:04:12Z")))).toBe("3m 50s");
  });
});

describe("a gate failing, the fix, the final check, the save (board R3)", () => {
  it("a check fails → fixing", () => {
    const r = clone();
    Object.assign(lane(r, "c"), { state: "fixing", fixes: 1, gates: [{ commit: "c1", fix: 0, notice: null, review: null, checks: [
      ok("test", 4200), { name: "typecheck", exit: 2, ms: 2100, timed_out: false, tail: "src/screens/agent/AgentPane.tsx(612,9): error TS2339" }] }] });
    expect(M.laneWord(r, lane(r, "c"))).toEqual({ word: "fixing", tone: "warn", pulse: false });
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Lane C’s typecheck failed. It is fixing that now — fix 1 of 2.");
    expect(M.gateLabel("c", lane(r, "c").gates[0], false)).toBe("Lane C · checked after save");
    expect(M.gateLabel("c", lane(r, "c").gates[0], true)).toBe("Checked after save");
    expect(M.checkRows(lane(r, "c").gates[0])).toEqual([
      { name: "test", state: "pass", word: "passed", dur: "4.2 s" },
      { name: "typecheck", state: "fail", word: "failed", dur: "2.1 s" },
    ]);
  });
  it("the fix passes", () => {
    const r = clone();
    Object.assign(lane(r, "a"), { state: "closed", cost_usd: 0.74, ended: "2026-10-15T09:05:00Z" });
    Object.assign(lane(r, "c"), { state: "closed", fixes: 1, ended: "2026-10-15T09:07:00Z", gates: [
      { commit: "c1", fix: 0, notice: null, review: null, checks: [ok("test", 4200), { name: "typecheck", exit: 2, ms: 2100, timed_out: false, tail: "x" }] },
      { commit: "c2", fix: 1, notice: null, review: { model: "review", pass: true, findings: [] }, checks: [ok("test", 4300), ok("typecheck", 2000), ok("build", 9800)] }] });
    Object.assign(lane(r, "d"), { state: "streaming", cost_usd: 0.12 });
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Lane C passed on its fix. Two lanes still working.");
    expect(M.gateLabel("c", lane(r, "c").gates[1], false)).toBe("Lane C · fix 1 · checked after save");
    expect(M.reviewLine(lane(r, "c").gates[1].review!)).toBe("Reviewed: looks right");
    expect(M.reviewLine({ model: "review", pass: false, findings: [{ file: "a", line: 1, what: "x" }, { file: "b", line: 2, what: "y" }] })).toBe("Reviewed: 2 findings");
  });
  it("the final check of the whole project, then saved", () => {
    const r = clone();
    r.lanes.forEach((l) => (l.state = "closed"));
    r.state = "gating";
    r.head_pending = ["test", "typecheck", "build", "cargo test"];
    expect(M.runWord(r).word).toBe("final check");
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Every lane passed on its own. One last check of the whole project, then it’s saved.");
    expect(M.finalCheckRows(r).map((c) => c.state)).toEqual(["run", "queued", "queued", "queued"]);
    r.state = "done";
    r.head_pending = [];
    r.head_check = { commit: "h", reopened: null, checks: [ok("test", 4400), ok("typecheck", 2100), ok("build", 9900), ok("cargo test", 31000)] };
    expect(M.finalCheckRows(r).map((c) => c.state)).toEqual(["pass", "pass", "pass", "pass"]);
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Saved as round 10. 7 items done.");
    expect(M.runWord(r)).toEqual({ word: "done", tone: "success", pulse: false });
  });
  it("the leak notice", () => {
    const r = clone();
    lane(r, "b").state = "closed";
    lane(r, "b").leak = [{ path: "src/index.css", owner: "c", by: null }];
    lane(r, "c").leak = [{ path: "src/index.css", owner: "c", by: "b" }];
    expect(M.leakParts(lane(r, "b").leak[0])).toEqual({ lead: "Also changed ", path: "src/index.css", tail: ", which lane C owns — left unsaved for you." });
    expect(M.leakParts(lane(r, "c").leak[0])).toEqual({ lead: "Lane B also changed ", path: "src/index.css", tail: ", which this lane owns — left unsaved for you." });
    expect(M.leakParts({ path: "NOTES.txt", owner: null, by: null }).tail).toBe(", which no lane owns — left unsaved for you.");
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Lane B also touched a file lane C owns. That change is left unsaved and listed above; nothing is undone for you.");
  });
});

describe("needs you (board R4)", () => {
  const r = clone();
  r.state = "needs-you";
  r.lanes.forEach((l) => (l.state = "closed"));
  Object.assign(lane(r, "a"), { cost_usd: 0.74, commit: "a1" });
  Object.assign(lane(r, "d"), { attempt: 2, cost_usd: 0.41 });
  r.cost.usd = 2.52;
  r.needs_you = [
    { kind: "parked", lane: "d", item: "T-147", reason: "The pane starts the session when it mounts. Starting it when the project opens needs a hook in src/App.tsx, which this lane doesn’t own.", reading: 0.86 },
    { kind: "refused", lane: "a", item: null, reason: "npm publish", reading: null },
  ];
  it("words the lane and the run", () => {
    expect(M.laneWord(r, lane(r, "d"))).toEqual({ word: "needs you", tone: "warn", pulse: false });
    expect(M.runWord(r).word).toBe("needs you");
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("One thing needs you: an item in lane D can’t be done as written. The other six are saved.");
  });
  it("draws the two rows with their buttons", () => {
    const [parked, refused] = M.needsYouRows(r);
    expect(parked).toMatchObject({ title: "Pre-warm the session when the project opens", reading: "0.86",
      body: "Lane D: “The pane starts the session when it mounts. Starting it when the project opens needs a hook in src/App.tsx, which this lane doesn’t own.”" });
    expect(parked.actions.map((a) => a.label)).toEqual(["Rewrite the item", "Skip it"]);
    expect(refused).toMatchObject({ title: "The agent tried to publish. Chronicle doesn’t publish for you.", body: "Lane A, after its last save. Nothing left your Mac." });
    expect(refused.actions.map((a) => a.label)).toEqual(["Open the lane"]);
  });
  it("mirrors into the notes card and tells an away user", () => {
    expect(M.runSubline(r, 6, 7)).toBe("needs you · 3 lanes saved · 6 of 7 done · $2.52");
    expect(M.noteChip(r, "Tasks/T-147.md", "in_progress")).toEqual({ label: "needs you", tone: "queued" });
    expect(M.transitionNotice(clone(), r)).toBe("Round 10 needs you.");
    expect(M.transitionNotice(r, r)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("the edges the boards don't draw", () => {
  it("formats time and counts", () => {
    expect(M.fmtElapsed(42_000)).toBe("42s");
    expect(M.fmtElapsed(65_000)).toBe("1m 05s");
    expect(M.fmtElapsed(3_780_000)).toBe("1h 03m");
    expect(M.countWord(2)).toBe("two");
    expect(M.countWord(14)).toBe("14");
  });
  it("says why a run stopped, failed or was interrupted", () => {
    const r = clone();
    r.state = "interrupted";
    expect(M.runWord(r).word).toBe("interrupted");
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Chronicle stopped while this round was running. Resume picks up the lanes that hadn’t finished.");
    r.state = "stopped";
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Stopped. Nothing was undone; each lane’s unsaved changes are listed in its thread.");
    r.state = "failed";
    r.planner_error = "- Item T-3 isn't in any lane.";
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("The plan couldn’t be split into lanes: - Item T-3 isn't in any lane.");
    expect(M.runOver(r)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("an exhausted lane and a failed final check each say what to do", () => {
    const r = clone();
    r.state = "needs-you";
    r.needs_you = [{ kind: "exhausted", lane: "d", item: null, reason: "Lane D didn’t finish after three tries.", reading: null }];
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("One thing needs you: lane D didn’t finish after three tries.");
    expect(M.needsYouRows(r)[0].actions.map((a) => a.label)).toEqual(["Try the lane again", "Open the lane"]);
    r.needs_you = [{ kind: "head", lane: null, item: null, reason: "`build` failed (exit 1)", reading: null }];
    expect(M.needsYouRows(r)[0]).toMatchObject({ title: "The whole-project check failed after every lane passed.", mono: "`build` failed (exit 1)" });
    expect(M.needsYouRows(r)[0].actions.map((a) => a.label)).toEqual(["Check again"]);
  });
});
```

Create `src/lib/runs-store.test.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * The run store: Rust is the writer, the store only keeps the newest copy of
 * each record it has heard of, by project and number.
 */
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { RunRecord } from "./runs-model";

const base = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "__fixtures__/run-record.json"), "utf8")) as RunRecord;
let push: ((u: { dir: string; record: RunRecord }) => void) | null = null;
vi.mock("./runs-ipc", () => ({
  onRunUpdate: vi.fn(async (cb: (u: { dir: string; record: RunRecord }) => void) => { push = cb; return () => {}; }),
  runsList: vi.fn(async () => [structuredClone(base)]),
}));
vi.mock("./ipc", () => ({ notifyNative: vi.fn(async () => {}) }));

const store = await import("./runs-store");
const DIR = "/home/u/proj";

describe("the run store", () => {
  it("loads a project's runs and hears every update after", async () => {
    const seen = vi.fn();
    store.subscribeRuns(seen);
    await store.refreshRuns(DIR);
    expect(store.runFor(DIR, 10)?.state).toBe("running");
    expect(store.liveRun(DIR)?.n).toBe(10);
    const v = store.runsVersion();
    push!({ dir: DIR, record: { ...structuredClone(base), state: "gating", updated: "2026-10-15T09:05:00Z" } });
    expect(store.runFor(DIR, 10)?.state).toBe("gating");
    expect(store.runsVersion()).toBe(v + 1);
    expect(seen).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("never goes back to an older copy", () => {
    push!({ dir: DIR, record: { ...structuredClone(base), state: "running", updated: "2026-10-15T09:01:00Z" } });
    expect(store.runFor(DIR, 10)?.state).toBe("gating");
    expect(store.ingest(DIR, { ...structuredClone(base), state: "done", updated: "2026-10-15T09:05:00Z" }).accepted).toBe(true);
    expect(store.liveRun(DIR)).toBeNull();
    expect(store.runFor("/other", 10)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The ipc module**

Create `src/lib/runs-ipc.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * The run commands and the `run-update` event (src-tauri/src/runs/commands.rs).
 * Its own module so the agent-session suites, which mock ./ipc wholesale,
 * never need to know runs exist.
 */
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { listen, type UnlistenFn } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
import type { RunRecord } from "./runs-model";

export type RunSource = { kind: "notes" } | { kind: "plan"; path: string } | { kind: "prompt"; text: string };
export type NeedsAct =
  | { kind: "skip"; item: string }
  | { kind: "rewrite"; item: string; text: string }
  | { kind: "retry"; lane: string }
  | { kind: "recheck" }
  | { kind: "dismiss"; index: number };

export const runBegin = (dir: string, source: RunSource) =>
  invoke<{ n: number; total: number; prompt: string }>("run_begin", { dir, source });
export const runPlanSettle = (dir: string, n: number, plannerCost: number) =>
  invoke<{ state: "running" | "retry" | "failed"; prompt?: string; reason?: string }>("run_plan_settle", { dir, n, plannerCost });
export const runPlanCancel = (dir: string, n: number) => invoke<void>("run_plan_cancel", { dir, n });
export const runsList = (dir: string) => invoke<RunRecord[]>("runs_list", { dir });
export const runStop = (dir: string, n: number) => invoke<void>("run_stop", { dir, n });
export const runResume = (dir: string, n: number) => invoke<{ state: "running" | "planning"; prompt?: string }>("run_resume", { dir, n });
export const runNeedsYou = (dir: string, n: number, act: NeedsAct) => invoke<void>("run_needs_you", { dir, n, act });
export const runLaneNudge = (dir: string, n: number, lane: string, text: string) => invoke<void>("run_lane_nudge", { dir, n, lane, text });
export const runLaneDiscard = (dir: string, n: number, lane: string) => invoke<number>("run_lane_discard", { dir, n, lane });
export const onRunUpdate = (cb: (u: { dir: string; record: RunRecord }) => void): Promise<UnlistenFn> =>
  listen<{ dir: string; record: RunRecord }>("run-update", (e) => cb(e.payload));
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The model**

Create `src/lib/runs-model.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * The run record as the frontend reads it (src-tauri/src/runs/record.rs is the
 * only writer), and every word the run card, the lane view and the notes card
 * say — copied from the approved boards (design/comps/runs/gen.py). Strings
 * the boards don't draw are marked (new copy). Pure: the components render
 * what these return.
 */
export type RunState = "planning" | "running" | "gating" | "closing" | "done" | "needs-you" | "failed" | "stopped" | "interrupted";
export type LaneState =
  | "waiting" | "preparing" | "building_prompt" | "launching" | "initialising" | "streaming" | "finishing" | "gating" | "fixing"
  | "closed" | "failed" | "timed_out" | "stalled" | "cancelled" | "parked";
export interface Item { id: string; title: string; note?: string; rewrite?: string }
export interface LaneSpec { id: string; title: string; items: string[]; owns: string[]; depends_on: string[]; checks: string[] }
export interface CheckResult { name: string; exit: number | null; ms: number; timed_out: boolean; tail: string }
export interface Finding { file: string; line: number; what: string }
export interface Review { model: string; pass: boolean; findings: Finding[] }
export interface Gate { commit: string; checks: CheckResult[]; review: Review | null; notice: string | null; fix: number }
export interface Leak { path: string; owner: string | null; by: string | null }
export interface LaneRecord {
  id: string; state: LaneState; attempt: number; started: string | null; ended: string | null; cost_usd: number;
  tokens: { in: number; out: number }; commit: string | null; leak: Leak[]; gates: Gate[]; parked: string | null;
  retry_at_ms: number | null; session: string | null; fixes: number;
}
export interface HeadCheck { commit: string; checks: CheckResult[]; reopened: string | null }
export interface DecisionLog {
  at: string; kind: string; model: string; choice: string; probabilities: Record<string, number>; confidence: number;
  band: string; action: string; self_reported: boolean; lane: string | null; subject: string; reason: string;
}
export interface NeedsYou { kind: "parked" | "exhausted" | "head" | "refused"; lane: string | null; item: string | null; reason: string; reading: number | null }
export interface RunRecord {
  n: number; round: number | null; kind: "notes" | "plan" | "prompt"; state: RunState; created: string; updated: string;
  plan_path: string; prompt_path: string; source: string; lane_map: { items: Item[]; lanes: LaneSpec[] } | null;
  lanes: LaneRecord[]; head_check: HeadCheck | null; decisions: DecisionLog[]; needs_you: NeedsYou[];
  cost: { usd: number; in: number; out: number }; planner_tries: number; planner_error: string | null; planner_cost_usd: number;
  notices: string[]; target_lanes: number | null; interrupted_from: RunState | null; skipped: string[]; head_pending: string[];
}

export type Tone = "neutral" | "success" | "warn" | "error" | "dim";
export interface Word { word: string; tone: Tone; pulse: boolean }

const ID = (id: string | null | undefined) => (id ?? "").toUpperCase();
const NUM = ["no", "one", "two", "three", "four", "five", "six", "seven", "eight", "nine", "ten"];
export const countWord = (n: number) => (n >= 0 && n < NUM.length ? NUM[n] : String(n));
const cap = (s: string) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
const plural = (n: number, w: string) => `${n} ${w}${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
const BUSY: LaneState[] = ["preparing", "building_prompt", "launching", "initialising", "streaming", "finishing", "gating", "fixing"];
export const laneBusy = (l: LaneRecord) => BUSY.includes(l.state);
export const runOver = (r: RunRecord) => r.state === "done" || r.state === "failed" || r.state === "stopped";
const blocking = (r: RunRecord) => r.needs_you.filter((x) => x.kind !== "refused");
const laneNeedsYou = (r: RunRecord, id: string) => r.needs_you.some((x) => x.lane === id && x.kind !== "refused");
const itemCount = (r: RunRecord | null, fallback = 0) => r?.lane_map?.items.length ?? fallback;
const spec = (r: RunRecord, id: string) => r.lane_map?.lanes.find((l) => l.id === id);

export function laneWord(r: RunRecord, l: LaneRecord): Word {
  if (l.state === "parked" || laneNeedsYou(r, l.id)) return { word: "needs you", tone: "warn", pulse: false };
  switch (l.state) {
    case "waiting": return { word: "waiting", tone: "dim", pulse: false };
    case "finishing": case "gating": return { word: "being checked", tone: "neutral", pulse: true };
    case "fixing": return { word: "fixing", tone: "warn", pulse: false };
    case "closed": return { word: "finished", tone: "success", pulse: false };
    case "cancelled": return { word: "stopped", tone: "error", pulse: false };
    default: return { word: "working", tone: "neutral", pulse: true };
  }
}

export const tryLabel = (l: LaneRecord) => (l.attempt > 1 ? `try ${l.attempt} of 3` : null);

export function runWord(r: RunRecord): Word {
  switch (r.state) {
    case "planning": return { word: "planning", tone: "neutral", pulse: true };
    case "running": return { word: "running", tone: "neutral", pulse: true };
    case "gating": case "closing": return { word: "final check", tone: "neutral", pulse: true };
    case "done": return { word: "done", tone: "success", pulse: false };
    case "needs-you": return { word: "needs you", tone: "warn", pulse: false };
    case "interrupted": return { word: "interrupted", tone: "warn", pulse: false }; // (new copy)
    case "stopped": return { word: "stopped", tone: "error", pulse: false };
    case "failed": return { word: "didn’t start", tone: "error", pulse: false }; // (new copy)
  }
}

export const runTitle = (r: RunRecord, fallback = 0) => `Round ${r.round ?? r.n} · ${plural(itemCount(r, fallback), "item")}`;
export const lanesSummary = (r: RunRecord) => `${r.lanes.filter((l) => l.state !== "waiting").length} of ${r.lanes.length} lanes`;
export const fmtCost = (usd: number) => `$${usd.toFixed(2)}`;

export function fmtElapsed(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  if (s < 60) return `${s}s`;
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}s`;
  return `${Math.floor(m / 60)}h ${String(m % 60).padStart(2, "0")}m`;
}
export const elapsedMs = (r: RunRecord, now: number) => Math.max(0, (runOver(r) ? Date.parse(r.updated) : now) - Date.parse(r.created));
export const laneElapsed = (l: LaneRecord, now: number) =>
  l.started ? Math.max(0, (l.ended ? Date.parse(l.ended) : now) - Date.parse(l.started)) : 0;

function needsFooter(r: RunRecord, rows: NeedsYou[]): string {
  const parked = rows.filter((x) => x.kind === "parked").length;
  const others = Math.max(0, itemCount(r) - parked);
  const saved = others === 1 ? "The other one is saved." : `The other ${countWord(others)} are saved.`;
  if (rows.length === 1) {
    const x = rows[0];
    const what = x.kind === "parked" ? `an item in lane ${ID(x.lane)} can’t be done as written`
      : x.kind === "exhausted" ? x.reason.replace(/\.$/, "").replace(/^Lane/, "lane")
      : "the whole-project check failed after every lane passed";
    return `One thing needs you: ${what}.${x.kind === "parked" ? ` ${saved}` : ""}`;
  }
  return `${cap(countWord(rows.length))} things need you.${parked > 0 ? ` ${saved}` : ""}`;
}

/** The one sentence at the foot of the card: the most important thing happening. */
export function runFooter(r: RunRecord): string {
  const round = r.round ?? r.n;
  if (r.state === "done") return `Saved as round ${round}. ${plural(itemCount(r), "item")} done.`;
  if (r.state === "stopped") return "Stopped. Nothing was undone; each lane’s unsaved changes are listed in its thread."; // (new copy)
  if (r.state === "failed") return r.planner_error ? `The plan couldn’t be split into lanes: ${r.planner_error}` : "The plan couldn’t be split into lanes."; // (new copy)
  if (r.state === "interrupted") return "Chronicle stopped while this round was running. Resume picks up the lanes that hadn’t finished."; // (new copy)
  const rows = blocking(r);
  if (rows.length > 0) return needsFooter(r, rows);
  if (r.state === "gating" || r.state === "closing") return "Every lane passed on its own. One last check of the whole project, then it’s saved.";
  const fixing = r.lanes.find((l) => l.state === "fixing");
  if (fixing) {
    const failed = fixing.gates.at(-1)?.checks.find((c) => c.exit !== 0)?.name;
    return `Lane ${ID(fixing.id)}’s ${failed ? `${failed} failed` : "review found problems"}. It is fixing that now — fix ${Math.max(1, fixing.fixes)} of 2.`;
  }
  const leaker = r.lanes.find((l) => l.leak.some((k) => k.by == null && k.owner != null));
  if (leaker) {
    const k = leaker.leak.find((x) => x.by == null && x.owner != null)!;
    return `Lane ${ID(leaker.id)} also touched a file lane ${ID(k.owner)} owns. That change is left unsaved and listed above; nothing is undone for you.`;
  }
  const fixed = r.lanes.filter((l) => l.state === "closed" && (l.gates.at(-1)?.fix ?? 0) > 0).sort((a, b) => (b.ended ?? "").localeCompare(a.ended ?? ""))[0];
  const working = r.lanes.filter((l) => l.state !== "closed" && l.state !== "waiting").length;
  if (fixed && working > 0) return `Lane ${ID(fixed.id)} passed on its fix. ${cap(countWord(working))} ${working === 1 ? "lane" : "lanes"} still working.`;
  return "Nothing needs you. Chronicle saves each lane as it passes.";
}

export interface NeedsRow {
  index: number; kind: NeedsYou["kind"]; lane: string | null; item: string | null;
  title: string; body: string; mono?: string; reading: string | null;
  actions: { label: string; act: "rewrite" | "skip" | "retry" | "recheck" | "open" | "dismiss"; primary?: boolean }[];
}

export function needsYouRows(r: RunRecord): NeedsRow[] {
  return r.needs_you.map((x, index): NeedsRow => {
    const head = { index, kind: x.kind, lane: x.lane, item: x.item, reading: null };
    switch (x.kind) {
      case "parked": {
        const it = r.lane_map?.items.find((i) => i.id === x.item);
        return { ...head, title: it?.title ?? x.item ?? "", body: `Lane ${ID(x.lane)}: “${x.reason}”`, reading: x.reading == null ? null : x.reading.toFixed(2),
          actions: [{ label: "Rewrite the item", act: "rewrite", primary: true }, { label: "Skip it", act: "skip" }] };
      }
      case "refused": {
        const saved = x.lane ? r.lanes.find((l) => l.id === x.lane)?.commit : null;
        return x.lane
          ? { ...head, title: "The agent tried to publish. Chronicle doesn’t publish for you.", body: `Lane ${ID(x.lane)}${saved ? ", after its last save" : ""}. Nothing left your Mac.`, actions: [{ label: "Open the lane", act: "open" }] }
          : { ...head, title: "The agent tried to publish. Chronicle doesn’t publish for you.", body: "The chat session, during the round. Nothing left your Mac.", actions: [{ label: "Dismiss", act: "dismiss" }] }; // (new copy)
      }
      case "exhausted":
        return { ...head, title: x.reason, body: "Its work so far is left as it is.", actions: [{ label: "Try the lane again", act: "retry", primary: true }, { label: "Open the lane", act: "open" }] }; // (new copy)
      case "head":
        return { ...head, title: "The whole-project check failed after every lane passed.", body: "", mono: x.reason, actions: [{ label: "Check again", act: "recheck", primary: true }] }; // (new copy)
    }
  });
}

export function decisionLine(r: RunRecord): { lead: string; command: string; tail: string; prob: string } | null {
  const d = [...r.decisions].reverse().find((x) => x.kind === "shell" && x.lane);
  if (!d) return null;
  return { lead: `Lane ${ID(d.lane)} asked to run`, command: d.subject, tail: `— ${d.action === "allow" ? "allowed" : "refused"}${d.reason ? `, ${d.reason}` : ""}.`, prob: d.confidence.toFixed(2) };
}

export function planRowText(r: RunRecord | null, n: number, total: number): string {
  if (!r?.lane_map) return `Planning round ${n}${total > 0 ? ` · ${plural(total, r?.kind === "plan" || r?.kind === "prompt" ? "item" : "note")}` : ""}`;
  return `Planned round ${r.round ?? r.n} · ${plural(itemCount(r), "item")} into ${plural(r.lane_map.lanes.length, "lane")}`;
}

export function runSubline(r: RunRecord, done: number, total: number): string {
  const tail = `${done} of ${total} done · ${fmtCost(r.cost.usd)}`;
  if (r.state === "needs-you") return `needs you · ${r.lanes.filter((l) => l.state === "closed" && !laneNeedsYou(r, l.id)).length} lanes saved · ${tail}`;
  if (r.state === "planning") return `${plural(total, "note")} · planning the lanes…`; // (new copy)
  if (r.state === "gating" || r.state === "closing") return `final check · ${tail}`;
  if (runOver(r) || r.state === "interrupted") return `${runWord(r).word} · ${tail}`;
  return `${r.lanes.filter(laneBusy).length} lanes working · ${tail}`;
}

/** A note row's chip on the notes card while its round runs (board R1/R4). */
export function noteChip(r: RunRecord, notePath: string, status: string | null): { label: string; tone: string } | null {
  const item = r.lane_map?.items.find((i) => i.note === notePath);
  if (!item) return null;
  if (r.needs_you.some((x) => x.kind === "parked" && x.item === item.id)) return { label: "needs you", tone: "queued" };
  const ln = r.lane_map?.lanes.find((l) => l.items.includes(item.id));
  const rec = ln ? r.lanes.find((l) => l.id === ln.id) : undefined;
  if (status === "done" || rec?.state === "closed") return { label: "done", tone: "done" };
  if (rec && (rec.state === "finishing" || rec.state === "gating")) return { label: "checking", tone: "progress" };
  if (rec && laneBusy(rec)) return { label: "working", tone: "progress" };
  return { label: "in round", tone: "unknown" };
}

export interface CheckRowView { name: string; state: "pass" | "fail" | "run" | "queued"; word: string; dur: string }
const dur = (ms: number) => `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`;
const WORD = { pass: "passed", fail: "failed", run: "running", queued: "queued" } as const;

export const checkRows = (g: Gate): CheckRowView[] =>
  g.checks.map((c) => { const state = c.exit === 0 ? "pass" : "fail"; return { name: c.name, state, word: WORD[state], dur: dur(c.ms) }; });

export function finalCheckRows(r: RunRecord): CheckRowView[] {
  if (r.head_pending.length > 0) return r.head_pending.map((name, i) => ({ name, state: i === 0 ? "run" : "queued", word: WORD[i === 0 ? "run" : "queued"], dur: "" }));
  return r.head_check ? checkRows({ commit: r.head_check.commit, checks: r.head_check.checks, review: null, notice: null, fix: 0 }) : [];
}

export function gateLabel(laneId: string, g: Gate, inLane: boolean): string {
  const fix = g.fix > 0 ? `fix ${g.fix} · ` : "";
  return inLane ? cap(`${fix}checked after save`) : `Lane ${ID(laneId)} · ${fix}checked after save`;
}

export const reviewLine = (rv: Review) => (rv.pass ? "Reviewed: looks right" : `Reviewed: ${plural(rv.findings.length, "finding")}`);

export function leakParts(k: Leak): { lead: string; path: string; tail: string } {
  if (k.by) return { lead: `Lane ${ID(k.by)} also changed `, path: k.path, tail: ", which this lane owns — left unsaved for you." }; // (new copy)
  return { lead: "Also changed ", path: k.path, tail: k.owner ? `, which lane ${ID(k.owner)} owns — left unsaved for you.` : ", which no lane owns — left unsaved for you." };
}

export const laneItemTitles = (r: RunRecord, l: LaneRecord) =>
  (spec(r, l.id)?.items ?? []).map((i) => { const it = r.lane_map?.items.find((x) => x.id === i); return it?.rewrite ?? it?.title ?? i; }).join(" · ");
export const ownsLabel = (r: RunRecord, l: LaneRecord) => `Owns ${plural(spec(r, l.id)?.owns.length ?? 0, "file")}`;
export const laneDivider = (r: RunRecord, l: LaneRecord) => `lane ${ID(l.id)} · ${laneWord(r, l).word}`;

/** What a native notification says when a round needs the user or is saved, and they're away. */
export function transitionNotice(prev: RunRecord | null, next: RunRecord): string | null {
  if (prev?.state === next.state) return null;
  if (next.state === "needs-you") return `Round ${next.round ?? next.n} needs you.`;
  if (next.state === "done") return `Round ${next.round ?? next.n} is saved.`;
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The store**

Create `src/lib/runs-store.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * The run records the frontend knows of, by project and number. Rust is the
 * writer and pushes every change as `run-update`; this only keeps the newest
 * copy, lets React subscribe, and taps the user on the shoulder (a native
 * notification) when a round needs them or is saved while they're away.
 */
import { notifyNative } from "./ipc";
import { onRunUpdate, runsList } from "./runs-ipc";
import { runOver, transitionNotice, type RunRecord } from "./runs-model";

const byDir = new Map<string, Map<number, RunRecord>>();
const subs = new Set<() => void>();
let version = 0;
let listening = false;

function bump() {
  version++;
  for (const cb of subs) cb();
}

/** Keep the newer copy; `updated` is to the second, and a tie takes the incoming one. */
export function ingest(dir: string, rec: RunRecord): { accepted: boolean; prev: RunRecord | null } {
  const m = byDir.get(dir) ?? new Map<number, RunRecord>();
  const prev = m.get(rec.n) ?? null;
  if (prev && Date.parse(prev.updated) > Date.parse(rec.updated)) return { accepted: false, prev };
  m.set(rec.n, rec);
  byDir.set(dir, m);
  return { accepted: true, prev };
}

function ensure() {
  if (listening) return;
  listening = true;
  void onRunUpdate((u) => {
    const { accepted, prev } = ingest(u.dir, u.record);
    if (!accepted) return;
    const say = transitionNotice(prev, u.record);
    if (say && typeof document !== "undefined" && !document.hasFocus()) void notifyNative("Chronicle", say).catch(() => {});
    bump();
  });
}

export function subscribeRuns(cb: () => void): () => void {
  ensure();
  subs.add(cb);
  return () => { subs.delete(cb); };
}
export const runsVersion = () => version;
export const runFor = (dir: string, n: number): RunRecord | null => byDir.get(dir)?.get(n) ?? null;
export function liveRun(dir: string): RunRecord | null {
  return [...(byDir.get(dir)?.values() ?? [])].filter((r) => !runOver(r)).sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)[0] ?? null;
}
export function refreshRuns(dir: string): Promise<void> {
  ensure();
  return runsList(dir).then((list) => { for (const r of list) ingest(dir, r); bump(); }).catch(() => {});
}
```

```ts
/*
 * Runs in the agent store: a lane's wire lands in its own session keyed under
 * its run; a run's card enters the chat thread and its planner prompt is sent;
 * the planning turn's end starts the run, or sends the reasons and waits again.
 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

type Update = { dir: string; lane?: string; message: { method: string; params?: Record<string, unknown> } };
let onUpdate: ((u: Update) => void) | null = null;

vi.mock("./ipc", () => ({
  agentsAvailable: vi.fn(async () => ({ claude: null, codex: null, default: "" })),
  onAcpUpdate: vi.fn(async (cb: (u: Update) => void) => { onUpdate = cb; return () => {}; }),
  agentPrompt: vi.fn(async () => {}), roundRunMessage: vi.fn(async () => ""), agentSessionStart: vi.fn(async () => {}),
  roundPlanBegin: vi.fn(), roundPlanCancel: vi.fn(async () => {}), roundPlanSettle: vi.fn(),
  agentCancel: vi.fn(), agentEdits: vi.fn(async () => ({ files: [] })), agentHistoryRead: vi.fn(async () => ({ lines: [] })),
  agentSetConfigOption: vi.fn(), agentRespondPermission: vi.fn(), agentSessionResume: vi.fn(),
  agentSessionState: vi.fn(), agentSessionStop: vi.fn(), agentSessionsList: vi.fn(), agentSetMode: vi.fn(), designOutsideFiles: vi.fn(async () => []),
}));
vi.mock("./runs-ipc", () => ({
  runBegin: vi.fn(async () => ({ n: 10, total: 7, prompt: "PLAN IT" })),
  runPlanSettle: vi.fn(async () => ({ state: "running" })),
  runPlanCancel: vi.fn(async () => {}),
  runResume: vi.fn(async () => ({ state: "planning", prompt: "PLAN AGAIN" })),
}));
vi.mock("./runs-store", () => ({ refreshRuns: vi.fn(async () => {}) }));
vi.mock("./notes-store", () => ({
  indexFor: () => ({ notes: [], rounds: [], generation: 0, vault: "", borrowed: false }),
  refreshNotes: vi.fn(async () => {}), roundGenerating: vi.fn(() => false), roundNotesFor: vi.fn(() => []), setRoundGenerating: vi.fn(),
}));
vi.mock("./journal", () => ({ announce: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("@/overlays/toasts", () => ({ toastAction: vi.fn(), toastError: vi.fn() }));

const ipc = await import("./ipc");
const runsIpc = await import("./runs-ipc");
const agent = await import("./agent-session");
const DIR = "/home/u/proj";
const chunk = (text: string) => ({ method: "session/update", params: { update: { sessionUpdate: "agent_message_chunk", content: { type: "text", text } } } });
const usage = (amount: number) => ({ method: "session/update", params: { update: { sessionUpdate: "usage_update", used: 1, size: 10, cost: { amount } } } });
const turnEnd = () => ({ method: "_chronicle/turn_end", params: { stopReason: "end_turn" } });
const runCard = () => agent.agentSessionFor(DIR).entries.find((e) => e.kind === "run") as Extract<agent.AgentEntry, { kind: "run" }> | undefined;

describe("runs in the agent store", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    Object.assign(agent.agentSessionFor(DIR), { phase: "ready", turnActive: false, entries: [], queue: [], cost: 1.0 });
  });

  it("a lane's updates land in its own session, keyed under its run", () => {
    onUpdate!({ dir: DIR, lane: "w/10-2", message: chunk("lane says hi") });
    agent.flushAgentNotifications();
    const key = agent.laneKey(DIR, "w/10-2");
    expect(key).toBe(`${DIR}#w/10-2`);
    expect(agent.splitLaneKey(key)).toEqual({ dir: DIR, lane: "w/10-2" });
    expect(agent.splitLaneKey(`/a#b/p#w/1-0`)).toEqual({ dir: "/a#b/p", lane: "w/1-0" });
    expect(agent.agentSessionFor(key).entries[0]).toMatchObject({ kind: "assistant", text: "lane says hi" });
    expect(agent.agentSessionFor(DIR).entries).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("a decision rides on its guard row", () => {
    const key = agent.laneKey(DIR, "w/10-1");
    onUpdate!({ dir: DIR, lane: "w/10-1", message: { method: "_chronicle/guard", params: { allowed: true, path: "npx vitest run src/lib", reason: "it reads only",
      decision: { choice: "allow", confidence: 0.93, model: "jev-1.13.0", band: "act" } } } });
    expect(agent.agentSessionFor(key).entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ kind: "guard", allowed: true, decision: { confidence: 0.93, model: "jev-1.13.0" } });
  });

  it("a run lands its card and sends the planner prompt", async () => {
    expect(await agent.startRunInPane(DIR, { kind: "notes" })).toBe(10);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(ipc.agentPrompt).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(runCard()).toMatchObject({ kind: "run", n: 10, total: 7, queued: false, costBase: 1.0 });
    expect(vi.mocked(ipc.agentPrompt).mock.calls[0][2]).toBe("PLAN IT");
  });

  it("the planning turn's end starts the run with what the turn cost", async () => {
    await agent.startRunInPane(DIR, { kind: "notes" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(ipc.agentPrompt).toHaveBeenCalled());
    onUpdate!({ dir: DIR, lane: "chat", message: usage(1.26) });
    onUpdate!({ dir: DIR, lane: "chat", message: turnEnd() });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(runsIpc.runPlanSettle).toHaveBeenCalledWith(DIR, 10, expect.closeTo(0.26, 5)));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(runCard()).toMatchObject({ ended: true, outcome: "running" }));
  });

  it("a turn that declared no map sends the reasons and waits again", async () => {
    vi.mocked(runsIpc.runPlanSettle).mockResolvedValueOnce({ state: "retry", prompt: "FIX THE MAP" });
    await agent.startRunInPane(DIR, { kind: "notes" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(ipc.agentPrompt).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    onUpdate!({ dir: DIR, lane: "chat", message: turnEnd() });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(vi.mocked(ipc.agentPrompt).mock.calls.at(-1)?.[2]).toBe("FIX THE MAP"));
    expect(runCard()).toMatchObject({ ended: false, queued: false });
  });

  it("a stopped planning turn cancels the run", async () => {
    await agent.startRunInPane(DIR, { kind: "notes" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(ipc.agentPrompt).toHaveBeenCalled());
    onUpdate!({ dir: DIR, lane: "chat", message: { method: "_chronicle/turn_end", params: { stopReason: "cancelled" } } });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(runsIpc.runPlanCancel).toHaveBeenCalledWith(DIR, 10));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(runCard()).toMatchObject({ ended: true, outcome: "cancelled" }));
  });

  it("resuming a run caught while planning sends its planner prompt again", async () => {
    await agent.resumeRunInPane(DIR, 10);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(vi.mocked(ipc.agentPrompt).mock.calls.at(-1)?.[2]).toBe("PLAN AGAIN"));
    expect(runCard()).toMatchObject({ n: 10 });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The lane key**

In `src/lib/ipc.ts`, replace the `Lane` type and `splitLaneKey`:

```ts
/** The chat lane is keyed by the project dir alone; the design lane suffixes
 *  it with `#design`; a run's lane k of run n with `#w/<n>-<k>`. */
export type Lane = "chat" | "design" | `w/${number}-${number}`;
export function splitLaneKey(key: string): { dir: string; lane: Lane } {
  if (key.endsWith("#design")) return { dir: key.slice(0, -"#design".length), lane: "design" };
  const i = key.lastIndexOf("#w/");
  if (i >= 0) return { dir: key.slice(0, i), lane: key.slice(i + 1) as Lane };
  return { dir: key, lane: "chat" };
}
```

In `src/lib/agent-session.ts`, replace `laneKey` and `splitKey` with:

```ts
export const laneKey = (dir: string, lane: Lane): string => (lane === "chat" ? dir : `${dir}#${lane}`);
function splitKey(key: string): { dir: string; lane: Lane } {
  if (key.endsWith("#design")) return { dir: key.slice(0, -"#design".length), lane: "design" };
  const i = key.lastIndexOf("#w/");
  if (i >= 0) return { dir: key.slice(0, i), lane: key.slice(i + 1) as Lane };
  return { dir: key, lane: "chat" };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Starting, settling and resuming a run**

Add the imports `import { runBegin, runPlanCancel, runPlanSettle, runResume, type RunSource } from "./runs-ipc";` and `import { refreshRuns } from "./runs-store";`, then add below `startRoundInPane`:

```ts
type RunEntry = Extract<AgentEntry, { kind: "run" }>;

function runCardFor(dir: string, n: number): RunEntry | undefined {
  const es = agentSessionFor(dir).entries;
  for (let i = es.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const e = es[i]; if (e.kind === "run" && e.n === n) return e; }
  return undefined;
}

/**
 * A run (Runs §7): Chronicle opens the round, the planner writes the plan and
 * declares the lanes as a turn in this pane, and when that turn ends the run
 * starts — Rust drives the lanes from there — or is asked again, or fails.
 */
export async function startRunInPane(dir: string, source: RunSource): Promise<number> {
  const { n, total, prompt } = await runBegin(dir, source);
  if (source.kind === "notes") setRoundGenerating(dir, true);
  void refreshNotes(dir);
  void refreshRuns(dir);
  landRunPrompt(dir, n, total, prompt);
  return n;
}

/** Resume an interrupted run. One caught while planning sends its planner prompt again. */
export async function resumeRunInPane(dir: string, n: number): Promise<void> {
  const r = await runResume(dir, n);
  void refreshRuns(dir);
  if (r.state === "planning" && r.prompt) landRunPrompt(dir, n, 0, r.prompt);
}

/** A lane's transcript after a restart (its live wire is gone; its records are not). */
export async function loadLaneHistory(key: string, sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
  const r = await agentHistoryRead(key, sessionId).catch(() => null);
  const lines = Array.isArray(r?.lines) ? (r!.lines as unknown[]) : [];
  if (lines.length === 0) return;
  agentSessionFor(key).entries = reduceLines(key, lines).entries;
  notify();
}

/** The run card enters the thread and the planner's prompt goes out — now, or
 *  when the pane is free, by the same rules a round card follows. */
function landRunPrompt(dir: string, n: number, total: number, prompt: string): void {
  const s = agentSessionFor(dir);
  s.viewing = null;
  let over = false;
  const giveUp = () => {
    if (over) return;
    over = true;
    const e = runCardFor(dir, n);
    if (e && !e.ended) { e.ended = true; e.outcome = "cancelled"; touch(e); notify(); }
    void runPlanCancel(dir, n).catch(() => {});
    setRoundGenerating(dir, false);
    void refreshNotes(dir);
    void refreshRuns(dir);
  };
  const push = (queued: boolean) => {
    agentSessionFor(dir).entries.push({ kind: "run", rev: 0, n, total, queued, costBase: agentSessionFor(dir).cost ?? 0 });
    notify();
  };
  if (s.phase === "ready" && !s.turnActive && s.queue.length === 0) {
    push(false);
    void sendAgentMessage(dir, prompt).catch(giveUp);
    return;
  }
  let landed = false;
  const land = (queued: boolean) => { if (!landed) { landed = true; push(queued); } };
  const needsStart = s.phase !== "ready" && s.phase !== "installing" && s.phase !== "starting";
  const un = sendWhenPaneIsFree(dir, "run", prompt, { land, stillWanted: () => !over, abandon: () => { over = true; }, fail: giveUp });
  if (!needsStart) { land(true); return; }
  void startAgentSession(dir).then(() => { if (!over) land(true); }).catch(() => { un(); giveUp(); });
}

/** The planning turn ended. Rust reads what was declared and says what happens next. */
async function settleRunPlan(dir: string, entry: RunEntry, stopReason: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const s = agentSessionFor(dir);
  const cost = s.cost != null && entry.costBase != null ? Math.max(0, s.cost - entry.costBase) : 0;
  try {
    if (stopReason === "cancelled") {
      await runPlanCancel(dir, entry.n);
      entry.outcome = "cancelled";
    } else {
      const r = await runPlanSettle(dir, entry.n, cost);
      if (r.state === "retry" && r.prompt) {
        entry.ended = false;
        entry.queued = true;
        touch(entry);
        sendWhenPaneIsFree(dir, "run", r.prompt, {
          land: () => {},
          stillWanted: () => !entry.ended,
          abandon: () => {},
          fail: () => { entry.ended = true; entry.outcome = "failed"; touch(entry); notify(); },
        });
        notify();
        return;
      }
      entry.outcome = r.state === "running" ? "running" : "failed";
      if (r.state === "failed") toastError("The round didn’t start", (r.reason ?? "The plan couldn’t be split into lanes").slice(0, 110));
    }
  } catch (e) {
    entry.outcome = "failed";
    toastError("Couldn’t start the round", String(e).slice(0, 110));
  }
  touch(entry);
  setRoundGenerating(dir, false);
  notify();
  void refreshNotes(dir);
  void refreshRuns(dir);
}
```
