- [ ] **Step 2: The shared pieces**

Create `src/screens/agent/run-atoms.tsx`:

```tsx
/*
 * The pieces the run card, the lane view and the notes card share — each a
 * transcription of one gen.py helper (sw, mono, tchip, chat_btn, check_row,
 * decision_line, leak_notice, divider_label).
 */
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/chrome/atoms";
import { CheckGlyph, ErrorGlyph, SparkGlyph, WarnGlyph } from "@/components/chrome/icons";
import type { CheckRowView, Word } from "@/lib/runs-model";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const TONE: Record<Word["tone"], string> = {
  neutral: "text-state-neutral", success: "text-state-success", warn: "text-state-warn", error: "text-state-error", dim: "text-text-dim",
};

/** gen.py `sw`: a check, an error or a warn glyph, or a dot that pulses while running. */
export function RunWord({ w, size = 11.5 }: { w: Word; size?: number }) {
  return (
    <span className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-[5px] whitespace-nowrap", TONE[w.tone])} style={{ fontSize: size }}>
      {w.tone === "success" ? <CheckGlyph size={12} /> : w.tone === "error" ? <ErrorGlyph size={12} /> : w.tone === "warn" ? <WarnGlyph size={12} /> : (
        <span className="size-[5px] shrink-0 rounded-full bg-current" style={w.pulse ? { animation: "wv-pulse 1.6s ease-in-out infinite" } : undefined} />
      )}
      <span key={w.word} className="wv-word">{w.word}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

export const Mono = ({ children, className }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string }) => (
  <span className={cn("whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[10.5px] tabular-nums text-text-dim", className)}>{children}</span>
);

export const Chip = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
  <span className="inline-flex h-[18px] items-center whitespace-nowrap rounded-[5px] bg-fill-subtle px-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-text-subtle">{children}</span>
);

const BTN = {
  primary: "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-(--primary-hover)",
  secondary: "border-border-strong text-text-primary hover:bg-fill-hover",
  quiet: "border-border-hairline text-text-muted hover:bg-fill-hover hover:text-text-primary",
};
export function ChatBtn({ kind = "secondary", onClick, disabled, children }: { kind?: keyof typeof BTN; onClick?: () => void; disabled?: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled} className={cn("h-[26px] whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border px-[11px] text-[11.5px] font-medium disabled:opacity-50", BTN[kind])}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export function CheckRow({ row }: { row: CheckRowView }) {
  const lead = row.state === "pass" ? <span className="text-state-success"><CheckGlyph size={12} /></span>
    : row.state === "fail" ? <span className="text-state-error"><ErrorGlyph size={12} /></span>
    : row.state === "run" ? <Spinner size={10} />
    : <span className="inline-block size-2.5 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-border-strong" />;
  return (
    <div className="flex h-[26px] items-center gap-2">
      <span className="flex w-3 justify-center">{lead}</span>
      <Mono className="text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{row.name}</Mono>
      <span className={cn("text-[11.5px]", row.state === "fail" ? "text-state-error" : row.state === "queued" ? "text-text-dim" : "text-text-muted")}>{row.word}</span>
      <span className="flex-1" />
      <Mono>{row.dur}</Mono>
    </div>
  );
}

export function DecisionLine({ children, prob }: { children: ReactNode; prob: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="mx-3.5 my-0.5 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-md border border-border-hairline bg-surface-card px-[11px] py-[7px]">
      <span className="mt-px shrink-0 text-text-subtle"><SparkGlyph size={12} /></span>
      <span className="flex-1 text-xs leading-[1.55] text-text-muted">{children}</span>
      <Mono>{prob}</Mono>
    </div>
  );
}

export function LeakNotice({ parts }: { parts: { lead: string; path: string; tail: string } }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-2 ml-[18px] mr-0.5 flex items-start gap-[7px] rounded-md border border-border-hairline bg-fill-subtle px-2.5 py-[7px]">
      <span className="mt-px shrink-0 text-state-warn"><WarnGlyph size={12} /></span>
      <span className="text-[11.5px] leading-normal text-text-muted">{parts.lead}<Mono className="text-text-secondary">{parts.path}</Mono>{parts.tail}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

export function Divider({ label }: { label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-4 items-center gap-2 px-3.5">
      <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-divider" />
      <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-text-faint">{label}</span>
      <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-divider" />
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The run card**

Create `src/screens/agent/RunCard.tsx`:

```tsx
/*
 * The run card in the chat thread (boards R1, R3, R4): the plan row, the
 * latest decision, then one row per lane with the run's cost and elapsed time
 * in the header, the final check while it runs, the needs-you rows with their
 * one or two buttons, and one sentence saying what is happening. Every word
 * comes from runs-model.ts.
 */
import { useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { resumeRunInPane, type AgentEntry } from "@/lib/agent-session";
import { every } from "@/lib/scheduler";
import { runFor, runsVersion, subscribeRuns } from "@/lib/runs-store";
import { runNeedsYou, runStop, type NeedsAct } from "@/lib/runs-ipc";
import {
  decisionLine, elapsedMs, finalCheckRows, fmtCost, fmtElapsed, laneWord, lanesSummary, leakParts, needsYouRows,
  planRowText, runFooter, runOver, runTitle, runWord, tryLabel, type LaneRecord, type NeedsRow, type RunRecord,
} from "@/lib/runs-model";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/chrome/atoms";
import { CheckGlyph, ChevronRightGlyph, ErrorGlyph, LanesGlyph, SparkGlyph, WarnGlyph } from "@/components/chrome/icons";
import { toastError } from "@/overlays/toasts";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { ChatBtn, Chip, CheckRow, DecisionLine, LeakNotice, Mono, RunWord } from "./run-atoms";

type RunEntry = Extract<AgentEntry, { kind: "run" }>;

function PlanRow({ entry, rec, onOpenFile }: { entry: RunEntry; rec: RunRecord | null; onOpenFile?: (p: string) => void }) {
  const planned = rec?.lane_map != null;
  const failed = rec?.state === "failed" || entry.outcome === "failed" || entry.outcome === "cancelled";
  const lead = planned ? <span className="text-state-success"><CheckGlyph size={12} /></span>
    : failed ? <span className="text-state-error"><ErrorGlyph size={12} /></span> : <Spinner size={11} />;
  const text = failed && !planned
    ? entry.outcome === "cancelled" ? `Round ${entry.n} · stopped before the lanes were planned` /* (new copy) */ : rec ? runFooter(rec) : `Round ${entry.n} didn’t start` /* (new copy) */
    : planRowText(rec, entry.n, entry.total);
  return (
    <div className="mx-3.5 my-1 flex h-[38px] items-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] border border-border-hairline bg-surface-card-raised px-3.5">
      {lead}
      <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-[12.5px] text-text-primary">{entry.queued ? `${text} · waiting for the pane` : text}</span>
      <span className="flex-1" />
      {rec && rec.planner_cost_usd > 0 && <Mono className="text-[11px]">{fmtCost(rec.planner_cost_usd)}</Mono>}
      {planned && onOpenFile && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onOpenFile(rec!.plan_path)} className="whitespace-nowrap text-[11.5px] text-text-secondary hover:text-text-primary">Open the plan ›</button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function LaneRow({ rec, lane, last, onOpen }: { rec: RunRecord; lane: LaneRecord; last: boolean; onOpen: () => void }) {
  const spec = rec.lane_map?.lanes.find((l) => l.id === lane.id);
  const t = tryLabel(lane);
  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col", !last && "border-b border-divider")}>
      <button type="button" onClick={onOpen} data-run-lane={lane.id} className="flex h-[34px] items-center gap-2 rounded-sm px-0.5 text-left hover:bg-fill-hover">
        <Mono className="w-2.5 text-text-faint">{lane.id.toUpperCase()}</Mono>
        <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[12.5px] text-text-primary">{spec?.title}</span>
        <span className="flex gap-1">{spec?.items.map((i) => <Chip key={i}>{i}</Chip>)}</span>
        <span className="flex-1" />
        {t && <Mono>{t}</Mono>}
        <RunWord w={laneWord(rec, lane)} />
        {lane.cost_usd > 0 && <Mono className="text-[11px]">{fmtCost(lane.cost_usd)}</Mono>}
        <span className="text-text-dim"><ChevronRightGlyph size={11} /></span>
      </button>
      {lane.leak.map((k) => <LeakNotice key={`${k.path}:${k.by ?? ""}`} parts={leakParts(k)} />)}
    </div>
  );
}

function NeedsYouRow({ row, onAct }: { row: NeedsRow; onAct: (row: NeedsRow, act: NeedsRow["actions"][number]["act"], text?: string) => void }) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const buttons = (
    <div className="flex gap-1.5">
      {row.actions.map((a) => (
        <ChatBtn key={a.label} kind={a.primary ? "primary" : a.act === "open" ? "quiet" : "secondary"} onClick={() => (a.act === "rewrite" ? setDraft(row.title) : onAct(row, a.act))}>{a.label}</ChatBtn>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
  if (row.kind === "parked") {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-[7px] rounded-lg border border-border-hairline bg-surface-card px-3 py-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <RunWord w={{ word: "needs you", tone: "warn", pulse: false }} />
          {row.item && <Chip>{row.item}</Chip>}
          <span className="text-[12.5px] font-medium">{row.title}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs leading-[1.55] text-text-muted">{row.body}</div>
        {row.reading && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-text-subtle"><SparkGlyph size={12} /></span>
            <span className="flex-1 text-[11.5px] text-text-muted">likely impossible as written</span>
            <Mono>{row.reading}</Mono>
          </div>
        )}
        {draft === null ? buttons : (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <textarea value={draft} onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} rows={2}
              className="resize-none rounded-md border border-border-field bg-surface-input px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-text-primary outline-none" />
            <div className="flex gap-1.5">
              <ChatBtn kind="primary" disabled={!draft.trim()} onClick={() => onAct(row, "rewrite", draft)}>Run it as one more lane</ChatBtn>
              <ChatBtn kind="quiet" onClick={() => setDraft(null)}>Cancel</ChatBtn>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border-hairline bg-surface-card px-3 py-[9px]">
      <span className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-state-warn"><WarnGlyph size={12} /></span>
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-[3px]">
        <span className="text-[12.5px]">{row.title}</span>
        {row.body && <span className="text-[11.5px] text-text-muted">{row.body}</span>}
        {row.mono && <pre className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-surface-input px-2.5 py-2 font-mono text-[10.5px] leading-[1.6] text-text-muted">{row.mono}</pre>}
      </div>
      {buttons}
    </div>
  );
}

export function RunEntryCard({ dir, entry, onOpenLane, onOpenFile }: {
  dir: string; entry: RunEntry; onOpenLane: (n: number, lane: string) => void; onOpenFile?: (path: string) => void;
}) {
  useSyncExternalStore(subscribeRuns, runsVersion, runsVersion);
  const rec = runFor(dir, entry.n);
  const live = rec != null && !runOver(rec) && rec.state !== "interrupted";
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => (live ? every(1000, () => setNow(Date.now())) : undefined), [live]);
  const act = (row: NeedsRow, a: NeedsRow["actions"][number]["act"], text?: string) => {
    if (a === "open") { if (row.lane) onOpenLane(entry.n, row.lane); return; }
    const payload: NeedsAct = a === "skip" ? { kind: "skip", item: row.item! } : a === "rewrite" ? { kind: "rewrite", item: row.item!, text: text ?? "" }
      : a === "retry" ? { kind: "retry", lane: row.lane! } : a === "recheck" ? { kind: "recheck" } : { kind: "dismiss", index: row.index };
    runNeedsYou(dir, entry.n, payload).catch((e) => toastError("Couldn’t do that", String(e).slice(0, 110)));
  };
  const decision = rec ? decisionLine(rec) : null;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <PlanRow entry={entry} rec={rec} onOpenFile={onOpenFile} />
      {decision && live && (
        <DecisionLine prob={decision.prob}>{decision.lead} <Mono className="text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{decision.command}</Mono> {decision.tail}</DecisionLine>
      )}
      {rec?.lane_map && (
        <div data-run-card className="mx-3.5 my-1 flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-[10px] border border-border-hairline bg-surface-card-raised px-3.5 pb-2.5 pt-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
            <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold">{runTitle(rec, entry.total)}</span>
            <RunWord w={runWord(rec)} size={12} />
            <span className="flex-1" />
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-[5px] text-[11.5px] text-text-muted"><LanesGlyph size={12} />{lanesSummary(rec)}</span>
            <Mono className="text-[11px] text-text-muted">{fmtCost(rec.cost.usd)}</Mono>
            <Mono className="text-[11px]">{fmtElapsed(elapsedMs(rec, now))}</Mono>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            {rec.lanes.map((l, i) => (
              <LaneRow key={l.id} rec={rec} lane={l} last={i === rec.lanes.length - 1 && rec.state !== "gating"} onOpen={() => onOpenLane(entry.n, l.id)} />
            ))}
            {(rec.state === "gating" || rec.state === "closing") && (
              <div className="mt-1.5 border-t border-divider px-0.5 pb-0.5 pt-2">
                <div className="flex h-[26px] items-center gap-2">
                  <Spinner size={11} />
                  <span className="text-[12.5px]">Checking everything together</span>
                  <span className="flex-1" />
                  <Mono className="text-[11px]">{finalCheckRows(rec).length} checks</Mono>
                </div>
                <div className="ml-[19px]">{finalCheckRows(rec).map((c) => <CheckRow key={c.name} row={c} />)}</div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          {rec.needs_you.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
              {needsYouRows(rec).map((row) => <NeedsYouRow key={`${row.kind}:${row.index}`} row={row} onAct={act} />)}
            </div>
          )}
          {rec.notices.at(-1) && <span className="text-[11px] leading-normal text-text-faint">{rec.notices.at(-1)}</span>}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pt-0.5">
            <span className="flex-1 text-[11.5px] leading-normal text-text-muted">{runFooter(rec)}</span>
            {rec.state === "interrupted" ? (
              <ChatBtn kind="primary" onClick={() => void resumeRunInPane(dir, entry.n).catch((e) => toastError("Couldn’t resume the round", String(e).slice(0, 110)))}>Resume</ChatBtn>
            ) : live ? (
              <ChatBtn kind="quiet" onClick={() => void runStop(dir, entry.n).catch((e) => toastError("Couldn’t stop the round", String(e).slice(0, 110)))}>Stop</ChatBtn>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The lane view's pieces**

Create `src/screens/agent/LaneThread.tsx`:

```tsx
/*
 * A lane opened (board R2): back to the run, the lane's title and state, its
 * items, the files it owns, and — after its thread, which AgentPane renders
 * with the same rows as the chat — its gate cards: the checks after each
 * save, the failing output, the review. A stopped lane offers Discard.
 */
import { useState } from "react";
import { Eyebrow } from "@/components/chrome/atoms";
import { CheckGlyph, ChevronLeftGlyph, ChevronRightGlyph, WarnGlyph } from "@/components/chrome/icons";
import { runLaneDiscard } from "@/lib/runs-ipc";
import {
  checkRows, fmtCost, fmtElapsed, laneElapsed, laneItemTitles, laneWord, ownsLabel, reviewLine,
  type Gate, type LaneRecord, type RunRecord,
} from "@/lib/runs-model";
import { toastError, toastSuccess } from "@/overlays/toasts";
import { ChatBtn, Chip, CheckRow, Mono, RunWord } from "./run-atoms";

export function LaneHeader({ dir, rec, lane, onBack, now }: { dir: string; rec: RunRecord; lane: LaneRecord; onBack: () => void; now: number }) {
  const spec = rec.lane_map?.lanes.find((l) => l.id === lane.id);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true);
  const discard = () =>
    runLaneDiscard(dir, rec.n, lane.id)
      .then((k) => toastSuccess("Discarded", `${k} ${k === 1 ? "file" : "files"} put back`))
      .catch((e) => toastError("Couldn’t discard", String(e).slice(0, 110)));
  return (
    <div className="mx-3.5 mb-0.5 mt-1 flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={onBack} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11.5px] text-text-secondary hover:text-text-primary"><ChevronLeftGlyph size={11} />Back to the run</button>
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <Mono className="text-text-faint">Round {rec.round ?? rec.n} · lane {lane.id.toUpperCase()}</Mono>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">{spec?.title}</span>
        <RunWord w={laneWord(rec, lane)} />
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <Mono className="text-[11px]">{fmtCost(lane.cost_usd)} · {fmtElapsed(laneElapsed(lane, now))}</Mono>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
        {spec?.items.map((i) => <Chip key={i}>{i}</Chip>)}
        <span className="ml-1 text-[11.5px] text-text-muted">{laneItemTitles(rec, lane)}</span>
      </div>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex h-[22px] items-center gap-1.5">
        <span className="text-text-dim transition-transform duration-(--dur-fast)" style={{ transform: open ? "rotate(90deg)" : undefined }}><ChevronRightGlyph size={11} /></span>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-text-faint">{ownsLabel(rec, lane)}</span>
      </button>
      {open && <div className="-mt-1 mb-1 ml-[17px] flex flex-col gap-[3px]">{spec?.owns.map((p) => <Mono key={p} className="text-text-subtle">{p}</Mono>)}</div>}
      {lane.state === "cancelled" && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="flex-1 text-[11.5px] text-text-muted">Stopped. This lane’s unsaved changes are still in your project.</span>
          <ChatBtn kind="quiet" onClick={discard}>Discard them</ChatBtn>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function GateCard({ label, gate }: { label: string; gate: Gate }) {
  const failed = gate.checks.find((c) => c.exit !== 0);
  const rv = gate.review;
  return (
    <div className="mx-3.5 mb-1 mt-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border-hairline bg-surface-card px-3 py-2">
      <div className="mb-0.5 flex items-center gap-2"><Eyebrow>{label}</Eyebrow></div>
      {checkRows(gate).map((r) => <CheckRow key={r.name} row={r} />)}
      {failed?.tail && <div className="mt-1.5 overflow-hidden whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-surface-input px-2.5 py-2 font-mono text-[10.5px] leading-[1.6] text-text-muted">{failed.tail}</div>}
      {gate.notice && <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-text-faint">{gate.notice}</div>}
      {rv && (
        <div className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-divider pt-1">
          <div className="flex h-[26px] items-center gap-2">
            <span className={rv.pass ? "flex w-3 justify-center text-state-success" : "flex w-3 justify-center text-state-warn"}>{rv.pass ? <CheckGlyph size={12} /> : <WarnGlyph size={12} />}</span>
            <span className="text-[11.5px] text-text-muted">{reviewLine(rv)}</span>
          </div>
          {!rv.pass && rv.findings.map((f, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="ml-5 text-[11.5px] leading-[1.5] text-text-muted">{f.file && <Mono className="text-text-secondary">{f.file}:{f.line}</Mono>} {f.what}</div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
/** A lane opened (board R2): its header, its thread, its gate cards, and a composer that nudges it. */
function LaneView({ dir, n, id, onBack, onConfirm }: { dir: string; n: number; id: string; onBack: () => void; onConfirm: (spec: ConfirmSpec) => void }) {
  useSyncExternalStore(subscribeRuns, runsVersion, runsVersion);
  useSyncExternalStore(subscribeAgent, agentVersion, agentVersion);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => every(1000, () => setNow(Date.now())), []);
  const rec = runFor(dir, n);
  const lane = rec?.lanes.find((l) => l.id === id);
  const k = Math.max(0, rec?.lane_map?.lanes.findIndex((l) => l.id === id) ?? 0);
  const key = laneKey(dir, `w/${n}-${k}` as Lane);
  const ls = agentSessionFor(key);
  useEffect(() => { if (lane?.session && agentSessionFor(key).entries.length === 0) void loadLaneHistory(key, lane.session); }, [key, lane?.session]);
  if (!rec || !lane) return null;
  const items = groupEntries(ls.entries);
  const keys = rowKeys(items);
  const who = `Lane ${id.toUpperCase()}`;
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <div className="thread-scroller flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto py-2">
        <LaneHeader dir={dir} rec={rec} lane={lane} onBack={onBack} now={now} />
        <Divider label={laneDivider(rec, lane)} />
        {items.map((entry, i) => (
          <Appear key={keys[i]}>
            <ThreadRow sig={rowSignature(entry)} dir={key} entry={entry} turnActive={ls.turnActive} onConfirm={onConfirm} readOnly who={who} />
          </Appear>
        ))}
        {lane.gates.length > 0 && <Divider label="saved · checked" />}
        {lane.gates.map((g, i) => <GateCard key={i} label={gateLabel(lane.id, g, true)} gate={g} />)}
      </div>
      <Composer dir={key} nudge disabled={!(laneBusy(lane) || lane.state === "waiting")} onConfirm={onConfirm}
        placeholder={`Nudge lane ${id.toUpperCase()} — it reads this between steps`}
        onSend={(t) => runLaneNudge(dir, n, id, t)} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

  (`ThreadRow`'s comparator already ignores handler props; add `who` to what it forwards — it spreads `rest`, so nothing else changes.)

In `src/screens/notes/RoundCard.tsx`: import `runStop` from `@/lib/runs-ipc`; `noteChip, runOver, runSubline, runWord, type RunRecord` from `@/lib/runs-model`; `RunWord` from `@/screens/agent/run-atoms`. Add `run: RunRecord | null;` to `RoundCardData` ("the run driving this round, when there is one — the card mirrors it, board R4"). In `RoundCard`'s body, directly before its `return (` (after `stopPlan`, so every hook has already run):

```tsx
  if (round.run) {
    const run = round.run;
    const chips = notes.map((note) => noteChip(run, note.path, note.status) ?? rowStatus(note, phase));
    const doneNow = chips.filter((c) => c.label === "done").length;
    const stopRun = () => {
      setBusy(true);
      (agentSessionFor(dir).turnActive && run.state === "planning" ? cancelAgentTurn(dir) : Promise.resolve())
        .then(() => runStop(dir, run.n))
        .catch((e) => toastError("Couldn't stop it", String(e).slice(0, 90)))
        .finally(() => setBusy(false));
    };
    return (
      <div className="mx-2 mb-2 mt-1 rounded-lg border border-border-strong bg-surface-card px-3 py-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-text-primary">Round {n} · {kind}</span>
          <RunWord w={runWord(run)} />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-text-muted">{runSubline(run, doneNow, total)}</div>
        {!runOver(run) && total > 0 && (
          <div className="my-2.5 h-[2px] overflow-hidden rounded-[1px] bg-fill-subtle">
            <div className="h-full rounded-[1px] bg-state-neutral" style={{ width: `${Math.round((doneNow / total) * 100)}%` }} />
          </div>
        )}
        {total > 0 && (
          <div className={cn("flex flex-col text-[12.5px] text-text-secondary", runOver(run) && "mt-2")}>
            {notes.map((note, i) => (
              <TreeRow key={note.path} name={note.title} marquee
                icon={<DocGlyph size={13} strokeWidth={1.2} className="shrink-0 text-text-subtle" />}
                selected={note.path === openPath} trailing={<StatusChip {...chips[i]} />} onClick={() => onOpenNote(note.path)} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="mt-2.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {!runOver(run) && onRevealPane && <button type="button" onClick={onRevealPane} className={BTN}>Open the pane</button>}
          {!runOver(run) && <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={stopRun} className={BTN}>Stop</button>}
          {runOver(run) && <button type="button" onClick={() => dismissRound(dir, n)} className={BTN}>Dismiss</button>}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
```

```ts
/** The newest round whose plan is written and not yet run. */
function newestReadyRound(dir: string): number | null {
  const ready = indexFor(dir).rounds.filter((r) => r.state === "ready").map((r) => r.n);
  return ready.length ? Math.max(...ready) : null;
}
```

and append to `LOCAL_COMMANDS`:

```ts
  {
    name: "run-plan",
    description: "Run the newest written plan as a round, in lanes",
    enabled: (dir) => newestReadyRound(dir) != null && liveRun(dir) == null,
    run: (dir) => {
      const n = newestReadyRound(dir);
      if (n == null) return Promise.reject(new Error("there's no written plan to run"));
      return startRunInPane(dir, { kind: "plan", path: `fixes/phase_${n}_fixes_plan.md` });
    },
  },
```

```ts
/*
 * The replay harness for runs, as round 9's was for subagents: a run's records
 * fed through the store in order, the card's words read at each one; and a
 * lane's transcript fed through the one reducer, the lane view's rows counted.
 * The fixtures are hand-built to the boards until the live check (Task 18)
 * replaces them with recorded excerpts; numbers here change on purpose only.
 */
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { RunRecord } from "./runs-model";

vi.mock("./runs-ipc", () => ({
  onRunUpdate: vi.fn(async () => () => {}), runsList: vi.fn(async () => []),
  runBegin: vi.fn(), runPlanSettle: vi.fn(), runPlanCancel: vi.fn(), runResume: vi.fn(),
}));
vi.mock("./ipc", () => ({
  notifyNative: vi.fn(), agentsAvailable: vi.fn(async () => ({ claude: null, codex: null, default: "" })), onAcpUpdate: vi.fn(async () => () => {}),
  agentPrompt: vi.fn(), roundRunMessage: vi.fn(), agentSessionStart: vi.fn(), roundPlanBegin: vi.fn(), roundPlanCancel: vi.fn(), roundPlanSettle: vi.fn(),
  agentCancel: vi.fn(), agentEdits: vi.fn(), agentHistoryRead: vi.fn(), agentSetConfigOption: vi.fn(), agentRespondPermission: vi.fn(),
  agentSessionResume: vi.fn(), agentSessionState: vi.fn(), agentSessionStop: vi.fn(), agentSessionsList: vi.fn(), agentSetMode: vi.fn(),
}));
vi.mock("./notes-store", () => ({
  indexFor: () => ({ notes: [], rounds: [], generation: 0, vault: "", borrowed: false }),
  refreshNotes: vi.fn(), roundGenerating: vi.fn(() => false), roundNotesFor: vi.fn(() => []), setRoundGenerating: vi.fn(),
}));
vi.mock("./journal", () => ({ announce: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("@/overlays/toasts", () => ({ toastAction: vi.fn(), toastError: vi.fn() }));

const store = await import("./runs-store");
const M = await import("./runs-model");
const { groupEntries, reduceLines } = await import("./agent-session");
const read = (f: string) => readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "__fixtures__", f), "utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean).map((l) => JSON.parse(l));

describe("a run's records replay into the card, board by board", () => {
  const updates = read("run-10.jsonl") as { dir: string; record: RunRecord }[];
  const at = (i: number) => { store.ingest(updates[i].dir, updates[i].record); return store.runFor(updates[i].dir, 10)!; };
  it("keeps the fixture honest", () => {
    expect(updates).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(updates.map((u) => u.record.state)).toEqual(["running", "running", "running", "gating", "done"]);
  });
  it("R1 · working", () => {
    const r = at(0);
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Nothing needs you. Chronicle saves each lane as it passes.");
    expect(M.lanesSummary(r)).toBe("3 of 4 lanes");
  });
  it("R3.1 · a check fails → fixing", () => {
    expect(M.runFooter(at(1))).toBe("Lane C’s typecheck failed. It is fixing that now — fix 1 of 2.");
  });
  it("R3.2 · the fix passes", () => {
    const r = at(2);
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Lane C passed on its fix. Two lanes still working.");
    expect(M.fmtCost(r.cost.usd)).toBe("$2.02");
  });
  it("R3.3 · the final check", () => {
    const r = at(3);
    expect(M.runWord(r).word).toBe("final check");
    expect(M.finalCheckRows(r).map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(["test", "typecheck", "build", "cargo test"]);
  });
  it("R3.4 · saved", () => {
    const r = at(4);
    expect(M.runFooter(r)).toBe("Saved as round 10. 7 items done.");
    expect(M.fmtElapsed(M.elapsedMs(r, Date.now()))).toBe("11m 40s");
  });
});

describe("a lane's transcript replays into the lane view (board R2)", () => {
  const s = reduceLines("/home/u/proj#w/10-2", read("run-lane.jsonl"));
  const items = groupEntries(s.entries);
  it("opens with Chronicle's instruction and the lane's own words", () => {
    expect(items[0]).toMatchObject({ kind: "user", text: "You are lane C of round 10: Scroll anchoring." });
    expect(items[1]).toMatchObject({ kind: "assistant" });
  });
  it("folds the subagent's three calls under its card", () => {
    const sub = items.find((e) => e.kind === "subagent");
    expect(sub && sub.kind === "subagent" ? sub.children.map((c) => c.toolCallId) : []).toEqual(["c4", "c5", "c6"]);
  });
  it("keeps Chronicle's two answers as guard rows — one judged, one refused", () => {
    const guards = s.entries.filter((e) => e.kind === "guard");
    expect(guards).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(guards[0]).toMatchObject({ allowed: true, decision: { confidence: 0.88 } });
    expect(guards[1]).toMatchObject({ allowed: false, reason: "Chronicle commits for you" });
  });
  it("shows the rest as tool cards, in order", () => {
    expect(s.entries.filter((e) => e.kind === "tool").map((e) => (e.kind === "tool" ? e.toolCallId : ""))).toEqual(["c1", "c2", "c3", "c4", "c5", "c6", "c8"]);
  });
});
```

### Task 17: The gated end-to-end test against the real adapter

**Files:**
- Create: `src-tauri/src/runs/live_test.rs`
- Modify: `src-tauri/src/runs/mod.rs` (`#[cfg(test)] mod live_test;`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `runner::{RunCtl, CtlParts, LaneHost, Ev, spawn}`, `host::{lane_event, launch_worker}`, `tools::{declare, lane_server, handle}`, `commands::{settle_in}`, `bridge::listen_at`, `oracle::{Oracle, Fallback}`, Plan C's `providers::{Decision, DecisionRequest, Criterion, Answer}`.
- Produces: `runs_real_adapter_two_lanes_a_leak_and_a_block`, `#[ignore]`d and gated on `CHRONICLE_RUNS_TEST=1`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `src-tauri/src/runs/live_test.rs`:

```rust
//! The real adapter, end to end (spec §12): a toy repo, two lanes, a
//! deliberate shell leak, one `block` claim. The judge and the reviewer are a
//! rule-based stand-in (the subject here is the lanes, the gate and the
//! tools, not a model's taste). Gated like `acp_real_adapter_round_trip`; run:
//!   cd src-tauri && cargo build && CHRONICLE_RUNS_TEST=1 CHRONICLE_BIN=$PWD/target/debug/chronicle \
//!     cargo test runs_real_adapter -- --ignored --nocapture
use super::checks::Check;
use super::judge::Judge;
use super::oracle::{Fallback, Oracle, DECISIONS_FALLBACK, REVIEW_FALLBACK};
use super::record::*;
use super::runner::{self, CtlParts, Ev, LaneHost, RunCtl};
use super::{commands, gate, tools, Runs};
use crate::acp::{self, LanePolicy};
use crate::providers::{Answer, Criterion, Decision, DecisionRequest};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

/// Allows every shell command, parks every block, triages to none, aims for two lanes, passes every review.
struct Rules;
impl Oracle for Rules {
    fn decide(&self, req: DecisionRequest) -> Result<Decision, String> {
        let mut answers = HashMap::new();
        for c in &req.criteria {
            match c {
                Criterion::Choice { name, options } if name == "action" => { answers.insert(name.clone(), Answer::Choice { choice: "allow".into(), probabilities: options.iter().map(|o| (o.clone(), if o == "allow" { 0.95 } else { 0.025 })).collect(), confidence: 0.95 }); }
                Criterion::Choice { name, .. } => { answers.insert(name.clone(), Answer::Choice { choice: "none".into(), probabilities: HashMap::new(), confidence: 0.9 }); }
                Criterion::Noul { name } if name == "writes_outside" => { answers.insert(name.clone(), Answer::Noul { yes: 0.05 }); }
                Criterion::Noul { name } => { answers.insert(name.clone(), Answer::Noul { yes: 0.9 }); }
                Criterion::Score { name, .. } => { answers.insert(name.clone(), Answer::Score { value: 2, confidence: 0.9 }); }
            }
        }
        Ok(Decision { answers, model: "rules".into(), self_reported: false })
    }
    fn complete_json(&self, _s: &str, _p: &str, schema: &Value) -> Result<(Value, String), String> {
        if schema.pointer("/properties/pass").is_some() { return Ok((json!({ "pass": true, "findings": [] }), "rules".into())); }
        Ok((json!({ "answer": "Do what the item says, within your files." }), "rules".into()))
    }
}

/// `AcpHost`, minus the app: the lane's run server is the built chronicle binary.
struct TestHost { agents: Arc<acp::AcpState>, bin: PathBuf }
impl TestHost {
    fn key(ctl: &RunCtl, k: u16) -> String { acp::Lane::Worker { run: ctl.n, k }.key(&ctl.dir.to_string_lossy()) }
}
impl LaneHost for TestHost {
    fn start(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, lane: &str, k: u16, policy: Arc<dyn LanePolicy>) -> Result<(), String> {
        let (tx, id) = (ctl.tx.clone(), lane.to_string());
        let emit: acp::Emit = Arc::new(move |v: Value| { let _ = tx.send(super::host::lane_event(&id, &v)); });
        let extras = acp::LaneExtras { mcp_servers: vec![tools::lane_server(&ctl.dir, &self.bin, ctl.n, lane)], policy: Some(acp::PolicyHandle(policy)), ..Default::default() };
        super::host::launch_worker(&self.agents, emit, Self::key(ctl, k), acp::Lane::Worker { run: ctl.n, k }, &ctl.dir, extras)
    }
    fn prompt(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, _lane: &str, k: u16, text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.agents.get(&Self::key(ctl, k)).ok_or("no session")?.prompt(vec![json!({ "type": "text", "text": text })], text.lines().next().unwrap_or("").into())
    }
    fn cancel(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, _lane: &str, k: u16) { if let Some(s) = self.agents.get(&Self::key(ctl, k)) { let _ = s.cancel(); } }
    fn stop(&self, ctl: &Arc<RunCtl>, _lane: &str, k: u16) { if let Some(s) = self.agents.get(&Self::key(ctl, k)) { s.stop(false); } }
}

fn toy_repo() -> PathBuf {
    let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-runs-live-{}", std::process::id()));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join("fixes")).unwrap();
    let d = d.canonicalize().unwrap();
    for a in [&["init", "-q"][..], &["config", "user.email", "t@t"], &["config", "user.name", "t"], &["config", "commit.gpgsign", "false"]] { gate::git(&d, a).unwrap(); }
    std::fs::write(d.join("README.md"), "toy\n").unwrap();
    std::fs::write(d.join(".gitignore"), ".chronicle/\n").unwrap();
    std::fs::write(d.join("chronicle.json"), r#"{ "checks": [{ "name": "test", "command": "test -f README.md" }] }"#).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(d.join("fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md"), "Round kind: feature additions\n\n- I-1: Create a/alpha.txt containing the word alpha. Then run exactly this shell command: echo leak >> NOTES.txt\n- I-2: Create b/beta.txt containing the word beta.\n- I-3: Rename the file b/does-not-exist.txt to b/renamed.txt. (That file does not exist; if you can't do the item as written, say so with chronicle_run_block.)\n").unwrap();
    gate::git(&d, &["add", "README.md", ".gitignore", "chronicle.json", "fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md"]).unwrap();
    gate::git(&d, &["commit", "-qm", "toy"]).unwrap();
    crate::notes::rounds::save(&d, &[crate::notes::rounds::Round { n: 1, state: "generating".into(), kind: None, task_ids: vec![], note_paths: vec![],
        created_at: 0, plan_path: "fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md".into(), prompt_path: "fixes/phase_1_fixes_prompt.md".into() }]).unwrap();
    d
}

#[test]
#[ignore]
fn runs_real_adapter_two_lanes_a_leak_and_a_block() {
    if std::env::var("CHRONICLE_RUNS_TEST").ok().as_deref() != Some("1") { eprintln!("skipped: set CHRONICLE_RUNS_TEST=1"); return; }
    let Ok(bin) = std::env::var("CHRONICLE_BIN") else { eprintln!("skipped: set CHRONICLE_BIN to the built target/debug/chronicle"); return; };
    let d = toy_repo();
    let p = crate::load_project(&d);
    // the bridge the lanes' run servers dial, answered by the same handler the app uses
    let sock = d.join(".chronicle/test.sock");
    let token_file = d.join(".chronicle/test.token");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(d.join(".chronicle")).unwrap();
    std::env::set_var("CHRONICLE_BRIDGE_SOCKET", &sock);
    std::env::set_var("CHRONICLE_BRIDGE_TOKEN", &token_file);
    let token = crate::bridge::write_token_at(&token_file).unwrap();
    let runs = Arc::new(Runs::new());
    let (r2, p2) = (runs.clone(), p.clone());
    crate::bridge::listen_at(&sock, token, Arc::new(move |req: crate::bridge::Request| tools::handle(&r2, &p2, &req.action, &req.args))).unwrap();
    // the planner's map, declared through the real tool path
    let mut rec = RunRecord::new(1, RunKind::Plan, Some(1), "fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md", "fixes/phase_1_fixes_prompt.md", "fixes/phase_1_fixes_plan.md");
    save(&d, &mut rec).unwrap();
    tools::declare(&runs, &p, &json!({
        "items": [{ "id": "I-1", "title": "alpha" }, { "id": "I-2", "title": "beta" }, { "id": "I-3", "title": "rename" }],
        "lanes": [{ "id": "a", "title": "Alpha", "items": ["I-1"], "owns": ["a/**"], "checks": ["test"] },
                  { "id": "b", "title": "Beta", "items": ["I-2", "I-3"], "owns": ["b/**"], "checks": ["test"] }] })).unwrap();
    let commands::Settle::Launch(rec) = commands::settle_in(&p, 1, 0.0).unwrap() else { panic!("the declared map launches") };
    let rules: Arc<dyn Oracle> = Arc::new(Rules);
    let (ctl, rx) = RunCtl::new(CtlParts {
        dir: d.clone(), repo: d.clone(), scratch: std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("chronicle-runs-live-scratch-{}", std::process::id())), rec,
        checks: vec![Check { name: "test".into(), command: "test -f README.md".into() }], max_lanes: 3,
        judge: Judge::new(Fallback::new(rules.clone(), rules.clone(), DECISIONS_FALLBACK)),
        reviewer: Fallback::new(rules.clone(), rules, REVIEW_FALLBACK),
        host: Arc::new(TestHost { agents: Arc::new(acp::AcpState::new()), bin: PathBuf::from(&bin) }), publish: runs.publisher(),
    });
    runs.insert(ctl.clone());
    runner::spawn(ctl.clone(), rx, vec![]);
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(900);
    let held = loop {
        let r = ctl.snapshot();
        if matches!(r.state, RunState::NeedsYou | RunState::Done | RunState::Failed | RunState::Stopped) { break r; }
        assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "the run never settled: {r:#?}");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2));
    };
    eprintln!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&held).unwrap());
    for id in ["a", "b"] {
        let l = held.lane(id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(l.state, LaneState::Closed, "lane {id}");
        assert!(l.commit.is_some(), "lane {id} was saved");
    }
    assert!(gate::git(&d, &["log", "--name-only", "--format="]).unwrap().contains("a/alpha.txt"));
    assert!(gate::git(&d, &["log", "--name-only", "--format="]).unwrap().contains("b/beta.txt"));
    assert!(held.lane("a").unwrap().leak.iter().any(|k| k.path == "NOTES.txt"), "the shell write is the lane's leak");
    assert!(gate::git(&d, &["status", "--porcelain"]).unwrap().contains("?? NOTES.txt"), "and is left unsaved");
    assert!(held.needs_you.iter().any(|n| n.kind == "parked" && n.item.as_deref() == Some("I-3")), "the block claim was parked for the user");
    assert_eq!(held.state, RunState::NeedsYou);
    let _ = ctl.tx.send(Ev::Stop);
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the release build; a throwaway project; the window capture and click recipe in memory (`release-binary-build-and-window-capture.md`).
- Produces: the live-check table, recorded fixtures, and the roadmap proof — RN-3's `done_when` is a commit subject matching `^docs\(runs\): runs live check`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Build the release binary and a throwaway project (never this repo)**

```bash
export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH"
cd src-tauri && cargo build --release --features tauri/custom-protocol && cd ..
rm -rf ~/chronicle-runs-live && mkdir -p ~/chronicle-runs-live/src && cd ~/chronicle-runs-live && git init -q
printf '{ "name": "toy", "scripts": { "test": "node --test" } }\n' > package.json
printf 'export const greet = (n) => `hi ${n}`;\n' > src/greet.js
printf 'import { test } from "node:test"; import assert from "node:assert"; import { greet } from "../src/greet.js";\ntest("greet", () => assert.equal(greet("a"), "hi a"));\n' > src/greet.test.js
printf '{ "name": "toy", "checks": [{ "name": "test", "command": "npm run test" }], "maxLanes": 2 }\n' > chronicle.json
git add package.json src chronicle.json && git commit -qm toy && cd -
```

(The `git add` and `git commit` above run in the throwaway repo the step just created.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Drive the four boards and the edges, and record them**

Launch `src-tauri/target/release/chronicle`, open `~/chronicle-runs-live`, and in Notes create four queued notes: (1) "Add a farewell function beside greet, with a test"; (2) "Make greet trim its input, with a test"; (3) "Rename src/missing.js to src/found.js" (impossible as written); (4) "Add a CHANGELOG entry, then run npm publish". Then, capturing the window by id (`screencapture -x -l <id>`) at each moment:

| Moment | What must be true |
|---|---|
| Run a round | the plan row reads `Planning round 1 · 4 notes`, then `Planned round 1 · 4 items into N lanes` with the planner's cost |
| R1 | the run card's header, lane rows, the decision line for the first judged command; the notes card mirrors `running` and `N lanes working` |
| R2 | opening a lane shows its header, its thread (subagent tree if any), its gate card after its save |
| R3 | a lane's failing check (make note 2's test fail once by hand in the lane's file while it works, if it doesn't fail on its own) → fixing → the fix passes → the final check → saved; `git log` in the toy repo shows one commit per lane and `Close FX-1` |
| R4 | note 3 parked with the reading and the two buttons; note 4's publish refused as a needs-you row; the notes card reads `needs you · …` |
| Resume | quit the app while a lane works; reopen: the card reads `interrupted`, Resume continues and nothing is saved twice |
| Stop | start a second round and Stop it: lanes read `stopped`, their files stay, Discard in a lane view puts back that lane's files only |

Then copy the run's record snapshots and one lane's transcript into the fixtures: for each `run-update` you captured (Safari Web Inspector → Network is not needed: read `~/chronicle-runs-live/.chronicle/runs/1.json` at the five moments above) write one `{"dir": …, "record": …}` line into `run-10.jsonl`, with home paths replaced by `/home/u`; take the lane transcript from `~/chronicle-runs-live/.chronicle/runs/1/lanes/<k>/<session>.jsonl`, cut to its first sentence per long text and `{"_trimmed":true}` per big body as round 9 did, into `run-lane.jsonl`. Update the numbers in `runs-replay.test.ts` to what the recording shows; run `npx vitest run src/lib/runs-replay.test.ts` — Expected: PASS.

## Self-review (done while writing)

- **Spec coverage.** §1 decisions: one session per lane (Tasks 4, 9, 11), judge for bounded decisions only (6), all three entry points (11, 13, 15), refusals of push/publish/tag/release reach the user (3, 9, 11's chat screen), no budget stop (cost shown: 12, 14). §2 ground truth honoured (rounds unchanged in shape; `Lane` extended; `design_write_decision` generalised). §3 lifecycle: planning with validation and two retries (2, 10, 11), running with slots, attempt states, stall, backoff, three attempts (7, 9), gating with leak, explicit-path save under one mutex, scratch worktree, exit codes, review, two fix passes, head check and triage (5, 6, 9), closing by Chronicle (9), needs-you rows and their actions (9, 11, 14), Stop reverting nothing and Discard on click (5, 9, 11, 14), Resume from the record (7, 9, 11), the record (1, 12). §4 fence, git guard, shell hole, shared files, index (3, 4, 5). §5 amended engine (4, 9, 11); §5a not built. §6 four decisions, bands, never-delegated, every call logged, the provider layer (6). §7 entry points and the check list (2, 11, 15). §8 card, lane view, notes mirror, journal, vocabulary (1, 12, 14, 15). §10 files (all; `run-plugin/` is §5a's and is not built). §11 errors: planner never declares (11), adapter dies → interrupted (11's `recover_in`, 9's `Died`), no scratch copy → checks in the tree with lanes paused (5, 7), provider unreachable → agent for the rest of the run with one notice (6), a hung check killed at ten minutes (5), colliding leaks on both rows (5, 9). §12 tests (every task; gated test 17; replay 16; live 18). §14 boards 1–4 (14, 15, 16, 18). §15 choices kept as written.
- **Placeholders.** None in code steps. Task 18's PROGRESS cells are measurements the executor fills in, by instruction.
- **Types.** `RunRecord`/`LaneRecord`/`Gate`/`Leak`/`NeedsYou`/`DecisionLog` are defined once in Task 1 and mirrored field for field in Task 12 (pinned by `the_frontend_fixture_is_a_record_rust_reads`); `Lane::Worker { run: u64, k: u16 }` and the `"w/<run>-<k>"` key are the same in Tasks 4, 9, 11, 13, 14; `Fence`/`Verdict` (3) are consumed by name in 9; `Fallback`/`Oracle` (6) in 9, 11, 17; `Action`/`Tick`/`resume` (7) in 9 and 11; `Ev`/`LaneHost`/`CtlParts` (9) in 11 and 17; `RunScope`/`scoped_args` (10) in `mcp.rs` and `main.rs`; Plan C's names are the spec's (`Calls`, `DecisionRequest`, `Criterion`, `Decision`, `Answer`, `setup::agent_launch`, `acp::start(…, profile, …)`, `Lane::Reviewer`) plus the three assumed ones in the header table, each used in one function.
- **Review Focus.** All five pinned: Tasks 2/3 (symlink), 5 (hung check, index race), 6 (unreachable provider), 7/9 (resume from gating).
- **Deviations, for the user at plan review.** (1) The record gains additive fields §3 doesn't list (header list). (2) Gates and the head check run one at a time — one scratch copy holds one checkout. (3) The chat lane always carries the `chronicle-run` server (declare/state only) so the planner can declare without restarting the session; the project's own `.mcp.json` server never shows run tools. (4) A shell write into another *working* lane's files is that lane's only when it went through a tool Chronicle allowed; otherwise it is reported on both rows (§11's "both rows show it"). (5) The Setup "Decisions" row is Plan C's "Providers" row.
